# 03-详细设计 · L4-observability

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 文档状态 | current formal baseline after full-restart M3 Step 19 assembly and full-document gate |
| 直接上游 | `00-需求文档.md`;`01-架构设计.md`;`02-概要设计.md` |
| 目标实现仓 | `/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-observability`（当前尚未创建） |
| 实现形态 | Rust workspace,七个 role crate |
| 核心边界 | 只拥有 observation fact、audit projection、body-free linkage、handoff、retention / no-write / gap marker、history / outbox / stored result 与派生维护事实 |
| 实施状态 | 本文完成不等于 implementation ready;当前轮 `04~07`、目标仓、逐 boundary 审计、ledger / skeleton 和真实测试 / evidence 仍未完成 |

本文使用 `Command`、`Query`、`Inbound Consumer`、`Outbound Event`、`Operations Job` 表示五类公开协议。Rust 标识符和代码注释使用英文;设计解释使用中文。文中的 `planned`、`pending`、`not-run` 均不是实现或验收完成声明。

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_01_upstream_boundary.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读上述中间产物的“上游关系映射表”“本文不再回答”“本文必须回答”和“输入不足风险清单”,了解本详细设计的输入边界。

### 1.1 上游关系映射

| 来源 | 已收稳结论 | 本文继续展开 |
|---|---|---|
| `projects/L4-observability/00-需求文档.md` | 仓定位、核心能力、forbidden body、body-free evidence linkage、report handoff、retention marker、active reference protection、Query / Consumer / Job no-write 和验收红线 | 对象不变量、DTO 校验、函数分支、错误、状态、测试切口和实施承接 |
| `projects/L4-observability/01-架构设计.md` | 职责边界、依赖裁剪、数据所有权、一致性分层、产品中立适配、运行承载和演进边界 | crate / module、port / adapter、repository、transaction、outbox、handoff 和 config binding |
| `projects/L4-observability/02-概要设计.md` §4~§12 | 七模块代码主体、十个主要组成部分、关键对象、五类接口、十组 flow、十一组状态族、异常边界、配置影响和详细设计承接 | 文件、对象、trait、协议、函数 flow、状态矩阵、持久化、错误、幂等、配置引用和测试切口 |

| `design-calibration/02_hld_step_13_risks_open_questions.md` | 概要阶段风险与保守处理 | 由 §17 记录仍开放事项、阻塞范围和未确认前处理 |

### 1.2 本文不再回答

下列结论已由需求或架构层固定,本文只负责将其落实为实现约束:

- Observability 不是 Governance、Artifact、Identity、Runtime、Sandbox、Archive、Bus、Console、dashboard、GRC 或其他产品的业务 truth owner。
- forbidden body、raw payload、secret、credential、source audit body、evidence / artifact body、identity / governance / runtime body、provider response body 和 archive package body不得入仓。
- Query、diagnostic、projection rebuild、replay、retention、handoff、external export、Consumer 和 Operations Job 不得修复或反写 source truth。
- correlation id、trace id、span id、causation id 和 opaque hint 只提供关联语境,不得替代业务主键或业务事实。
- report handoff、authenticity hint 和 evidence-index input 不生成 final verdict、真实 evidence alias、真实 `run_id` 或验收 signoff。
- 外部 APM、OTel、Prometheus、Grafana、TimescaleDB、dashboard、alert、GRC 或 external audit 产品不是本仓 truth source。
- 除 `L0-core` 已裁剪契约外,其他 sibling repo 不得成为核心语义编译期依赖。
- 十个主要组成部分、五类接口、十组处理流和十一组状态族不在详细设计阶段增删或改义。

### 1.3 本文必须回答

- workspace、crate、module、file、binary 如何布局,以及七模块如何承接十个业务组成部分。
- 每个模块的 capability、对象、trait、adapter、repository、service、错误和测试切口。
- 进入实现边界的 struct / enum / value object / state / policy / record 的字段、类型、来源、函数和不变量。
- 16 Command、14 Query、9 Consumer、12 Outbound Event 和 9 Job 的 schema、metadata、result / receipt / view / report 与错误面。
- 每类 flow 的 validation、load、domain transition、save order、history、outbox、projection stale、stored result、external intent 和 transaction cut。
- 27 个正式状态机的 variant、trigger、合法 / 非法 / terminal / reserved 迁移及测试入口。
- logical persistence、transaction、consistency、error recovery、concurrency、idempotency、reentry、config binding 和 observability 如何落码。
- 本文如何向 `05/06/07` 交付测试和实施审计输入,以及何时必须停止并回报设计缺口。

### 1.4 上游边界结论

当前正式 `00/01/02` 足以支撑本详细设计,未发现要求回退上游的 blocker。旧 README、旧正式 `03~07`、旧 Step 产物和旧 implementation ledger / boundaries 均为 historical material,不得恢复其中的产品、对象、状态、数字、phase 或 evidence 口径。

## 2. 本次详细设计目标与范围

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_02_scope.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读上述中间产物的“设计目标表”“本轮覆盖范围表”“非范围表”和“实现者可完成的代码范围”,了解本轮详细设计的完整边界。

### 2.1 设计目标

| 目标 | 说明 | 交付给实现者的结果 |
|---|---|---|
| 实现组织闭环 | 将七模块和十个业务组成部分落到 workspace、crate、module、file、entry、service、store 与 port | 可创建目标仓骨架并判断每个文件 owner |
| 对象闭环 | 将概要对象展开为 Rust-facing struct、enum、value object、state、policy、record 和 error | 可实现 contracts / domain / application 类型而不猜字段或状态 |
| 协议闭环 | 将五类接口展开为 DTO、receipt、view、event payload、job report、metadata、idempotency 和 error surface | 可实现 contracts、API、worker 和 jobs entry mapping |
| flow 与 transaction 闭环 | 固定 validation、lookup、domain call、write set、outbox、history、stale marker、stored result 和 external call cut | 可实现 application service、UoW 与 repository 调用顺序 |
| 状态闭环 | 固定 27 个状态 owner、variant、trigger、非法 / terminal / reserved 迁移 | 可实现 state guard、transition helper 和状态测试 |
| persistence 闭环 | 固定 truth / projection / reference / history / outbox / handoff / idempotency / job logical stores | 可实现 repository port、fake、durable adapter 和一致性检查 |
| error / concurrency 闭环 | 固定 typed error、duplicate / conflict / in-flight、source order、claim / fence、token / probe | 可实现失败恢复且不盲重试副作用 |
| config / runtime 闭环 | 固定 typed config owner、adapter binding、runtime builder 和不可配置 invariant | `04` 可补 exact key / profile / values 而不改代码契约 |
| telemetry / audit 闭环 | 固定 runtime telemetry 与 durable audit 分层、字段 allowlist、redaction 和 recursion guard | 可实现安全 log / metric / trace / native audit hooks |
| test / handoff 闭环 | 固定模块、协议、状态、一致性、安全测试切口和 `07` 审计输入 | `05/06/07` 可继续展开而不发明 schema / state / boundary |

### 2.2 覆盖范围

| 实现面 | 本文覆盖 |
|---|---|
| 工程 | Rust workspace、七 crate、文件路径、binary、依赖方向、源码 / rustdoc 约束 |
| contracts | typed refs、metadata、Command / Query / Event / Job / View / Receipt / Error |
| domain | observation-owned fact / state / policy / marker / history formation |
| application | service façade、port、UoW、idempotency、stored result、flow orchestration |
| infra | repository / projection / reference / outbox / resolver / publisher / delivery / config / runtime builder contract |
| entries | sync API、inbound worker、outbox / maintenance loop、one-shot operations job |
| protocols | 16 Command、14 Query、9 Consumer、12 Outbound Event、9 Job |
| behavior | 逐类 flow、27 状态机、事务、一致性、error、recovery、concurrency、reentry |
| cross-cutting | typed config binding、log / metric / trace / native audit、redaction、self-recursion |
| verification | 最小测试切口、planned script contract、实施前闭环审计输入 |

### 2.3 非范围

| 非范围 | 正式承接位置 / 处理方式 |
|---|---|
| 重写需求目标、业务规则、数据归属、验收方向 | `00-需求文档.md` |
| 重写系统上下文、依赖方向、ownership、一致性策略 | `01-架构设计.md` |
| 增删七模块、十组成部分、五类接口、flow / state skeleton | 回退 `02-概要设计.md` 对应 Step |
| 完整配置 key、默认值、环境变量、secret、profile、numeric range 和迁移矩阵 | 当前轮 `04-配置设计.md` |
| 完整 suite / case / fixture / CI / coverage / artifact / report | 当前轮 `05-测试方案.md` |
| AC / VETO、真实 evidence、verdict、risk acceptance、signoff 和 release gate | 当前轮 `06-验收标准.md` |
| phase、task、commit boundary、rollback plan、implementation ledger 和 skeleton | 当前轮 `07-实施计划.md` |
| 部署 topology、生产 alert threshold、capacity plan、on-call 和 incident runbook | 部署 / 运维文档 |
| 物理 DB / broker / object store、HTTP / RPC、OTel / dashboard / GRC 等产品最终选择 | ADR、`04`、`07` 或受控 spike |
| 自动 remediation、双向 GRC sync、高级 dashboard / alert / prediction | 后续能力设计 |
| 相邻仓业务 truth 或任何外部正文模型 | 对应 truth owner 文档 |

### 2.4 实现者可完成的代码范围

在正式 `04~07`、目标仓和 boundary 门禁完成后,实现者可依据本文完成:

- 七 crate workspace 和文件 skeleton。
- public DTO、view、event、job、receipt、error 和 typed ref carrier。
- domain fact / state / policy / guard / record / error。
- Command / Query / Consumer / Job services 与 publication service。
- repository / resolver / publisher / delivery / UoW / clock / ID / idempotency ports 及正式 fake。
- logical persistence adapter、projection / reference store、outbox、stored result 和 staged job support。
- entry mapper、runtime builder、typed config binding 和安全 telemetry hooks。
- unit / contract / service / integration test shell,但完整用例和验收仍以当前轮 `05/06/07` 为准。

## 3. 实现约束与编码规范承接

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_03_constraints.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读上述中间产物的“编码规范承接表”“实现约束表”“本地多仓依赖约束表”和“实施前阅读要求”,了解约束来源与本地现实核验。

### 3.1 规范承接

| 规范来源 | 必须遵守 | 对实现的影响 |
|---|---|---|
| `standards/coding/rust.md` | Rust 标识符、源码注释、rustdoc、错误说明和测试名使用英文;public API 与 enum variant 有注释 | contracts / domain / application public surface 必须可读且可生成文档 |
| `standards/document/子项目目录与代码文件组织规范.md` | 实现仓位于 `/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-observability`;使用 `crates/<role>`;内部名不带 `L4` / `quantalithos` 前缀 | §4 固定 workspace、package、crate、binary 和 script / artifact / report 位置 |
| `standards/document/全局项目依赖关系与裁剪规则.md` | 仅编译期依赖进入 Cargo;运行期 / event 协作不得转为 path dependency | 只有 `core-contracts` 是 sibling path dependency,其他关系通过 port / event / handoff |
| `standards/document/设计真相源闭环与可落码性标准.md` | 字段、DTO、Query、state、metadata、idempotency、event、job、projection 和 phase 必须闭环 | 发现缺口必须暂停并回设计,不得由代码补 placeholder |
| `projects/README.md` 与实施计划规范 | design 仓提交中文,实现仓提交英文;提交前核实 local git identity 与工作区 | 具体 commit boundary 由 current `07` 固定,本文不伪造 commit |

### 3.2 实现与安全约束

| 约束 | 正式规则 | 影响范围 |
|---|---|---|
| 语言 | Rust;edition / rust-version 在目标 workspace 创建时由 `07` reality check 固定 | 全部 crate |
| 源码语言 | Rust identifier / rustdoc / error / test name 英文;设计解释中文 | public types、traits、tests |
| 运行形态 | 同步 Command / Query、异步 Consumer、resident worker、one-shot Job 分离 | application / api / worker / jobs |
| 编译依赖 | `L0-core` 的 `core-contracts` 是唯一 sibling 编译依赖 | Cargo workspace |
| 运行期依赖 | Bus、Identity、Governance、Artifact、Runtime、Sandbox、Archive、Console 和外部产品只通过 event / port / typed ref / handoff / export 协作 | contracts / application / infra |
| truth ownership | 只写 observation-owned fact / marker / projection / history / outbox / stored result / derived maintenance | domain / persistence / flow |
| forbidden body | raw log / metric / trace / audit / evidence / artifact / identity / governance / runtime / provider / archive body 永不进入 public、domain、store、telemetry 或 report | all boundaries |
| no-write | Query、diagnostic、rebuild、replay、handoff、export、retention 和 maintenance 不得反写 source truth | protocol / service / job / tests |
| config | 只影响 adapter、参数、schedule 和降级;不得改变 truth、schema、state、redaction、idempotency 或 no-write | infra / runtime builder |
| product neutral | 产品只作为受控 adapter / binding 候选,不是 schema / truth owner | infra / config / integration |
| evidence truth | telemetry、script exit、job result、delivery receipt 不是验收 verdict | tests / reports / handoff |

### 3.3 本地多仓依赖约束

| 依赖仓 | 类型 | 默认路径 | 当前方式 | 中期方式 | 影响单元 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `quantalithos-core` | compile-time | `/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-core` | `core-contracts = { path = "../quantalithos-core/crates/contracts" }` | private git tag / rev,由发布阶段决定 | contracts、domain、application、infra |
| `quantalithos-bus` | runtime / event | `/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-bus` | event envelope、Consumer、publisher port、fake | runtime binding | worker、application、infra |
| Identity / Governance / Artifact / Runtime / Sandbox / Archive | runtime / event / handoff | sibling paths only for local discovery | typed ref、safe summary resolver、event、handoff / export port、fake | versioned runtime contracts | application、infra、worker |

实现不得因为 sibling repo 在本机存在而增加 Cargo path dependency。若 `core-contracts` 实际 package / crate / type 与本文不符,必须在相应 implementation boundary 前暂停,记录具体差异并回设计或 core contracts;不得复制上游 shared type。

### 3.4 实施前置纪律

- 目标实现仓当前不存在;正式 `07` 必须定义 initialization / reality-check boundary。
- 首次代码改动前必须核实 workspace、Cargo、Rust toolchain、`core-contracts` path、dirty state 和项目级 git config。
- 目标仓 git identity 必须现场核实为项目规范要求,不能以 design 仓 identity 代替。
- 实现仓 commit message 使用英文并遵守 `type(scope): subject`;实际 commit 和 hash 只能由真实实施产生。
- 任何 source code 中的 public struct、field、enum、variant、trait 和 function 必须按 Rust 规范提供适当 rustdoc;禁止把中文设计段落直接粘贴成源码注释。

## 4. 实现单元与文件布局

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_04_file_layout.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读上述中间产物的“布局形态决策表”“文件布局树”“文件职责表”“命名检查表”和“编译期依赖表”,了解路径和 owner 的推导与检查。

### 4.1 布局决策与实现单元

| 候选布局 | 采用 | 判断与影响 |
|---|---|---|
| 单 crate 模块分层 | 否 | 多入口、公共协议和重 infra 需要 Cargo 强制边界 |
| workspace 多 crate | 是 | 七 role crate 分离 contracts / domain / application / infra / entries |
| 十个业务组成部分各拆 crate | 否 | 业务组成部分跨工程分层,不能取代 role boundary |
| 产品 adapter 各拆核心 crate | 否 | 产品尚未绑定且不能成为 truth / layout 主轴 |

| 目录 | Package | Rust crate / binary | 责任 | 暴露 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `crates/contracts` | `observability-contracts` | `observability_contracts` | public DTO、view、event、job、receipt、error、typed refs | public protocol |
| `crates/domain` | `observability-domain` | `observability_domain` | observation fact / state / policy / marker / history formation | internal |
| `crates/application` | `observability-application` | `observability_application` | services、ports、UoW、idempotency、flow orchestration | internal |
| `crates/infra` | `observability-infra` | `observability_infra` | repositories、stores、resolvers、publisher / delivery、config、runtime builder | internal |
| `crates/api` | `observability-api` | `observability_api` / `observability-api` | synchronous Command / Query entry | binary-oriented |
| `crates/worker` | `observability-worker` | `observability_worker` / `observability-worker` | inbound Consumers、outbox publisher、resident maintenance | binary-oriented |
| `crates/jobs` | `observability-jobs` | `observability_jobs` / action binaries | one-shot publish / rebuild / refresh / scan / replay / handoff / export | binary-oriented |

### 4.2 依赖方向

```text
core-contracts
      ^
      |
contracts <- domain <- application <- infra
    ^          ^           ^           ^
    +----------+-----------+-----------+
                           |
                    api / worker / jobs
```

关键说明:

- `contracts` 只能依赖 `core-contracts` 和通用基础库,不得依赖其他本仓 crate。
- `domain` 依赖 `contracts`;不知道 config、repository、adapter、route 或产品。
- `application` 依赖 `domain` 与 `contracts`,并拥有 port;不得依赖 `infra`。
- `infra` 实现 application-owned port,可以依赖 application / domain / contracts。
- `api` / `worker` / `jobs` 只做 entry mapping、composition 和 runner,不能直接访问 repository / UoW / resolver / publisher。

### 4.4 关键文件职责

| 文件 | Owner | 定义 / 责任 |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/contracts/src/refs.rs` | contracts | typed identity、reason、digest、cursor、visibility / availability carriers |
| `crates/contracts/src/commands.rs` | contracts | 16 Command request / response schema |
| `crates/contracts/src/queries.rs` / `views.rs` | contracts | 14 Query request、view、page、marker、surface |
| `crates/contracts/src/events.rs` | contracts | 9 inbound payload family 与 12 outbound payload |
| `crates/contracts/src/jobs.rs` | contracts | 9 Job input / response / report surface |
| `crates/domain/src/observation_intake.rs` | domain | `ObservationReceipt`, `SafetyDisposition`, intake records |
| `crates/domain/src/safe_signal.rs` | domain | `CorrelationContext`, `SafeSignal`, `SignalRollupWindow` |
| `crates/domain/src/audit_evidence.rs` | domain | `AuditProjection`, `EvidenceLinkage` |
| `crates/domain/src/report_handoff.rs` | domain | handoff、readiness、authenticity objects |
| `crates/domain/src/retention_replay.rs` / `no_write.rs` | domain | retention、protection、replay 和 no-write objects |
| `crates/domain/src/read_diagnostic.rs` / `gap_degraded.rs` | domain | visibility、diagnostic、gap、degraded semantics |
| `crates/application/src/ports.rs` | application | repository / resolver / publisher / delivery / clock / ID ports |
| `crates/application/src/*_service.rs` | application | 五类 façade 背后的 use-case orchestration |
| `crates/infra/src/runtime_builder.rs` | infra | validated config 到 complete-or-error runtime assembly |
| `crates/infra/src/*_stores.rs` | infra | logical store adapters,不得改 port / domain invariant |
| `crates/api/src/*_handlers.rs` | api | protocol envelope 到 service input / output mapping |
| `crates/worker/src/consumers.rs` | worker | inbound envelope mapping、ack / retry / dead-letter disposition |
| `crates/worker/src/outbox_publisher.rs` | worker | stored outbox snapshot publication loop |
| `crates/jobs/src/bin/*.rs` | jobs | one-shot Job entry,不承载业务逻辑 |

### 4.5 Cargo 与命名约束

```toml
[workspace.dependencies]
core-contracts = { path = "../quantalithos-core/crates/contracts" }
```

- workspace member 目录使用 `crates/<role>`;package 使用 `observability-<role>`;library crate 使用 `observability_<role>`。
- binary 名表达入口或动作;代码名不得包含 `L0`~`L4` / `l4_` 等架构层级。
- 不创建 `common`、`shared`、`utils` 作为未知 owner 的收纳桶。
- Bus、相邻业务仓和外部产品不进入 Cargo dependency。
- `scripts/`、`artifacts/`、`reports/` 是 planned layout;真实 `<run_id>` 和输出只能由后续真实执行产生。

## 5. 模块实现契约

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_05_module_contracts.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_06_object_contracts.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_07_trait_port_adapter_contracts.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_12_error_recovery.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_16_test_cuts.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议先读 Step 05 §7 的模块职责与依赖图,再按模块读 Step 06 §7 的字段级对象卡、Step 07 §7~§13 的 callable port 签名、Step 12 §8 的完整错误类型和 Step 16 §8 的测试切口。本章保留 owner、required field group、关键 callable 和禁止边界;长枚举迁移矩阵见 §9 及对应 calibration。

第 5 章是实现主轴。七个 crate 必须分别完成以下八段契约,不得先建立一个全局 struct / trait 集合再由实现者猜测 owner。

| 模块 | 核心职责 | 允许依赖 | 禁止依赖 / 越界 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `contracts` | public DTO、typed ref、digest、cursor、visibility / degraded / consistency / availability carrier | `core-contracts` | `domain`、`application`、`infra` 和 entry crate;不得承载 domain transition |
| `domain` | observation-owned fact、state、policy、guard、append-only record formation | `contracts`、`core-contracts` | repository、config、adapter、entry、external body / SDK |
| `application` | 五个 service facade、UoW、幂等、repository / resolver / publisher / delivery port、flow orchestration | `domain`、`contracts`、`core-contracts` | 具体 infra adapter、DB / bus / HTTP SDK |
| `infra` | application-owned port 的 fake / durable 实现、typed config、complete-or-error runtime builder | `application`、`domain`、`contracts`、`core-contracts` | entry crate;不得定义业务 policy 或修改 port 语义 |
| `api` | 同步 Command / Query 的 route、metadata、DTO 和 error mapping | `application`、`infra`、`contracts`、`core-contracts` | repository / UoW / resolver / publisher / domain transition 直连 |
| `worker` | inbound envelope、ack / retry / dead-letter、outbox publication loop mapping | `application`、`infra`、`contracts`、`core-contracts` | 绕过 facade 写 store、修 source truth、one-shot Job 逻辑 |
| `jobs` | 9 个 one-shot Operations Job 的 input、dispatch、report / exit mapping | `application`、`infra`、`contracts`、`core-contracts` | 常驻 consumer、直接 adapter / repository 调用、真实验收结论生成 |

### 5.1 `contracts`

#### 5.1.1 模块职责

`contracts` 是全部 public protocol type 的唯一项目内 owner。16 Command、14 Query、9 Inbound Consumer、12 Outbound Event、9 Operations Job 及其 request / response / receipt / view / payload / report / public error 所引用的二级类型,必须在本 crate 或 `core-contracts` 闭合。它只验证 protocol shape、typed owner 和 body-free 约束,不判断 admission、retention、handoff 或 visibility 的领域合法性。

#### 5.1.2 文件与代码主体映射

| 文件 | 代码主体 | 必须保持的边界 |
|---|---|---|
| `src/refs.rs` | `BodyFreeRef`、typed ref family、digest、committed cursor、source version、projection / refresh scope | 不用裸 `String` 替代具名 ref;不解析正文位置 |
| `src/metadata.rs` | Command / Query / Event / Job metadata、actor / trace / idempotency carrier | metadata 不生成业务身份或 source version |
| `src/commands.rs` | 16 Command request / response / receipt | operation 名与 body 由静态映射绑定 |
| `src/queries.rs` / `src/views.rs` | 14 Query request、view、page、marker、visibility / freshness surface | Query DTO 不携带 write intent |
| `src/events.rs` | 9 inbound payload family、12 outbound committed snapshot payload | event payload 不含 raw source body |
| `src/jobs.rs` | 9 Job input、resume cursor、report / item surface | report 不声称真实 run、evidence 或 verdict |
| `src/errors.rs` | `ProtocolError` 与 public error DTO | 不通过 free-text 反推内部错误 |
| `src/lib.rs` | 明确 re-export | 不建立 `common` / `utils` 收纳桶 |

#### 5.1.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | 输入 | 输出 owner | 状态 / 副作用 | 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| body-free typed carrier | trusted boundary 的 opaque ref | `BodyFreeRef` 与 typed newtype | 无 | 保存 path、payload、secret、provider body |
| canonical digest carrier | canonical serializer 的 digest bytes | `RequestDigest` / `DigestSummary` | 无 | 对 forbidden raw body 求 digest 后入仓 |
| committed ordering carrier | UoW allocator / repository scan | observation / reference tagged cursor、outbox cursor | 无 | 混用 row version、source version、page cursor |
| source ordering carrier | trusted producer / resolver marker | `ObservationSourceVersionRef` | 无 | 用时间戳或本地 cursor 伪造 source version |
| public read helper | domain / application 已完成的判断 | visibility、degraded、consistency、availability surface | 无 | 在 DTO 内执行 policy 或刷新 projection |
| protocol surface | entry / downstream structured input | Command / Query / Event / Job / View / Error DTO | 无 | 暴露 domain-private type 或外部产品 schema |

#### 5.1.4 对象实现契约

`BodyFreeRef` 是所有项目内 opaque public ref 的基础值对象:

```rust
pub struct BodyFreeRef {
    pub value: String,
}

impl BodyFreeRef {
    pub fn new(value: String) -> Result<Self, ProtocolError>;
    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str;
}
```

`new` 必须拒绝空值和正文型材料;`as_str` 只返回 opaque value,不得附带 path、URL、provider locator 或正文解析能力。以下类型必须是具名 newtype,不得互换或退化成裸字符串:

| Ref family | 正式类型 |
|---|---|
| intake / signal | `ObservationReceiptRef`;`SafetyDispositionRef`;`CorrelationContextRef`;`SafeSignalRef`;`SignalRollupWindowRef` |
| audit / evidence / handoff | `AuditProjectionRef`;`EvidenceLinkageRef`;`ReportHandoffRecordRef`;`AuthenticityHintRef` |
| retention / replay / no-write / gap | `RetentionMarkerRef`;`ActiveReferenceProtectionRef`;`ReplayScopeRef`;`NoWriteViolationRef`;`GapStateRef` |
| read / diagnostic | `ReadVisibilityRef`;`DiagnosticSummaryRef`;`DiagnosticScopeRef`;`DiagnosticRequestContextRef` |
| reference / maintenance | `ReferenceSnapshotRef`;`MaintenanceTargetRef`;`ProjectionMaintenanceRef`;`PeripheralDeliveryRef` |
| projection | `ObservationReadModelRef`;`DiagnosticViewRef`;`DashboardAlertExportViewRef`;`RebuildProgressViewRef`;`ProjectionFreshnessMarkerRef` |

公共 digest 与 ordering carrier 的字段和不变量如下:

| 对象 | Required fields | 构造 / 来源 | 不变量 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RequestDigest` | `profile_version: DigestProfileVersion`;`digest_value: DigestValue` | application canonical request serializer | v1 value 为 64 个小写十六进制字符;不可覆盖 retained profile |
| `DigestSummary` | 同上 | body-free material / payload / outcome serializer | material 必须 body-free;不是 raw body hash |
| `ObservationCursor` | `u64` | `ObservationUnitOfWork.assign_observation_cursor` | 只排序 observation-owned accepted change |
| `ReferenceCursor` | `u64` | `ObservationUnitOfWork.assign_reference_cursor` | 只排序 reference-only accepted change |
| `ObservationCommittedCursor` | `Observation(ObservationCursor)` 或 `Reference(ReferenceCursor)` | application 包装当前 UoW 唯一 allocator 结果 | adapter 不得丢 tag 后比较数值;一个 UoW 不得混用两类 cursor |
| `OutboxCursor` | opaque `String` | outbox pending scan | 只恢复稳定 scan;不是 committed cursor / event identity / claim |
| `ObservationSourceVersionRef` | `producer_family`;`source_ref`;`version_token: OpaqueSourceVersionToken` | producer envelope / resolver | 仅同 producer + source 且 adapter 声明可比较时做 Older / Equal / Newer;否则 `Uncomparable` |

以下 public helper 的字段必须直接可被协议构造,不得依赖 domain-private type:

```rust
pub struct VisibilitySurface {
    pub kind: PublicVisibilityKind,
    pub gap_ref: Option<GapStateRef>,
    pub degraded: Option<DegradedSurface>,
}

pub struct DegradedSurface {
    pub reason: DegradedReason,
    pub gap_ref: Option<GapStateRef>,
    pub limited_consumption_allowed: bool,
}
```

| Helper | Exact variants / fields | 规则 |
|---|---|---|
| `PublicVisibilityKind` | `Visible`;`Restricted`;`NotVisible`;`Blocked`;`Degraded` | not-visible 不得映射为 missing;blocked 不得映射为 success |
| `DegradedReason` | `NotVisible`;`UnresolvedReference`;`Stale`;`SafetyLimited`;`GuardBlocked` | reason 不含正文;limited consumption 只能来自 policy 输出 |
| `ObservationConsistencyHint` | `AllowStale`;`RequireFresh`;`BestEffort` | 只影响已提交 body 是否返回;不得等待或触发 rebuild / refresh |
| `ObservationProjectionScope` | `ByObservation`;`ByCorrelation`;`ByAuditSubject`;`ByReportHandoff`;`ByMaintenanceTarget` | canonical scope 可作 lookup key,不能直接生成 owned view ref |
| `ObservationReferenceRefreshScope` | `ExplicitRefs`;`BySourceFamily`;`UnhealthyOnly`;`ByMaintenanceTarget` | explicit refs canonical sorted / unique;不得触发 raw body scan |
| availability carrier | `AdapterFamily`、`AdapterAvailabilityScope`、public availability surface | 产品中立;exact binding 只在 infra config / runtime 内部出现 |

60 个正式 protocol surface 的 DTO owner 位于本模块,完整 schema 在 §7。`PrepareExternalAuditExport` 的 Command operation 与 delivery Job 名称相近但不是同一 operation;二者必须由 typed enum family 和静态 route table 区分,禁止字符串解析。

#### 5.1.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

`contracts` 不定义 repository、resolver、publisher、clock、ID、UoW 或 adapter trait,也不实现任何外部 adapter。它只提供可由各边界传递的 owned DTO / enum / value object。serialization / deserialization 实现必须保持 enum discriminator、typed ref owner、required field 和 schema version,不得在 decode 时调用 repository 或 domain policy。

#### 5.1.6 关键函数

| Callable | 返回 | 必须行为 | 失败行为 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BodyFreeRef::new(value)` | `Result<BodyFreeRef, ProtocolError>` | 接受合法非空 opaque value | empty / malformed / body-bearing -> `InvalidRef` |
| `VisibilitySurface::visible()` | `VisibilitySurface` | 生成无 gap 的 `Visible` surface | 无隐式 I/O |
| `VisibilitySurface::blocked(gap_ref)` | `VisibilitySurface` | 保留 typed gap 并输出 `Blocked` | 不吞 gap |
| `VisibilitySurface::is_visible()` | `bool` | 仅按 public kind 判断 | 不执行授权查询 |
| `VisibilitySurface::requires_redaction()` | `bool` | 对 restricted / degraded 等正式 surface 给出确定结果 | 不改 surface |
| `DegradedSurface::from_gap(reason, gap_ref)` | `DegradedSurface` | 保留 reason + gap | 不自动允许 limited consumption |
| `DegradedSurface::blocks_handoff()` | `bool` | 根据正式 reason / allowed flag 判断 | 不调用 handoff policy |
| public scope canonicalization | canonical bytes / key | lossless discriminator + typed ref;set sorted / unique | unsupported variant / malformed ref -> protocol error |

#### 5.1.7 错误类型

```rust
pub enum ProtocolError {
    InvalidRef,
    InvalidEnvelope,
    RouteBodyMismatch,
    UnsupportedSchemaVersion,
    InvalidPageCursor,
}
```

这些错误必须在 application write UoW 之前产生。除 `UnsupportedSchemaVersion` 在 producer 升级前属于同输入不可重试外,其余都要求调用方修改输入;不得把 internal error message 复制到 public DTO。

#### 5.1.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `contracts_protocol_roundtrip` | 16 / 14 / 9 / 12 / 9 的 operation、metadata、required field、enum discriminator roundtrip 不丢失 |
| `contracts_typed_ref_owner_isolation` | 不同 owner ref 无隐式互转,serialization 后仍保留类型边界 |
| `contracts_body_free_schema_scan` | DTO / event / report / error 不出现 raw body、secret、endpoint、provider response、真实 run / evidence 字段 |
| `contracts_digest_golden_vectors` | profile、canonical bytes、64 位小写 hex 与 unknown profile failure 稳定 |
| `contracts_cursor_namespace_isolation` | observation / reference / outbox / source version 不可比较或互换 |
| `contracts_visibility_semantics` | not-visible、blocked、degraded、stale 不被序列化为 missing / visible / fresh |

### 5.2 `domain`

#### 5.2.1 模块职责

`domain` 只定义 Observability 自有的 fact、state、policy、guard 和 append-only record formation。它可以表达“收到并安全处置了某个 body-free source ref”“形成了 audit projection / evidence linkage”“某个 handoff、retention、gap、reference snapshot 或 derived maintenance 处于何种 observation-side 状态”,但不得表达相邻业务 truth 已改变、source body 已被修复、交付已验收或 evidence 已签署。

#### 5.2.2 文件与代码主体映射

| 文件 | 主要对象 / 责任 |
|---|---|
| `observation_intake.rs` | `ObservationReceipt`;`SafetyDisposition`;intake / safety decision records |
| `safe_signal.rs` | `CorrelationContext`;`SafeSignal`;`SignalRollupWindow`;correlation records |
| `audit_evidence.rs` | `AuditProjection`;`EvidenceLinkage`;audit append records |
| `report_handoff.rs` | `ReportHandoffRecord`;`HandoffReadinessState`;`AuthenticityHint`;handoff lifecycle records |
| `retention_replay.rs` | `RetentionMarker`;`ActiveReferenceProtection`;`ReplayScope`;retention / replay records |
| `no_write.rs` | `NoWriteViolation`;`NoWriteGuardPolicy`;violation records |
| `read_diagnostic.rs` | `ReadVisibilityState`;`DiagnosticScope`;`DiagnosticRequestContext`;`DiagnosticSummary` |
| `gap_degraded.rs` | `GapState`;`DegradedOutputState`;gap transition records |
| `peripheral.rs` | `ExternalAuditExportPreparation`;`PeripheralDeliveryState`;delivery records |
| `reference.rs` | `ReferenceSnapshotState`;reference refresh records |
| `maintenance.rs` | `ProjectionMaintenanceState`;`ReplayCoordinationState`;`RollupRebuildState`;maintenance records |
| `policies.rs` / `history.rs` / `errors.rs` | stateless policy / guard、append-only record family、`DomainError` |

#### 5.2.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | 输入 | Domain 输出 | Owner 对象 | 不拥有 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| intake / redaction-first admission | body-free source、purpose、safe material summary | receipt、safety disposition、decision record | `ObservationReceipt`;`SafetyDisposition` | raw log / metric / trace / payload |
| correlation / safe signal | receipt、opaque trace / causation、safe summary | correlation context、safe signal、rollup state | `CorrelationContext`;`SafeSignal`;`SignalRollupWindow` | business identity / execution truth |
| audit / evidence observation | source audit ref、typed subject、body-free evidence summary | append-only audit projection、linkage | `AuditProjection`;`EvidenceLinkage` | source audit / evidence body 或 verdict |
| report handoff / authenticity | immutable evidence-index input、consumer ref、visibility / gap | handoff、readiness、authenticity hint | `ReportHandoffRecord`;`AuthenticityHint` | final report、真实 evidence alias / signoff |
| retention / replay / no-write | protected ref、active consumers、approved observation-only scope | marker、protection、scope、violation | `RetentionMarker`;`ActiveReferenceProtection`;`ReplayScope`;`NoWriteViolation` | source cleanup / repair |
| read / gap / degraded | committed projection scope、visibility、safe gap basis | diagnostic / visibility / gap / degraded state | `ReadVisibilityState`;`DiagnosticSummary`;`GapState`;`DegradedOutputState` | authorization truth / Query repair |
| peripheral / reference / maintenance | product-neutral consumer、safe resolver result、captured source position | preparation / delivery、snapshot、maintenance state | peripheral / reference / maintenance families | external lifecycle / product truth |

#### 5.2.4 对象实现契约

以下 11 个核心对象的字段是实现必需字段。字段来源缺失时必须返回 typed error 或形成明确 degraded / gap 状态,不得以空字符串、当前时间或默认 ref 补齐。

| 对象 | Exact required fields | Factory / 初始态 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationReceipt` | `receipt_ref: ObservationReceiptRef`;`source_ref: ObservationSourceRef`;`admission_state: ObservationReceiptState`;`safety_disposition_ref: Option<SafetyDispositionRef>`;`submission_purpose: SubmissionPurpose`;`received_at: ObservedAt` | `receive(ref, source, purpose, time)` -> `Received` |
| `SafetyDisposition` | `disposition_ref: SafetyDispositionRef`;`receipt_ref: ObservationReceiptRef`;`state: SafetyDispositionState`;`redaction_marker: RedactionMarker`;`forbidden_body: ForbiddenBodyFlag`;`sanitized_summary_ref: Option<SafeSummaryRef>` | `evaluate(ref, receipt, ReceivedMaterialSummary)` -> `Pending` |
| `CorrelationContext` | `context_ref: CorrelationContextRef`;`receipt_ref: ObservationReceiptRef`;`trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>`;`causation_ref: Option<CausationRef>`;`source_ref: ObservationSourceRef`;`state: CorrelationContextState` | `from_receipt(ref, receipt, source, seed)` -> `Unbound` |
| `SafeSignal` | `signal_ref: SafeSignalRef`;`signal_kind: SafeSignalKind`;`correlation_context_ref: CorrelationContextRef`;`state: SafeSignalState`;`summary_ref: SafeSignalSummaryRef`;`runtime_signal_ref: Option<RuntimeSandboxSignalRef>` | `from_summary(ref, context, summary, kind)` -> `Candidate` |
| `AuditProjection` | `projection_ref: AuditProjectionRef`;`subject_ref: AuditSubjectRef`;`correlation_context_ref: CorrelationContextRef`;`source_audit_ref: SourceAuditRef`;`state: AuditProjectionState`;`latest_append_record_ref: Option<AuditAppendRecordRef>` | `create(ref, subject, context, source_audit)` -> `PendingAppend` |
| `EvidenceLinkage` | `linkage_ref: EvidenceLinkageRef`;`projection_ref: AuditProjectionRef`;`boundary_ref: GovernanceArtifactEvidenceReference`;`evidence_purpose: EvidenceConsumerPurpose`;`state: EvidenceLinkageState`;`digest_summary: DigestSummary`;`visibility_reason: Option<EvidenceVisibilityReason>` | `candidate(ref, projection, boundary, purpose, digest)` -> `Candidate` |
| `ReportHandoffRecord` | `handoff_ref: ReportHandoffRecordRef`;`handoff_scope_ref: ReportHandoffScopeRef`;`consumer_ref: ReportConsumerRef`;`state: ReportHandoffState`;`readiness: HandoffReadinessState`;`evidence_index_input_ref: EvidenceIndexInputViewRef`;`authenticity_hint_ref: Option<AuthenticityHintRef>` | `draft(ref, scope, consumer, input)` -> `Draft` / `PendingEvidence` |
| `RetentionMarker` | `marker_ref: RetentionMarkerRef`;`protected_ref: ProtectedObservationRef`;`state: RetentionMarkerState`;`active_protection_ref: Option<ActiveReferenceProtectionRef>`;`archive_eligibility_ref: Option<ArchiveEligibilityRef>` | `for_observation(ref, protected, purpose)` -> `Unmarked` |
| `NoWriteViolation` | `violation_ref: NoWriteViolationRef`;`trigger_context_ref: NoWriteTriggerContextRef`;`attempted_write_target: ForbiddenWriteTargetRef`;`state: NoWriteViolationState`;`record_ref: Option<NoWriteViolationRecordRef>` | `detect(ref, context, target)` -> `Detected` |
| `GapState` | `gap_ref: GapStateRef`;`source_ref: GapSourceRef`;`gap_kind: GapKind`;`state: GapLifecycleState`;`degraded_ref: Option<DegradedOutputRef>` | `open(ref, source, kind)` -> `Open` |
| `ReferenceSnapshotState` | `snapshot_ref: ReferenceSnapshotRef`;`subject_ref: ReferenceSubjectRef`;`state: ReferenceSnapshotStateKind`;`safe_summary_ref: Option<SafeExternalSummaryRef>`;`refresh_record_ref: Option<ReferenceRefreshRecordRef>` | `pending(ref, subject)` -> `Pending` |

核心对象的值和引用约束:

- `ObservationReceipt.source_ref`、`AuditProjection.source_audit_ref` 和 `EvidenceLinkage.boundary_ref` 只能来自 Command / Event / resolver 的 typed body-free input,不能从 raw body 反向抽取后保存。
- `SafetyDisposition.sanitized_summary_ref` 只有 redaction / safe resolver 完成时才能出现;`forbidden_body=true` 不允许进入 `Safe` / `Redacted`。
- `CorrelationContext` 的 trace / causation 只用于关联;不能推导 actor、subject、work、decision 或 artifact truth。
- `EvidenceLinkage` 的唯一关系至少绑定 projection + boundary + purpose;`NotVisible` 与 `BodyBlocked` 不得折叠为 missing。
- `ReportHandoffRecord.readiness=Ready` 只说明 observation-side material 满足 handoff guard;`Delivered` 也不等于验收通过。
- `RetentionMarker.ReleaseEligible` 只允许后续评估,不是 cleanup 指令;active protection 非空时不得 `Released`。
- `NoWriteViolation` 记录失败也不能放行被阻断的 source write。
- `GapState.close` 只关闭 observation-side gap 语境,不得声称 source truth 已修复。
- `ReferenceSnapshotState` 只保存 safe summary ref 和 source version 语境;`Invalid` 为当前 snapshot 终态,后续有效解析必须建立新 snapshot。

其余 domain 对象不得因正文压缩而省略。实现至少按下表字段组和 callable 落码;exact enum 迁移见 §9。

| 对象 | Required field group | 关键 callable | 边界 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SignalRollupWindow` | window ref、scope、kind、state、signal count、source cursor | `accept_signal`;`seal`;`reopen_for_rebuild`;`fail` | 只聚合 stored `SafeSignal`,不读取 raw metric / trace |
| `AuthenticityHint` | hint ref、handoff ref、state、evidence origin、optional placeholder reason | `confirm_real_evidence`;`mark_placeholder`;`mark_insufficient` | 无正式 linkage basis 时只能 placeholder / insufficient |
| `ActiveReferenceProtection` | protection ref、protected ref、state、canonical consumer ref set | `attach_consumer`;`request_release`;`mark_expired`;`mark_conflicted`;`release` | 非空 consumer set 阻断 release |
| `ReplayScope` | scope ref、canonical target set、allowed effect、state | `approve`;`block`;`close` | target 仅限 observation-side derived effects |
| `ReadVisibilityState` | visibility ref、kind、optional request context / gap | `is_visible`;`restrict`;`block` | 不是业务授权 truth |
| `DiagnosticScope` | stable scope ref、projection scope、canonical targets、bounded time window、visibility scope | `define` | replacement 保留 scope ref;Query context 不持久化 |
| `DiagnosticRequestContext` | transient request ref、actor、projection / diagnostic / visibility scopes、requested time | `for_query` | read-only,不得成为 projection identity |
| `DiagnosticSummary` | summary ref、scope ref、freshness、safe signal / gap / violation ref sets | `attach_signal`;`attach_gap`;`attach_no_write_violation`;`mark_stale` | 不下发控制命令 |
| `DegradedOutputState` | degraded ref、typed reason、kind、optional gap | `block`;`allow_limited` | 不生成替代成功 |
| `PeripheralDeliveryState` | delivery / preparation / consumer / view refs、state、body-free result | `prepare`;`record_delivery`;`block` | delivery 不反写 consumer truth |
| `ExternalAuditExportPreparation` | preparation / consumer / view refs、visibility、state | `prepare`;`block`;`record_delivery` | 与 report handoff 分离 |
| `ProjectionMaintenanceState` | maintenance / target refs、state、optional progress ref、captured source positions | `for_missing_projection`;`schedule`;`start`;`complete`;`fail` | missing projection 初始 `Stale`,不能默认为 `Fresh` |
| `ReplayCoordinationState` | coordination / replay scope refs、state、no-write guard ref | `coordinate`;`block`;`complete`;`fail` | approved scope 和 target membership 必须 versioned load |
| `RollupRebuildState` | rebuild / window refs、state、source cursor | `start`;`complete`;`fail` | completion 必须覆盖 immutable plan source position |

Domain policy 是 stateless 或由 validated rule snapshot 驱动的纯对象。正式 policy family 为 intake admission、safety disposition、safe signal、body-free linkage、evidence visibility、authenticity hint、handoff readiness、retention protection、replay boundary、no-write guard、read visibility、gap classification、degraded output、peripheral export、reference freshness、adapter boundary、derived maintenance 和 replay coordination。它们只接受 typed value / loaded object,不得读取 config、repository 或 adapter。

所有 transition 产生的 `IntakeDecisionRecord`、`CorrelationLinkRecord`、`AuditAppendRecord`、`HandoffLifecycleRecord`、`RetentionChangeRecord`、`NoWriteViolationRecord`、`ReadAccessRecord`、`GapTransitionRecord`、`PeripheralDeliveryRecord`、`ReferenceRefreshRecord`、`ProjectionMaintenanceRecord`、`GapScanRecord`、`ReplayExecutionRecord` 均为 append-only;不得 in-place 修订或用当前对象重建历史。完整字段和 factory 见 Step 06 §7.6。

#### 5.2.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

`domain` 不定义 I/O port,也不实现 repository / resolver / publisher / delivery adapter。所有需要时钟、ID、source safe summary、visibility input、repository version 或 external result 的 factory / policy / transition 参数,必须由 application 预先取得后显式传入。Domain method 只允许:

1. 校验 typed owner、scope、policy precondition 和当前 state。
2. 更新当前 domain object 的字段。
3. 返回 domain decision 或 append-only record value。
4. 在失败时返回 `DomainError`,且不留下部分 mutation。

#### 5.2.6 关键函数

| 对象族 | 关键函数 | 正常结果 | 关键失败 / 禁止分支 |
|---|---|---|---|
| intake | `ObservationReceipt::receive/accept/reject/quarantine/degrade`;`SafetyDisposition::evaluate/allow_redacted/reject_unsafe/quarantine` | receipt + disposition + decision record | forbidden body 不得 accepted;reserved supersede 当前拒绝 |
| correlation / signal | `CorrelationContext::from_receipt/bind_source/link_runtime_signal/degrade/invalidate`;`SafeSignal::from_summary/record/mark_degraded/suppress` | body-free correlation / signal state | 不一致 source -> correlation conflict;raw summary -> body-free violation |
| audit / evidence | `AuditProjection::create/append_fact/restrict_visibility/attach_gap`;`EvidenceLinkage::candidate/link/body_block/mark_not_visible/mark_stale` | append-only projection / linkage | body blocked、not-visible、stale 保持 distinct |
| handoff | `ReportHandoffRecord::draft/prepare/attach_authenticity_hint/deliver/block` | lifecycle record | readiness 未满足 -> `HandoffNotReady`;delivery 不是 signoff |
| retention / no-write | retention hold / release-candidate / conflict;protection attach / release;replay approve / block;violation detect / block / escalate / close | marker / record | active ref、scope、no-write guard fail closed |
| gap / reference | gap open / acknowledge / mitigate / close;snapshot pending / refresh / stale / unresolved / invalid / unavailable | explicit state + record | not-visible 不当 missing;older / uncomparable source 不覆盖 current |
| derived maintenance | projection / rollup / replay coordination start / complete / fail | derived state + record | incomplete capture / fence / plan 不得 `Fresh` / `Completed` |

#### 5.2.7 错误类型

```rust
pub enum DomainError {
    MissingRequiredReference,
    ScopeMismatch,
    InvalidStateTransition,
    ReservedTransition,
    PolicyRejected,
    ReadNotAllowed,
    SafetyBoundaryViolation,
    BodyFreeBoundaryViolation,
    AuthenticityBoundaryViolation,
    ReferenceBoundaryViolation,
    ReplayBoundaryViolation,
    CorrelationConflict,
    ReferenceConflict,
    RetentionConflict,
    GapInvariantViolation,
    RollupInvariantViolation,
    RollupIncomplete,
    HandoffNotReady,
    MaintenanceIncomplete,
}
```

Domain error 不能携带 raw input、secret 或 provider response。invalid / reserved / terminal transition 不得产生 object save、history、outbox 或 stale marker;persisted invariant defect 必须由 application 映射为 consistency / manual intervention,不得盲重试。

#### 5.2.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `domain_factory_policy_invariants` | 合法 safe refs 可构造;missing / wrong owner / body-bearing input 返回 exact `DomainError` |
| `domain_state_and_history_pairing` | 每个合法 transition 返回匹配 record;非法、terminal、reserved 无状态和 record 副作用 |
| `domain_forbidden_body_negative` | raw log / metric / trace / audit / evidence / provider body 在所有 factory / policy 被拒绝 |
| `domain_correlation_not_truth` | trace / causation / opaque hint 不能推导或覆盖业务主键 / actor / subject |
| `domain_handoff_non_signoff` | Ready / Prepared / Delivered 均不能构造 verdict、evidence alias、真实 run 或 signoff |
| `domain_no_write_fail_closed` | violation record 保存失败时仍阻断 source write |
| `domain_reference_ordering` | older 不覆盖 current;uncomparable 显式 stale / degraded;Invalid 不复活 |
| `domain_no_external_dependency` | crate 静态扫描不出现 config、repository、runtime sink、external SDK 或 sibling Cargo dependency |

### 5.3 `application`

#### 5.3.1 模块职责

`application` 是全部 use-case orchestration 与 I/O port 的 owner。它通过五个 façade 承接 16 Command、14 Query、9 Inbound Consumer、9 Operations Job 和 outbox publication,统一管理 operation context、UoW、versioned load、domain transition、idempotency、stored result、outbox snapshot、projection stale、Job plan / claim / fence 和 external intent。它不实现 DB、bus、HTTP 或产品 adapter。

#### 5.3.2 文件与代码主体映射

| 文件 | 代码主体 |
|---|---|
| `services.rs` / `*_service.rs` | 五个 façade 与 use-case implementation |
| `operation_context.rs` | typed operation namespace、inbound event identity、operation context / factory |
| `unit_of_work.rs` | `ObservationUnitOfWork`;`ObservationUnitOfWorkManager` |
| `ports.rs` / `ports/*` | repository、projection、resolver、publisher、delivery、runtime probe、clock、ID ports |
| `idempotency.rs` | scope、reservation、reserve outcome、stored result / replay surface |
| `outbox.rs` | outbox record、stored payload snapshot、publication transition |
| `jobs.rs` | immutable execution plan、item outcome、claim / fence、job report draft |
| `external_effects.rs` | intent / binding refs、五类 stable token、probe-before-retry material |
| `visibility.rs` | `ObservationVisibilityDecision`;完整 Query result assembly |
| `errors.rs` | `ApplicationError`;recovery classification context |

#### 5.3.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | 主要输入 | 主要输出 / state | 关键对象 | 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Command orchestration | typed Command + metadata + actor | `ObservationCommandResult`;accepted / rejected stored result | truth-write façade、operation context、UoW | entry 开 UoW 或 domain 自取 ID |
| Query orchestration | typed Query + visibility / consistency metadata | `ObservationQueryResult<T>` | read façade、visibility decision | idempotency reservation、write UoW、同步 rebuild |
| Consumer orchestration | validated envelope + source event identity | `ObservationConsumerResult` | inbound façade、secondary dedup | dedup key drift 形成第二 writer |
| Job orchestration | Job DTO + validated config snapshot | immutable plan、fenced item outcome、durable report | maintenance façade、plan / claim / report | resume 重新 list 或使用 current config |
| Outbox publication | committed payload snapshot | publication batch result、marker update | publication façade、publication token | 回查 current truth 重建 payload |
| External handoff / export | durable preparation / intent + exact binding | formal prepare / delivery result | four handoff / export tokens | ambiguous result 盲重试 external call |
| Port ownership | domain / flow 的 I/O 需求 | callable trait | repositories、stores、resolvers、publishers | adapter 改写 port semantic |

#### 5.3.4 对象实现契约

Operation identity 必须是有限 typed namespace:

```rust
pub enum ObservationOperationName {
    Command(ObservationCommandOperation),
    Query(ObservationQueryOperation),
    InboundConsumer(ObservationInboundConsumerOperation),
    Job(ObservationJobOperation),
}

pub struct ObservationOperationContext {
    pub operation_name: ObservationOperationName,
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>,
    pub idempotency_key: Option<IdempotencyKey>,
    pub request_digest: RequestDigest,
    pub inbound_event_identity: Option<ObservationInboundEventIdentity>,
}
```

`ObservationInboundEventIdentity` 必须包含 exact consumer、producer family 和 `source_event_ref`;只有 Consumer context 允许该字段。Command / Consumer / Job 必须有 idempotency key,Query 必须没有 reservation。public operation 名通过静态表映射到 typed enum,不得 free-text parse。

幂等与 stored result 字段闭环如下:

| 对象 | Exact fields / variants | 不变量 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationIdempotencyScope` | `operation_name`;`actor_ref`;`idempotency_key` | logical key 是 operation + effective actor + key |
| `ObservationIdempotencyReservation` | `idempotency_ref`;`scope`;`request_digest`;optional inbound identity;`state`;optional `stored_result_ref` | durable state 只有 `Reserved` / `Completed`;completed 不可改写 |
| `ObservationIdempotencyReserveOutcome` | `Acquired`;`Replay`;`Conflict`;`InFlight` | 后三者是 incoming classification,不得写回 reservation row |
| `StoredObservationReplaySurface` | result kind、schema version、body-free serialized surface、digest summary | exact immutable protocol result,不从 current truth 重建 |
| `StoredObservationResult` | result / idempotency refs、operation、actor、request digest、public result ref、disposition、replay surface、stored time | operation + actor + digest + surface kind 必须与 reservation 一致 |

`ObservationOutboxRecord` 必须包含 `outbox_ref`、`event_ref`、`subject_ref`、`payload_snapshot_ref`、tagged `committed_cursor`、`state`、optional publication receipt / failure / dead-letter ref 和 `committed_at`。状态只能 `Pending -> Published | Failed | DeadLettered`;`Failed` 可在同 token 的受控 retry 后到 `Published` / `DeadLettered`,不得回到 `Pending`;publisher 只能使用 immutable stored payload snapshot。

Staged Job 使用以下不可变 / fenced 对象:

| 对象 | Exact field group / variants | 不变量 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationJobExecutionPlan` | plan / execution / idempotency refs、typed operation、request digest、complete config snapshot、plan digest、canonical items | work set 与 config snapshot immutable;resume 不重新 list |
| `ObservationJobPlanItem` | global typed work key、planned input digest、optional observed version、state、optional outcome | work key / digest / version immutable |
| `ObservationJobPlanItemState` | `Planned`;`Running`;`Succeeded`;`FailedRetryable`;`FailedPermanent`;`Blocked`;`SkippedTerminal` | application technical state,不计入 27 个 formal state owner |
| `ObservationJobPlanItemOutcome` | canonical affected / failed / gap / progress refs、optional typed reason、outcome digest | report 必须 lossless fold current finalizable outcomes |
| `ObservationExecutionClaim` | execution ref、optional work key、strictly increasing fencing token、`Active`;`Released`;`Expired` | claim 不是 exactly-once 或业务 truth 证明 |
| `ObservationJobReportDraft` | report / job / execution / plan refs、plan digest、last fence、state、canonical result sets、scope items、optional reason | terminal report + stored result + completed reservation 同 UoW;报告不是验收签署 |

五类 external-effect token 都是 immutable 且跨 retry 复用:

| Token | 必须字段 | Durable source / 规则 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationPublicationToken` | effect binding、event / outbox refs、payload digest、schema version | accepted outbox snapshot;不虚构 intent ref |
| `HandoffPreparationToken` | intent / binding refs、handoff / evidence input / consumer refs、material digest | prepare intent UoW |
| `HandoffDeliveryToken` | intent / binding refs、handoff / preparation / consumer refs、material digest | successful formal preparation |
| `ExportPreparationToken` | intent / binding refs、export preparation / view / consumer refs、material digest | export prepare intent UoW |
| `ExportDeliveryToken` | intent / binding refs、preparation / delivery / consumer refs、material digest | successful formal preparation |

`ExternalEffectBindingRef` 固定 product-neutral destination revision 与外部幂等 namespace;infra 负责解析 exact historical binding。token 不得包含 endpoint、topic、path、credential、job execution ref、claim token、attempt number、当前时间或 external body。

`ObservationVisibilityDecision` 包含 `surface`、`request_context_ref`、`assemble_allowed`;`require_assemblable()` 只能做只读 guard。`ObservationQueryResult<T>` 必须一次构造 `view`、visibility、freshness、degraded、availability、missing、rebuild 七个字段:NotVisible / Blocked 与 Missing 保持 distinct;stale / rebuilding 可否返回旧 committed body 由 consistency hint + visibility policy 决定,绝不能改写为 Fresh。

#### 5.3.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

五个 façade 是 entry 可调用的唯一业务面:

| Façade | 公开 callable inventory | 边界 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationTruthWriteService` | `submit_observation_material`;`record_safety_disposition`;`bind_correlation_context`;`record_safe_signal`;`append_audit_projection`;`link_body_free_evidence`;`prepare_report_handoff`;`evaluate_authenticity_hint`;`set_retention_marker`;`protect_active_reference`;`define_replay_scope`;`record_no_write_violation`;`record_gap_state`;`prepare_external_audit_export`;`register_reference_snapshot`;`update_reference_snapshot_state` | 16 Command,accepted mutation only |
| `ObservationReadService` | `get_observation_receipt`;`get_intake_status`;`get_safe_signal`;`get_signal_rollup`;`get_audit_timeline`;`get_evidence_index_input`;`get_report_handoff`;`get_retention_protection`;`get_observation_read_model`;`get_diagnostic_view`;`get_gap_status`;`get_peripheral_export_view`;`get_reference_snapshot_view`;`get_rebuild_progress` | 14 Query,zero write |
| `ObservationInboundEventService` | `consume_bus_observation_material`;`consume_source_audit_material`;`consume_identity_observation_context`;`consume_governance_audit_context`;`consume_artifact_evidence_context`;`consume_runtime_signal_summary`;`consume_sandbox_signal_summary`;`consume_archive_handoff_feedback`;`consume_report_consumer_feedback` | 9 Consumer,只写 local fact / marker / snapshot |
| `ObservationMaintenanceService` | `rebuild_observation_read_models`;`rebuild_signal_rollups`;`refresh_reference_snapshots`;`scan_observation_gaps`;`coordinate_observation_replay`;`prepare_report_handoff_delivery`;`prepare_external_audit_export_delivery`;`rebuild_peripheral_views` | 8 maintenance jobs;publish 单列 publication façade |
| `ObservationPublicationService` | `publish_observation_outbox` | 第 9 个 Job / resident worker publication path;只发布 committed snapshot |

基础 port 包括 `ObservationUnitOfWork` / `ObservationUnitOfWorkManager`、`ClockPort`、`IdGeneratorPort` 和 `ObservationOperationContextFactory`。repository / store port inventory:

| Port | 关键 callable |
|---|---|
| `ObservationIntakeRepository` | receipt / disposition versioned get / save、decision append、scope list |
| `CorrelationSignalRepository` | correlation / signal / rollup get / save / list、record append |
| `AuditEvidenceRepository` | projection / linkage get / save、audit append、timeline page |
| `ReportHandoffRepository` | immutable evidence input、handoff / authenticity get / save / list、lifecycle append |
| `PeripheralDeliveryRepository` | export preparation / delivery get / save / find、delivery append |
| `RetentionGuardRepository` | retention / protection / replay / no-write / gap / degraded get / save、records append |
| `ReferenceMaintenanceRepository` | snapshot / maintenance / coordination / rebuild get / save、target binding、refresh append |
| `ObservationJobReportRepository` | `get_report_with_version`;`find_report_by_job`;`save_report` |
| `ObservationProjectionSourceReader` / `ObservationProjectionMembershipPlanner` | bounded `capture`;typed `plan_updates` |
| `ObservationProjectionStore` | committed source index、view get / page / replace、affected view resolve / stale、gap / progress read |
| `ObservationIdempotencyRepository` / `ObservationStoredResultRepository` | atomic reserve / complete / load;result save / get |
| `ObservationOutboxRepository` | append、eligible snapshot list、published / failed / dead-letter marker update |
| `ObservationJobExecutionRepository` | plan get / find / save、execution / item claim、renew / release、fence register |

External ports 是 `ObservationSourceSummaryResolver`、`RuntimeSandboxSummaryResolver`、`GovernanceArtifactEvidenceResolver`、`SubjectObservationResolver`、`ObservationEventPublisher`、`ReportHandoffDeliveryPort`、`PeripheralExportDeliveryPort` 和 `AdapterAvailabilityProbe`。所有 resolver 返回 `SafeResolution<T> = Resolved | NotVisible | Stale | Unresolved | Unavailable`;不得返回 source body 或 provider response。

#### 5.3.6 关键函数

Accepted observation mutation 的唯一 single-UoW 顺序:

```text
validate and normalize operation context
reserve_or_load idempotency
versioned load and resolve body-free prerequisites
run domain policy / transition
stage owner/post-state + immutable input
plan exact source/projection memberships from staged + committed relations
assign exactly one ObservationCursor
construct and append cursor-bound native history / audit records
record committed source/index and append immutable outbox payload snapshot
mark exact affected projections stale with the same tagged cursor
save exact replay surface
mark reservation completed with that result ref
commit
```

Reference-only mutation 使用同一结构,但只能调用 `assign_reference_cursor` 并包装 `ObservationCommittedCursor::Reference`。rollback 后 reservation、cursor、truth / state、history、payload snapshot、stale marker、stored result 均不可见。Query 不调用 `begin`。外部调用不得包在持有本地长事务的 UoW 中;必须 prepare intent / token commit,external call,再以同 token finalize。ambiguous commit / external result 必须先 probe。

| Callable | Exact contract |
|---|---|
| `ObservationUnitOfWork::assign_observation_cursor()` | truth writes staged 后分配;一个 UoW 最多一次 |
| `ObservationUnitOfWork::assign_reference_cursor()` | reference-only writes staged 后分配;不得与 observation cursor 同 UoW |
| `reserve_or_load(context, uow)` | absent -> Acquired;same digest Reserved -> InFlight;Completed -> Replay;different digest -> Conflict |
| `save_result` then `mark_completed` | exact replay surface 必须先 durable stage;complete 不得指向 missing / mismatched result |
| `ObservationOutboxRecord::mark_published/failed/dead_letter` | 更新 stored marker,不回滚已提交 owner fact |
| `ObservationVisibilityDecision::require_assemblable()` | blocked / not-visible 拒绝 view assembly,零 write |
| Job claim / item CAS / finalize | 每次短 UoW 校验 current fence;terminal report 从 immutable item outcomes canonical fold |

#### 5.3.7 错误类型

`ApplicationError` 的 variant 名是控制流 authority,不得解析 message。正式错误组如下:

| 错误组 | Variants | Recovery 约束 |
|---|---|---|
| input / owned state | `InvalidRequest`;`InvalidPageCursor`;`UnsupportedSchemaVersion`;`OwnedStateNotFound`;`Domain(DomainError)` | input / state change;不开启或回滚 UoW |
| idempotency / stored result | `IdempotencyConflict`;`IdempotencyInFlight`;`CompletedReservationResultMissing`;`StoredResultKindMismatch` | conflict 不重跑;missing / mismatch manual |
| transition / concurrency | `InvalidStateTransition`;`ReservedTransition`;`OptimisticConflict`;`ExecutionFenceConflict` | reserved 拒绝;CAS reload;stale fence 不复用 token |
| dependency / config | repository / resolver / publisher / delivery unavailable;`AdapterDisabled` | dependency / config 恢复前不重试 |
| transaction | `SerializationFailed`;`CursorAllocationFailed`;`CommitFailed`;`CommitOutcomeUnknown`;`RollbackFailed` | unknown 必须 probe,不能当 known abort |
| outbox / projection | outbox invariant / payload missing / corrupt;projection assembly / scope / freshness / index / rebuild linkage defects | persisted mismatch fail closed / manual |
| maintenance / evidence / diagnostic | target / binding / replay / report missing;`MaintenanceIncomplete`;evidence input / diagnostic composite / persistence defect | typed state change 或 manual,不得补造 |
| external / no-write / job | `ExternalDeliveryFailed`;`ExternalFinalizeUnknown`;`NoWritePersistenceFailed`;`Job(JobError)` | finalize-only / probe / manual;source write 始终 blocked |

#### 5.3.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `application_command_consumer_uow_order` | reserve -> load -> transition -> stage owner/membership plan -> assign cursor -> construct/append cursor-bound history/index/outbox/stale -> result -> complete -> commit exact order |
| `application_query_no_write` | 14 Query 在 visible / missing / stale / blocked / unavailable 分支均零 write port 调用 |
| `application_idempotency_actor_scope` | same key 跨 actor / operation 不冲突;same scope different digest conflict;source event identity 防 key drift |
| `application_stored_result_exact_replay` | duplicate 返回原 schema / result kind / bytes / refs,不重跑 domain / external effect |
| `application_job_plan_item_finalize` | immutable plan、global work key、claim / fence、outcome fold、terminal seal 全部成立 |
| `application_external_token_stability` | 五类 token retry 时 byte-equivalent;binding revision 不切到 current config |
| `application_commit_unknown_probe` | unknown outcome 先查 reservation / result / marker,不得产生第二 writer |
| `application_typed_error_mapping` | 只按 enum variant 映射 recovery / public surface,不解析 message |

### 5.4 `infra`

#### 5.4.1 模块职责

`infra` 实现 application-owned ports,负责 logical persistence、projection / reference / idempotency / outbox / Job execution store、safe-summary resolver、publisher、handoff / export delivery、clock / ID、typed config、runtime assembly和entry-safe technical registration。它只把 domain / application 契约映射到 fake、controlled 或 durable mechanism,不拥有 observation truth、不定义 policy、不改变 port signature 或 formal outcome。Registrar不是application business port，也不是generic service locator。

#### 5.4.2 文件与代码主体映射

| 文件 | 实现责任 | 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|
| `config.rs` | raw source load、typed parse / validation、`ValidatedObservabilityConfig` 与locator-free entry slices | raw secret / endpoint / transport-policy-schedule locator进入application或entry-visible config |
| `runtime_builder.rs` | store / adapter capability gate、availability registry、五 façade + context factory complete-or-error assembly、technical registrar type/implementation | 返回partial runtime、依赖worker/jobs、暴露private registry/adapter或在builder中执行use case |
| `observation_repositories.rs` / `audit_stores.rs` | truth / state versioned repository、append-only record mapper | adapter 内执行 domain transition |
| `projection_stores.rs` | source / membership / position index、projection composite、freshness / stale marker | Query miss 时自动 rebuild |
| `reference_stores.rs` | reference scope index、snapshot / refresh / maintenance / report stores | 保存 external body 或拥有 external lifecycle |
| `idempotency_store.rs` | atomic reservation + immutable stored result | cache result 替代 durable replay authority |
| `outbox_store.rs` | immutable payload snapshot、eligible scan、publication marker CAS | 从 current truth 重建 payload |
| `source_resolvers.rs` | 四类 product-neutral safe-summary resolver | 返回 provider / source body |
| `publishers.rs` | exact binding publication + probe | current route 重定向旧 snapshot |
| `handoff_adapters.rs` / `external_export_adapters.rs` | prepare / deliver / probe formal outcome | receipt 映射为 verdict / signoff |
| `clock_id.rs` | production / deterministic `ClockPort`、`IdGeneratorPort` | repository / entry 私自生成 ID / time |
| `observability_hooks.rs` | allowlist-first log / metric / trace hooks 与 recursion suppression | telemetry 反向调用本仓 façade |
| `errors.rs` | provider-private cause 到 typed boundary error mapper | raw error、SQL、HTTP body 穿透 port |

#### 5.4.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | 实现对象 / adapter family | Formal output | Parity / ownership 约束 |
|---|---|---|---|
| transaction / repositories | UoW manager、truth / state adapters | versioned object、CAS / append result | fake / durable 同样执行 unique、CAS、rollback invisibility |
| projection | source reader、membership planner、projection store | bounded capture、atomic composite replace、freshness | 只从 committed body-free facts rebuild |
| idempotency / outbox / Job | reservation/result、outbox、plan/claim/fence/report adapters | exact stored material / typed outcome | private map 不是 truth;terminal material不可重建 |
| resolver | source、runtime/sandbox、governance/artifact、subject adapters | `SafeResolution<T>` | resolved / not-visible / stale / unresolved / unavailable 全分支 |
| publication / delivery | event publisher、handoff、peripheral export adapters | formal result + phase probe | same token + exact historical binding |
| availability | adapter registry / probe | family or exact-binding `AdapterAvailabilityState` | health 不等于 operation success |
| config / assembly | loader、validator、builder | validated immutable config / built runtime | missing required capability fail before façade escape |
| entry registration | pre-resolved transport/actor-policy/scheduler + finite handler catalog | locator-free safe metadata、two registrars、opaque registered sets | group registration all-or-nothing；infra不依赖entry crate |

#### 5.4.4 对象实现契约

Infra-owned public-to-composition objects 只有 config / assembly 类型;business object、port carrier 和 availability semantics 仍由 contracts / domain / application 拥有。

```rust
pub struct ValidatedObservabilityConfig {
    pub config_ref: ConfigBindingRef,
    pub profile: RuntimeProfileClass,
    pub technical: TechnicalRuntimeConfig,
    pub boundary: BoundaryConfig,
    pub safety: SafetyBindingConfig,
    pub stores: StoreBindingConfig,
    pub digest: DigestCompatibilityConfig,
    pub idempotency: IdempotencyRetentionConfig,
    pub projection: ProjectionRuntimeConfig,
    pub execution: ExecutionRuntimeConfig,
    pub external: ExternalBindingSet,
    pub entries: EntryBindingConfig,
}
```

`RawObservabilityConfig` 是 infra-private deserialize shape;`ValidatedObservabilityConfig` 是 immutable assembly input;`JobExecutionConfigSnapshot` 是 application-owned execution subset并与 plan 一起 durable 保存。只有 `infra::config` 可读 file / env / secret locator;application 和 entry 只能接收 validated executable values / slices。

Raw `InboundConsumerBindingConfig`和`JobScheduleBindingConfig`分别携带transport/actor-policy与schedule locator，只能存在于infra validated root和builder内部。Entry-safe projection固定为：

```rust
pub struct ValidatedInboundConsumerRegistration {
    pub operation: ObservationInboundConsumerOperation,
    pub producer_family: ObservationProducerFamily,
    pub accepted_schema_versions: Vec<SchemaVersion>,
}

pub struct ValidatedJobScheduleRegistration {
    pub operation: ObservationJobOperation,
}
```

Safe item按finite operation canonical sorted/unique，且必须与private resolved slot一一对应。它们不得提供到`TransportBindingRef`、`PolicyBindingRef`、`ScheduleBindingRef`的getter/conversion，不含endpoint、topic、cron、provider、credential、resolved material或private registry key。

```rust
pub struct BuiltObservabilityRuntime {
    pub truth_write_service: Arc<dyn ObservationTruthWriteService>,
    pub read_service: Arc<dyn ObservationReadService>,
    pub inbound_event_service: Arc<dyn ObservationInboundEventService>,
    pub maintenance_service: Arc<dyn ObservationMaintenanceService>,
    pub publication_service: Arc<dyn ObservationPublicationService>,
    pub operation_context_factory: Arc<dyn ObservationOperationContextFactory>,
    pub availability_probe: Arc<dyn AdapterAvailabilityProbe>,
    pub api_entry: ValidatedApiEntryConfig,
    pub worker_entry: ValidatedWorkerEntryConfig,
    pub jobs_entry: ValidatedJobsEntryConfig,
    pub inbound_consumer_registrar: Arc<dyn InboundConsumerRegistrar>,
    pub job_schedule_registrar: Arc<dyn JobScheduleRegistrar>,
}
```

`operation_context_factory`是Step 07既有context factory，不是第六个business façade；三个entry必须使用同一assembly handle，不能自行补actor/idempotency/source identity。Entry不得通过`Any`、downcast或concrete getter从service/context-factory/registrar取得repository、adapter、locator或private registry。配置和 adapter 对象必须满足:

| 对象 / mode | Exact rule | 禁止 / 兼容边界 |
|---|---|---|
| store row mapper | lossless 映射 exact field / enum / version;forbidden body 不进入 row / index / debug column | 不得私补字段或状态 |
| in-memory store | 仅 local / test;仍执行 atomic UoW、unique、CAS、cursor、claim / fence、stored replay contract | 不得暴露 private map 作为断言 truth |
| external `Fake` | 仅 `LocalTest`,显式 fixture ref,返回 formal outcomes;不得默认 Resolved / Published / Delivered | 不得绕过 token / probe / failure classification |
| `Controlled` | local / integration-like controlled seam,可产生 success / degraded / unknown / unsupported | 不得冒充 production endpoint |
| `Endpoint` | integration / runtime-like,provider detail只留 adapter-private memory | 不得泄漏 raw endpoint / credential / response body |
| `Disabled` | 只允许 non-startup-required family;formal unavailable / `AdapterDisabled` | 不得 no-op success 或自动 fallback fake |
| exact binding catalog | binding ref -> immutable product-neutral destination revision / capability descriptor | old pending snapshot / intent / plan 必须解析 old binding,不得 fallback current route |

`AdapterAvailabilityState` 由 infra 构造但类型和语义归 application。publisher / handoff / export 每次 effect 必须 probe exact `Some(effect_binding_ref)` scope;family aggregate 不能授权具体 target。

#### 5.4.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

| Application port group | Infra implementation | 必须模拟 / 实现 | 禁止行为 |
|---|---|---|---|
| UoW + repositories | in-memory / durable transaction adapters | same-UoW visibility、rollback、version、append-only、single tagged cursor | best-effort 多 store 拼接 |
| projection source / membership / store | logical source index与composite adapters | bounded complete capture、read fence、atomic replace、dependency / stale indexes | truncate 后标 Fresh |
| idempotency / stored result | atomic group adapter | actor-scoped insert-or-read、secondary event uniqueness、result-before-complete | process cache 接管 replay |
| outbox | snapshot / marker adapter | one marker + one immutable payload、stable eligible scan、CAS finalize | payload replacement / reconstruction |
| Job execution / report | plan / claim / fence / report adapter | global typed work uniqueness、monotonic fence、terminal seal | process mutex 替代 durable fence |
| safe summary resolvers | four resolver family adapters | exact typed ref / visibility context;formal `SafeResolution` | body / free-text provider error output |
| event publisher | publication adapter + probe | token / snapshot / binding equality、formal published / not / unknown / unsupported | ambiguous blind resend |
| handoff / export | four phase methods + probes | prepare-before-deliver、same token、formal receipt / outcome | target fallback、fake signoff |
| clock / ID / operation context | system / deterministic implementations | deterministic fake 与 production same owner contract | adapter row/default time 替代 `ClockPort` |
| entry registration technical seam | private transport/actor-policy/scheduler + safe projection | object-safe finite handler/catalog、prebuilt registrar、prepare-all/arm-all/revoke-all | application port、free-text callback map、locator lookup、partial active root |

`ObservabilityRuntimeBuilder::build(ValidatedObservabilityConfig) -> Result<BuiltObservabilityRuntime, RuntimeAssemblyError>` 是本模块唯一 assembly trait。它不依赖 `api`、`worker` 或 `jobs`;entry crate只在完整build后用assigned slice、façade/context factory和registrar注册route/loop/runner。

Technical registration callable必须object-safe：

```rust
pub struct InboundConsumerDelivery {
    pub operation: ObservationInboundConsumerOperation,
    pub producer_family: ObservationProducerFamily,
    pub schema_version: SchemaVersion,
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub envelope: InboundEnvelopeFrame,
}

pub struct InboundEnvelopeFrame {
    bytes: Box<[u8]>,
}

impl InboundEnvelopeFrame {
    pub fn into_bounded_bytes(self) -> Box<[u8]> {
        self.bytes
    }
}

pub enum InboundConsumerCompletion {
    Acknowledge(ObservationConsumerReceipt),
    Retry(ObservationConsumerReceipt),
    DeadLetter(ObservationConsumerReceipt),
}

pub type InboundConsumerHandlerFuture<'a> = Pin<
    Box<dyn Future<Output = InboundConsumerCompletion> + Send + 'a>,
>;

pub trait InboundConsumerHandler: Send + Sync {
    fn operation(&self) -> ObservationInboundConsumerOperation;

    fn handle<'a>(
        &'a self,
        delivery: InboundConsumerDelivery,
    ) -> InboundConsumerHandlerFuture<'a>;
}

pub struct InboundConsumerHandlerCatalog {
    pub consume_bus_observation_material: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_source_audit_material: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_identity_observation_context: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_governance_audit_context: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_artifact_evidence_context: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_runtime_signal_summary: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_sandbox_signal_summary: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_archive_handoff_feedback: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
    pub consume_report_consumer_feedback: Option<Arc<dyn InboundConsumerHandler>>,
}

pub enum ObservationJobInvocation {
    PublishObservationOutbox(ObservationJobRequest<PublishObservationOutboxJobInput>),
    RebuildObservationReadModels(ObservationJobRequest<RebuildObservationReadModelsJobInput>),
    RebuildSignalRollups(ObservationJobRequest<RebuildSignalRollupsJobInput>),
    RefreshReferenceSnapshots(ObservationJobRequest<RefreshReferenceSnapshotsJobInput>),
    ScanObservationGaps(ObservationJobRequest<ScanObservationGapsJobInput>),
    CoordinateObservationReplay(ObservationJobRequest<CoordinateObservationReplayJobInput>),
    PrepareReportHandoffDelivery(ObservationJobRequest<PrepareReportHandoffDeliveryJobInput>),
    PrepareExternalAuditExport(ObservationJobRequest<PrepareExternalAuditExportJobInput>),
    RebuildPeripheralViews(ObservationJobRequest<RebuildPeripheralViewsJobInput>),
}

pub enum ObservationJobInvocationResult {
    PublishObservationOutbox(ObservationJobResponse<PublishObservationOutboxJobOutput>),
    RebuildObservationReadModels(ObservationJobResponse<RebuildObservationReadModelsJobOutput>),
    RebuildSignalRollups(ObservationJobResponse<RebuildSignalRollupsJobOutput>),
    RefreshReferenceSnapshots(ObservationJobResponse<RefreshReferenceSnapshotsJobOutput>),
    ScanObservationGaps(ObservationJobResponse<ScanObservationGapsJobOutput>),
    CoordinateObservationReplay(ObservationJobResponse<CoordinateObservationReplayJobOutput>),
    PrepareReportHandoffDelivery(ObservationJobResponse<PrepareReportHandoffDeliveryJobOutput>),
    PrepareExternalAuditExport(ObservationJobResponse<PrepareExternalAuditExportJobOutput>),
    RebuildPeripheralViews(ObservationJobResponse<RebuildPeripheralViewsJobOutput>),
}

pub enum ObservationJobInvocationFailure {
    Protocol(ProtocolError),
    Application(ApplicationError),
}

pub type ObservationJobHandlerFuture<'a> = Pin<
    Box<
        dyn Future<
                Output = Result<
                    ObservationJobInvocationResult,
                    ObservationJobInvocationFailure,
                >,
            > + Send
            + 'a,
    >,
>;

pub trait ObservationJobHandler: Send + Sync {
    fn operation(&self) -> ObservationJobOperation;

    fn handle<'a>(
        &'a self,
        request: ObservationJobInvocation,
    ) -> ObservationJobHandlerFuture<'a>;
}

pub struct ObservationJobHandlerCatalog {
    pub publish_observation_outbox: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub rebuild_observation_read_models: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub rebuild_signal_rollups: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub refresh_reference_snapshots: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub scan_observation_gaps: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub coordinate_observation_replay: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub prepare_report_handoff_delivery: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub prepare_external_audit_export: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
    pub rebuild_peripheral_views: Option<Arc<dyn ObservationJobHandler>>,
}

pub type RegistrationFuture<'a, T> = Pin<
    Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<T, RuntimeAssemblyError>> + Send + 'a>,
>;

pub trait InboundConsumerRegistrar: Send + Sync {
    fn registrations(&self) -> &[ValidatedInboundConsumerRegistration];

    fn register_all<'a>(
        &'a self,
        handlers: InboundConsumerHandlerCatalog,
    ) -> RegistrationFuture<'a, Box<dyn RegisteredInboundConsumerSet>>;
}

pub trait JobScheduleRegistrar: Send + Sync {
    fn registrations(&self) -> &[ValidatedJobScheduleRegistration];

    fn register_all<'a>(
        &'a self,
        handlers: ObservationJobHandlerCatalog,
    ) -> RegistrationFuture<'a, Box<dyn RegisteredJobScheduleSet>>;
}

pub trait RegisteredInboundConsumerSet: Send + Sync {}
pub trait RegisteredJobScheduleSet: Send + Sync {}
```

Consumer catalog的9个字段与Job catalog的9个字段是finite total map：enabled operation必须是`Some(exact handler)`，disabled operation必须是`None`，字段、`handler.operation()`、safe registration item及private slot必须一致。`ObservationJobInvocation` / `Result`逐variant复用§7.6已有public Job wrapper，`ObservationJobInvocationFailure`只保留已有`ProtocolError` / `ApplicationError`，不是第二套DTO或错误分类。

`InboundEnvelopeFrame`的constructor保持infra-private；只有transport descriptor具有finite bound且实际frame未超限时才能构造。Frame不得`Clone` / `Copy` / `Debug` / `Display` / `Serialize`，transport ack token、offset、topic、provider metadata和private cause不进入delivery。Schedule registrar只能转交完整existing `ObservationJobRequest<T>`，不得补造actor、key、scope、target、cursor、input、run或evidence identity。

Opaque registered-set trait不得增加lookup/invoke/serialize/downcast API；handle只维持process-local registration生命周期，不是business state、durable recovery、health proof、run/evidence identity或acceptance result。`register_all`在返回`Ok`前执行prepare-all -> totality -> arm-all；任一失败revoke并join本次全部item，返回`EntryBindingIncomplete`且留下zero active root。

#### 5.4.6 关键函数

Runtime builder 必须按以下固定顺序执行并在任一失败点停止:

```text
load raw sources
parse / type / range
cross-field / profile / redline validation
establish body-free config identity
resolve sensitive refs inside infra
validate store schema / atomicity / CAS / fence capability
construct base technical adapters
construct external and entry technical adapters and verify descriptors
build availability registry
assemble five application façades and existing context factory
derive API slice, locator-free worker/jobs slices, and two prebuilt registrars
return complete runtime
```

Repository mapper 的关键行为:

- create 使用 explicit create expectation,update 使用 versioned load 返回的 `expected_version`;CAS 0 rows 映射 `OptimisticConflict`。
- append-only duplicate identity + different content 是 invariant / conflict,不能 overwrite。
- accepted UoW rollback 后 cursor、snapshot、result、stale marker不可被 read path 看见。
- projection capture 超限 / 不完整 / fence 缺失必须 item failure,不能 truncate;composite replace 任一 member 失败整体 rollback。
- `record_committed_sources` 必须原子更新 current source、exact membership、member position、bound target aggregate position / revision。
- outbox `list_eligible_with_payload` 返回 exact snapshot + version;missing / corrupt snapshot 是 consistency failure。
- Job claim renew 不确认 ownership 时旧 claimant 立即停止;commit-time fence 必须再次校验。
- resolver / publisher / delivery 的 provider error在 adapter boundary 立即映射,log / span 不读取 source error display chain。

#### 5.4.7 错误类型

运行时 adapter 不向 application 暴露 `InfraError`:private cause 必须映射为 Step 12 的 typed `ApplicationError` 或 formal resolver / delivery outcome。启动装配使用以下独立错误:

```rust
pub enum RuntimeAssemblyError {
    ConfigSourceUnavailable { issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
    InvalidConfiguration { config_ref: Option<ConfigBindingRef>, issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
    SensitiveReferenceUnavailable { config_ref: ConfigBindingRef, adapter_family: AdapterFamily, issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
    StoreCompatibilityMismatch { config_ref: ConfigBindingRef, adapter_family: AdapterFamily, issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
    AdapterConstructionFailed { config_ref: ConfigBindingRef, adapter_family: AdapterFamily, effect_binding_ref: Option<ExternalEffectBindingRef>, issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
    RequiredCapabilityMissing { config_ref: ConfigBindingRef, adapter_family: AdapterFamily, effect_binding_ref: Option<ExternalEffectBindingRef>, phase: Option<ExternalEffectPhase>, issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
    EntryBindingIncomplete { config_ref: ConfigBindingRef, issue_ref: RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef },
}
```

`RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef` 只是 body-free startup correlation ref,不是 evidence alias、run id 或验收签署。任何 variant 都不得携带 raw path / config / secret / endpoint / provider body。`EntryBindingIncomplete`还覆盖safe-item/private-slot/handler totality与group prepare/arm失败；失败必须撤销registration并返回zero exposed root。Builder只能返回完整 runtime 或一个 error。

#### 5.4.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `infra_repository_conformance` | fake / durable 对 unique、CAS、append、cursor、reservation、claim / fence、rollback 结果一致 |
| `infra_projection_composite_atomicity` | source capture / membership / view / diagnostic / freshness 任一失败不留下 partial composite |
| `infra_external_adapter_conformance` | resolver / publisher / handoff / export 只返回 formal outcome,token / probe / exact binding 不漂移 |
| `infra_runtime_builder_totality` | 每个 builder stage failpoint 都不泄漏 partial façade / route / adapter handle |
| `entry_registration_safe_projection` | entry constructor无法取得transport/policy/schedule locator、private registry或concrete adapter |
| `entry_registration_atomic_start` | handler totality或Nth prepare/arm失败revoke/join全部；no callback before success；zero partial handle |
| `infra_historical_binding_rotation` | new work 使用新 binding;old snapshot / intent / plan 继续旧 binding;旧 binding 丢失时 manual,不 fallback |
| `infra_forbidden_material_boundary` | secret、endpoint、route、provider body只留 private adapter memory,不进 service / store / log / report |
| `infra_fake_no_extra_success` | fake 不提供 durable adapter 不具备的成功路径或 private-state assertion |
| `infra_error_mapping` | not-found、CAS、fence、temporary、known abort、unknown、schema defect 映射 exact typed category |

### 5.5 `api`

#### 5.5.1 模块职责

`api` 是同步 16 Command / 14 Query 的 route-neutral entry composition。它校验 route 与 concrete body、构造 contracts request / metadata、调用 application context factory 与对应 façade,再把 typed result / error lossless 映射到 public response。传输产品、HTTP path / status 或 RPC method 留给 `04/07`;本模块不得将 transport 细节写进 operation、digest 或 domain object。

#### 5.5.2 文件与代码主体映射

| 文件 | 责任 |
|---|---|
| `command_handlers.rs` | Command envelope / body / metadata validation、input mapping、truth-write façade dispatch、response mapping |
| `query_handlers.rs` | Query metadata / page / visibility / consistency mapping、read façade dispatch、surface mapping |
| `routes.rs` | static public name + body type -> typed operation / handler table |
| `errors.rs` | `ProtocolError` / `ApplicationError` 到 `ApiError` 和 public code 的 lossless mapper |
| `lib.rs` | `ObservationApiServices`、handler constructors、route registry export |
| `main.rs` | 从 complete runtime 取得 API façade / validated slice 并启动 transport composition |

#### 5.5.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | 输入 | Entry 对象 / 输出 | 禁止副作用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Command mapping | static route、`ObservationCommandRequest<T>` | typed `*Input`;`ObservationCommandResponse<T>` | begin UoW、生成 owner ref、直接 domain call |
| Query mapping | `ObservationQueryMetadata`、typed request / page | typed query input、`ObservationQueryResponse<T>` / public page | refresh reference、mark stale、rebuild、read audit append |
| context construction | typed operation、actor、digest、idempotency / trace | `ObservationOperationContextFactory` result | free-text operation 作为 logical key |
| availability guard | validated API slice + safe availability state | disabled / unavailable public surface | fallback fake / direct adapter probe |
