| error mapping | `ProtocolError` / `ApplicationError` | `ApiError` + public error code | raw internal message / provider body 输出 |

#### 5.5.4 对象实现契约

```rust
pub struct ObservationApiServices {
    pub truth_write: Box<dyn ObservationTruthWriteService>,
    pub read: Box<dyn ObservationReadService>,
    pub context_factory: Box<dyn ObservationOperationContextFactory>,
}
```

API constructor 只接收上述 façade、safe availability view 和 `ValidatedApiEntryConfig`;不得接收 repository、UoW manager、resolver、publisher、delivery adapter、external target catalog 或 raw config。

| Carrier | Required fields | 构造 / 生命周期 | 边界 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ObservationCommandHandlerState` | typed `operation_name`;`runtime_available`;optional `last_entry_disposition` | static route registration + one entry result replacement | 一次性 entry carrier,不是 formal state machine |
| `ObservationQueryHandlerState` | typed operation、availability、`visibility_defaults` | static route registration | 不持久化,不得驱动 write |
| Command request mapping | exact Command metadata + concrete body | lossless `*Input` + operation context | idempotency / actor / digest / body operation 必须一致 |
| Query request mapping | exact Query metadata + concrete body / page | lossless `*Input` + query context | context 没有 idempotency reservation / write intent |

Route registry 必须是 16 + 14 个有限条目的 total static map。一个 public name 只能对应一个 concrete body、一个 typed operation 和一个 façade callable;unknown / duplicate / route-body mismatch 在 application 调用前失败。`PrepareExternalAuditExport` Command 不能被映射到同名 delivery Job operation。

#### 5.5.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

`api` 不定义或实现业务 port / adapter。允许调用面只有:

| 依赖 | 允许调用 | 禁止调用 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationTruthWriteService` | 16 个 exact Command method | generic free-text dispatch、repository / domain fallback |
| `ObservationReadService` | 14 个 exact Query method | maintenance service、projection replace、resolver refresh |
| `ObservationOperationContextFactory` | `for_command`;`for_query` | entry 直接 new idempotency scope / digest context |
| validated API slice | enabled operation、limits、static route selection | env / file / secret / endpoint / target catalog read |

Transport adapter 只包裹 handler result;它不能把 status code / timeout 解释为 application state,也不能在 response mapping failure 后重跑 use case。

#### 5.5.6 关键函数

```text
Command:
  select static route -> decode typed body -> validate metadata/ref
  -> canonical digest and context factory
  -> exact truth-write façade method
  -> map exact result/rejection/replay surface

Query:
  select static route -> validate actor/page/visibility/consistency
  -> query context factory -> exact read façade method
  -> map view + visibility + freshness + degraded + availability + missing + rebuild
```

`map_request(CommandRequestEnvelope) -> Result<ApplicationCommandInput, ApiError>` 和 `map_query(QueryRequestEnvelope) -> Result<ApplicationQueryInput, ApiError>` 必须 lossless。Command metadata 缺失、ref invalid、route/body mismatch、digest failure均不调用 façade;Query not-visible / blocked 是正常 typed response而非 transport not-found。Response mapping failure 不得构造 partial success。

#### 5.5.7 错误类型

```rust
pub enum ApiError {
    Protocol(ProtocolError),
    Application(ApplicationError),
    ResponseMappingFailed,
}
```

`Protocol` 在 façade / UoW 前产生;`Application` 保留 typed variant 和 recovery class;`ResponseMappingFailed` 表示 application result 无法 lossless 映射,不得降级为成功或重跑 mutation。Public mapper 不输出 raw request、internal error display、stack、SQL 或 provider response。

#### 5.5.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `api_handler_facade_only` | constructor / call graph 不含 repository、UoW、resolver、publisher、domain method |
| `api_route_body_totality` | 16 Command + 14 Query 每项唯一 typed route/body/callable;unknown / mismatch 在 façade 前失败 |
| `api_command_metadata_mapping` | actor、idempotency、trace、digest、operation lossless 且不从 path / free text 推导 |
| `api_query_surface_mapping` | not-visible、missing、stale、rebuilding、unavailable 保持 distinct,不触发 write |
| `api_protocol_before_uow` | invalid ref / page / metadata / route 不调用 service,service spy 和 write spy 均为零 |
| `api_error_no_message_parse` | public error 只按 typed variant 映射,不读取 error string / source chain |

### 5.6 `worker`

#### 5.6.1 模块职责

`worker` 承接 9 个 inbound event consumer 和 resident outbox publication loop。它负责 envelope / schema / dedup metadata 的 pre-parse validation、operation context mapping、façade dispatch、consumer receipt 与 runtime ack / dead-letter mapping,以及 publication loop 的 cadence / cursor carrier。它还负责实现9-slot finite Consumer handler catalog并把catalog提交给infra-owned registrar。它不得直接解析 external body、调用 resolver / publisher、读取 outbox repository、开启 UoW 或推进 domain / projection truth，也不得读取transport/policy locator或private registry。

#### 5.6.2 文件与代码主体映射

| 文件 | 责任 | 当前启用边界 |
|---|---|---|
| `consumers.rs` | 9 个 envelope validator / mapper / inbound façade dispatcher与exact handler implementation | enabled locator-free Consumer registration only |
| `outbox_publisher.rs` | cadence / candidate input mapping、publication façade dispatch、loop result mapping | `ValidatedWorkerEntryConfig` 提供 cadence / limit |
| `projection_worker.rs` | `ProjectionWorkerLoopState` 等 loop-local carrier skeleton | 当前无 maintenance façade / schedule 注入,不得推进 projection state |
| `errors.rs` | `WorkerError` 与 runtime ack / dead-letter / finalize mapping | 不暴露 transport/provider body |
| `lib.rs` | `ObservationWorkerServices`、9-slot handler catalog constructor、loop constructors | 只导出façade-based handler/catalog；不定义parallel registrar type |
| `main.rs` | 从complete runtime取得worker slice/services/registrar，`register_all`后持有opaque set | 不读raw config/adapter registry，不在成功前expose root |

#### 5.6.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | 输入 | Worker 对象 / 输出 | 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|---|
| envelope validation | `ObservationInboundEventEnvelope<T>` | typed consumer input或 rejected / unsupported receipt | required field缺失后继续 parse payload |
| Consumer dispatch | exact consumer operation + validated envelope | `ObservationConsumerReceipt` | generic free-text handler或 direct resolver call |
| runtime acknowledgement | classified receipt + transport handle | ack / redelivery / dead-letter action | ack 作为 accepted truth |
| publication scheduling | cadence、candidate limit、optional cursor | `PublishObservationOutboxInput` / batch result mapping | direct list outbox + publisher pair |
| loop diagnostics | cursor / local loop state / safe error ref | worker-local carrier | 作为 durable publication / maintenance state |
| group registration | locator-free Consumer metadata + prebuilt registrar + 9-slot catalog | `Box<dyn RegisteredInboundConsumerSet>` | locator lookup、adapter construction、partial active loops |

#### 5.6.4 对象实现契约

```rust
pub struct ObservationWorkerServices {
    pub inbound: Arc<dyn ObservationInboundEventService>,
    pub publication: Arc<dyn ObservationPublicationService>,
    pub context_factory: Arc<dyn ObservationOperationContextFactory>,
}
```

`ValidatedWorkerEntryConfig`只包含`Vec<ValidatedInboundConsumerRegistration>`、`outbox_loop_cadence`和`outbox_candidate_limit`;不包含transport/actor-policy locator、repository、resolver、publisher、external binding catalog、private registry或projection maintenance schedule。`Arc`只共享immutable façade/context factory，不能downcast。

| Carrier | Exact fields / behavior | Owner / persistence boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationConsumerDisposition` | `event_ref`;typed disposition state;optional receipt / dead-letter refs;`ack()` / `dead_letter(reason)` | worker-local result carrier;durable authority是 application stored receipt / marker |
| `OutboxPublisherLoopState` | `cursor: OutboxCursor`;typed loop kind;optional safe worker error ref;`advance(cursor)` / `mark_failed(reason)` | process-local loop carrier;cursor 只恢复 scan input |
| `ProjectionWorkerLoopState` | `target_ref`;typed loop kind;optional maintenance ref;`mark_stale(target_ref)` | 当前仅 loop-local skeleton;不得保存或推进 `ProjectionMaintenanceState` |
| `ObservationConsumerReceipt` | optional result ref、consumer name、outcome、changed / gap refs、optional quarantine / dead-letter / error | contracts public DTO,由 application result lossless 映射 |

Consumer outcome 必须保持 `Accepted`、`Duplicate`、`Delayed`、`Rejected`、`Quarantined`、`DeadLettered`、`UnsupportedSchema`、`NoOp` 八类语义。`UnsupportedSchema` 与 missing envelope field 不得解析 payload;`Duplicate` 返回 original stored receipt且不重跑 mutation;`Quarantined` 不保存 raw body;`DeadLettered` 只有正式 durable marker存在时才带 result / dead-letter ref。

`projection_worker.rs` 的 planned file 不等于当前已定义 resident maintenance capability。当前 Step 07 注入集和 Step 14 worker slice均未提供 `ObservationMaintenanceService` 或 schedule;因此该文件只能容纳 loop-local carrier / no-op registration boundary。若要启用 resident projection dispatch,必须回到 Step 07/14 明确 façade、validated slice、claim / fence 与 error mapping,不能在实现中直接调用 store。

#### 5.6.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

Worker 只允许以下三类 application callable:

| Surface | Worker 调用 | 禁止接缝 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationInboundEventService` | 9 个 exact `consume_*` method | resolver、repository、UoW、domain factory |
| `ObservationPublicationService` | `publish_observation_outbox(input)` | `ObservationOutboxRepository`、`ObservationEventPublisher` 直连 |
| `ObservationOperationContextFactory` | `for_inbound_event`;publication Job context mapping | 自行构造 actor-scope reservation / source identity |

Transport consumer / ack / dead-letter implementation属于 worker runtime seam,不是 application port 或业务 truth owner。Ack 只能在 receipt 已完成 formal classification 后执行;ack 失败保留已提交 local result,由 redelivery 触发 duplicate replay。Dead-letter runtime failure不得复制 raw envelope作为替代 marker。

`InboundConsumerHandlerCatalog`的类型shape归infra technical seam，worker按9个exact Consumer字段实现并构造。Enabled项必须`Some(handler)`、disabled项必须`None`；字段、`handler.operation()`、safe metadata的operation/producer/schema必须exact一致。禁止`Vec<dyn Fn>`、free-text map、default/unknown handler。

Registrar只向exact handler交付move-only bounded `InboundEnvelopeFrame`、operation、producer、schema和infra actor-policy已映射的`ActorSafeRef`。Frame constructor保持infra-private；unbounded descriptor或oversized frame不得调用handler，frame不得`Clone`/`Copy`/`Debug`/`Display`/`Serialize`。Transport ack token、offset、topic和private cause不进入worker；handler继续执行header-before-payload和unsupported-schema-no-parse，返回existing receipt的ack/retry/dead-letter completion。

#### 5.6.6 关键函数

```text
Inbound event:
  select exact consumer binding
  -> validate source_event_ref / source_ref / producer / schema / dedup / trace
  -> reject unsupported schema before payload parse
  -> scan typed payload boundary and compute canonical digest
  -> context_factory.for_inbound_event(...)
  -> exact inbound façade method
  -> lossless receipt mapping
  -> ack / retry / dead-letter according to typed outcome

Publication loop:
  cadence tick -> map cursor / limit to PublishObservationOutboxInput
  -> publication façade freezes or resumes immutable plan
  -> map batch result to loop cursor / safe diagnostics
```

Source version ordering只由同 producer + source 的 validated comparator决定;worker 不用 `occurred_at`、schema version、transport offset或local time选择 winner。Publication loop 不读取 payload、不选择 current route、不构造 token;这些都由 application + infra在 committed snapshot边界完成。

#### 5.6.7 错误类型

```rust
pub enum WorkerError {
    Protocol(ProtocolError),
    Application(ApplicationError),
    AckFailed,
    DeadLetterFailed,
    PublicationFinalizeFailed,
}
```

`AckFailed` 不回滚已提交 local truth;broker redelivery必须进入 duplicate replay。`DeadLetterFailed` 保留已有 local marker且不复制 raw payload。`PublicationFinalizeFailed` 不授权重新 publish;必须依据 stable token、stored marker和 probe / finalize-only规则恢复。所有 transport / provider detail均留在 runtime-private cause。

#### 5.6.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `worker_consumer_publisher_boundary` | worker 只调用 inbound / publication façade,无 repository / resolver / publisher / UoW call |
| `worker_unsupported_before_payload_parse` | unsupported schema / missing envelope field时 payload decoder和 application service均未调用 |
| `worker_duplicate_ack_recovery` | local commit后 ack fail,redelivery返回原 stored receipt且无第二 mutation / outbox |
| `worker_quarantine_body_free` | forbidden payload仅形成 formal quarantine surface,store / log / dead-letter无 raw body |
| `worker_publication_snapshot_only` | loop不读取 truth / outbox payload,只传 input并映射 batch result |
| `worker_publication_finalize_unknown` | unknown / finalize failure不盲发,保留 cursor / marker并走 probe / finalize-only |
| `worker_projection_loop_not_enabled` | current constructor无 maintenance / repository access,planned carrier不能推进 persistent projection state |

### 5.7 `jobs`

#### 5.7.1 模块职责

`jobs` 承接 9 个 one-shot Operations Job binary。每个 runner只解析 `ObservationJobRequest<T>`、校验 metadata / input、构造 operation context、选择 publication 或 maintenance façade,再把 exact stored result / report映射为 `ObservationJobResponse<T>` 与 process exit。Jobs还实现9-slot finite handler catalog并提交给infra-owned schedule registrar。Job可以维护本仓outbox marker、projection、rollup、reference snapshot、gap、replay coordination、handoff/export delivery和progress,不得作为业务Command、修source truth或生成验收结论，也不得读取schedule locator或补造Job request。

#### 5.7.2 文件与代码主体映射

| Binary / 文件 | Façade callable | 允许结果 |
|---|---|---|
| `publish_observation_outbox.rs` | `ObservationPublicationService::publish_observation_outbox` | publication marker / dead-letter / batch report |
| `rebuild_observation_read_models.rs` | `ObservationMaintenanceService::rebuild_observation_read_models` | read model、diagnostic、maintenance / progress refs |
| `rebuild_signal_rollups.rs` | `rebuild_signal_rollups` | rollup / rebuild / progress refs |
| `refresh_reference_snapshots.rs` | `refresh_reference_snapshots` | snapshot / refresh / reference view refs |
| `scan_observation_gaps.rs` | `scan_observation_gaps` | gap / scan / status refs |
| `coordinate_observation_replay.rs` | `coordinate_observation_replay` | coordination / execution / maintenance refs |
| `prepare_report_handoff_delivery.rs` | `prepare_report_handoff_delivery` | handoff / preparation / body-free receipt refs |
| `prepare_external_audit_export.rs` | `prepare_external_audit_export_delivery` | export preparation / delivery refs |
| `rebuild_peripheral_views.rs` | `rebuild_peripheral_views` | product-neutral view / progress refs |
| `lib.rs` / `errors.rs` | common metadata/input/context/report/exit mapping、9-slot handler catalog construction | 不定义第二套业务/report error taxonomy或parallel registrar type |

#### 5.7.3 Capability 与对象映射

| Capability | Input family | Application owner / result | Job entry 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|---|
| publication | cursor、limit、event filter | publication façade、immutable outbox plan / report | 直接 list / publish / finalize |
| projection / peripheral rebuild | target、canonical scopes、visibility / cursor | maintenance façade、projection / diagnostic / progress | 截断 source set后标 Fresh |
| rollup rebuild | typed scope / window refs | maintenance façade、stored SafeSignal rollup | 读取 raw metric / trace |
| reference refresh | refresh scope / snapshot refs | maintenance façade、safe resolver outcome / snapshot | 复制 external body |
| gap scan | expected source refs / visibility scope | maintenance façade、gap / degraded / report | 补造 missing source material |
| replay coordination | approved replay / no-write guard refs | maintenance façade、coordination / violation / report | source repair或扩大 scope |
| handoff / export | existing preparation / handoff + exact consumer | maintenance façade、stable intent / receipt / local marker | current target fallback、verdict / signoff |

#### 5.7.4 对象实现契约

```rust
pub struct ObservationJobMetadata {
    pub job_execution_ref: JobExecutionRef,
    pub idempotency_key: IdempotencyKey,
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>,
    pub requested_at: ObservedAt,
}
```

`job_execution_ref` 由 scheduler / job entry 提供并 lossless 映射为 application `JobRunRef` / `ObservationJobExecutionRef`;它不是 CI、runtime、report 或验收意义上的真实 `run_id`。`requested_at` 和 scheduler attempt metadata 不进入 request digest。

| Carrier | Required fields / variants | 规则 |
|---|---|---|
| `ObservationJobRequest<T>` | typed job name、metadata、exact input | job name / body type / typed operation必须静态一致 |
| `ObservationJobRunnerContext` | job / target / actor refs、idempotency key | target-bearing runner可从 metadata + DTO lossless构造;不得生成 external run identity |
| `ObservationJobResponse<T>` | result ref、job name、outcome、optional output、report surface、optional error | terminal exact stored surface;duplicate只改 public outcome为 `DuplicateReplayed` |
| `ObservationJobOutcome` | `Completed`;`PartiallyCompleted`;`FailedRetryable`;`FailedPermanent`;`Blocked`;`DuplicateReplayed` | 一次 public结果,不是 durable state machine |
| `ObservationJobReportSurface` | report ref、changed / failed / gap / progress refs、safe summary ref | draft / replay-safe report,不是 verdict / signoff / evidence alias |

Nine Job DTOs and their digest-bound target fields:

| Job | Required target / selection fields | Duplicate behavior / no-write boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `PublishObservationOutbox` | optional cursor、positive limit、canonical event filter | replay stored report;不 relist / republish |
| `RebuildObservationReadModels` | target、non-empty canonical scopes、optional approved replay scope、diagnostic visibility、minimum source cursor | 不修 source;resume用 immutable plan |
| `RebuildSignalRollups` | rollup scope、canonical window refs | 只读 stored `SafeSignal` |
| `RefreshReferenceSnapshots` | refresh scope、canonical snapshot refs | resolver只返回 body-free formal outcome |
| `ScanObservationGaps` | canonical expected source refs、visibility scope | no expected refs表示scan formal scope,不补 source |
| `CoordinateObservationReplay` | replay scope、no-write guard ref | 仅 approved observation-side derived effects |
| `PrepareReportHandoffDelivery` | handoff ref、consumer ref | same historical binding / token;无 final verdict |
| `PrepareExternalAuditExport` | preparation ref、consumer ref | Command / Job typed family静态区分;不拥有 external audit truth |
| `RebuildPeripheralViews` | canonical consumer scopes、minimum source cursor | product-neutral view;不反写核心 fact |

#### 5.7.5 Trait / Port / Adapter 契约

Jobs entry不定义业务trait或实现adapter。`ObservationJobHandler`与9-slot catalog的类型shape归infra technical seam，jobs只实现exact handler并从complete `BuiltObservabilityRuntime`选择：

| Binary group | 可持有 surface | 禁止持有 |
|---|---|---|
| publication binary | `ObservationPublicationService`;context factory;locator-free jobs slice / availability；schedule registrar where hosted | outbox repository、publisher、binding catalog、UoW、schedule locator |
| other eight binaries | `ObservationMaintenanceService`;context factory;locator-free jobs slice / availability；schedule registrar where hosted | repository、resolver、delivery、snapshot / plan factory、raw config、schedule locator |

`ObservationJobServices`统一使用`Arc`并同时持有publication、maintenance façade和context factory；每个handler只调用适用façade。`ValidatedJobsEntryConfig.job_schedules`是`Vec<ValidatedJobScheduleRegistration>`，只含operation。Schedule registrar只能转交existing九类完整`ObservationJobRequest<T>` invocation；不得从descriptor/current config/default补造`job_execution_ref`、actor、idempotency key、trace、requested time、scope、target、cursor、consumer或input。不能提供完整request的scheduler capability在startup失败，不能留到first call。

#### 5.7.6 关键函数

```text
select binary-specific typed Job operation
-> decode and validate ObservationJobRequest<T>
-> validate enabled Job / availability without reading raw config
-> canonicalize input and compute request digest
-> context_factory.for_job(operation, actor, key, digest, trace)
-> publication façade OR exact maintenance façade callable
-> lossless output + report + typed error mapping
-> deterministic process exit classification
```

Runner 不参与 application 内部 start / item / finalize UoW。Application 必须在 start UoW 固定 reservation、execution、immutable plan / config snapshot、Draft report和必要 target binding;每个 item使用 fresh claim / fence与短 UoW;finalize canonical fold item outcomes并同 UoW保存 terminal report、exact stored result和completed reservation。Runner timeout / cancellation不证明 item或 external call已回滚;重入必须重新调用同 façade,由 durable plan / probe决定 resume、in-flight、finalize-only或manual。

#### 5.7.7 错误类型

`jobs/src/errors.rs` 只做以下既有类型的 runner mapping,不得新增一个与 application report taxonomy竞争的公开 enum:

| Error source | Runner handling | 禁止映射 |
|---|---|---|
| `ProtocolError` | invalid metadata / input时在 start UoW 前返回 protocol failure,不创建假 report | `Completed` / fabricated report |
| `ApplicationError` | 按 typed recovery / public outcome映射 response与 exit | 解析 message决定 retry |
| `ApplicationError::Job(JobError)` | report mutation invariant;通常 fail closed / manual | 直接把 `JobError` 暴露为 public protocol code |
| valid `ObservationJobReportDraft` terminal state | 映射 matching public outcome / report surface | report多/少 refs或改写 terminal state |

`JobError` 的 exact variants仍是 `MissingReportReference`、`InvalidReportTransition`、`ScopeClassificationConflict`、`ReportInvariantViolation`,owner在 application durable report mutation。`FailedRetryable` 只描述本次 terminal report,不自动授权原 execution继续 mutation;下一次调用必须通过 formal idempotency / claim / plan gate。

#### 5.7.8 测试切口

| 测试 ID / 切口 | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `jobs_runner_facade_only` | 9 binary仅解析 / dispatch / map,无 repository / resolver / publisher / delivery / UoW调用 |
| `jobs_static_name_body_mapping` | 9 name、DTO、typed operation、façade callable一一对应;Command / Job同名不混淆 |
| `jobs_metadata_not_real_run` | scheduler ref / requested time不被输出或审计成真实 run、evidence、verdict / signoff |
| `jobs_duplicate_exact_report` | terminal duplicate零 candidate read / item / external call,原 report / result exact replay |
| `jobs_resume_immutable_plan` | nonterminal resume不重读 current config、不 relist / resize / reorder work set |
| `jobs_partial_and_blocked_mapping` | item outcome fold与 report / public changed / failed / gap / progress refs lossless |
| `jobs_external_unknown_no_retry` | handoff / export unknown / unsupported停止,不换 binding / token / package或伪造 Delivered |
| `jobs_no_source_repair` | 9 Job全部对 forbidden source-write adapter spy为零 |
| `jobs_registration_no_synthesized_request` | incomplete scheduler invocation不注册；无default actor/key/scope/target/cursor/input或fake run/evidence identity |
| `jobs_registration_atomic_start` | 9-slot operation/body/enablement exact；Nth prepare/arm失败zero active schedule/partial handle |

### 5.8 跨模块收口

| 检查 | 正式结论 |
|---|---|
| 七模块八段结构 | `contracts`、`domain`、`application`、`infra`、`api`、`worker`、`jobs` 均已固定职责、文件、capability、对象、port、callable、error和test切口 |
| type owner | public carrier在contracts；fact/policy在domain；service/port/technical state在application；adapter/config/builder/technical registrar shape在infra；entry只实现handler、构造finite catalog并持有opaque process handle |
| I/O owner | application定义business port、infra实现；infra另拥有非业务registration seam；entry只调用façade并提交finite handler catalog |
| truth / no-write | 任何 projection、replay、retention、handoff、export、Query、Consumer、Job均不能反写 source truth |
| external effect | accepted snapshot / intent先 durable,stable token绑定 exact historical destination,unknown先probe |
| evidence truth | report / receipt / telemetry / job execution ref均不等于真实 evidence、run、verdict或signoff |
| implementation gap policy | 若实现需要新增字段、state、trait、port、entry capability或error variant,必须回到对应 calibration Step,不得在代码中私补 |

## 6. 全局对象 / Trait / API / 状态索引

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_06_object_contracts.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_07_trait_port_adapter_contracts.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_08_protocol_contracts.md`
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_10_state_matrix.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 对象字段、factory和不变量读 Step 06 §7.4~§7.8;trait完整签名读 Step 07 §7~§12;协议 schema与DTO构造映射读 Step 08 §7.4~§7.12;状态 variant、trigger、terminal与reserved转换读 Step 10 §9~§15。本章只提供导航索引,不替代这些字段级与函数级真相源。

### 6.1 Owner 快速判定

| 需要实现的内容 | 唯一 owner | 深读入口 |
|---|---|---|
| public ref、metadata、DTO、view、event、job、receipt、public error | `contracts` | §5.1;§7;Step 06 §7.4;Step 08 |
| observation fact、policy、state、guard、append-only record formation | `domain` | §5.2;§9;Step 06 §7.5~§7.6;Step 10 |
| service、port、UoW、idempotency、stored result、outbox、Job coordination | `application` | §5.3;§8~§12;Step 06 §7.7;Step 07 |
| repository / adapter实现、config、runtime builder、technical registrar type/implementation | `infra` | §5.4;§10;§13;Step 07 §11~§12;Step 14 |
| request / envelope / runner mapping、finite handler implementation/catalog construction、opaque handle ownership | `api` / `worker` / `jobs` | §5.5~§5.7;§7~§8 |

### 6.2 对象索引

| 对象族 | 主要对象 / carrier | Owner | 关键边界 |
|---|---|---|---|
| public identity | `BodyFreeRef`;typed ref family;`RequestDigest`;`DigestSummary` | `contracts` | 不退化为裸 string;body-free digest不是raw body hash |
| ordering / scope | `ObservationCursor`;`ReferenceCursor`;`ObservationCommittedCursor`;`OutboxCursor`;`ObservationSourceVersionRef`;projection / refresh scopes | `contracts` | cursor、source version、row version、page cursor互不替代 |
| public read helper | `VisibilitySurface`;`DegradedSurface`;`ObservationConsistencyHint`;availability / missing / rebuild surface | `contracts` | not-visible、missing、stale、blocked保持distinct |
| intake / safety | `ObservationReceipt`;`SafetyDisposition`;`IntakeDecisionRecord` | `domain` | redaction-first;不保存输入正文 |
| correlation / signal | `CorrelationContext`;`SafeSignal`;`SignalRollupWindow`;`CorrelationLinkRecord` | `domain` | opaque correlation不升级为业务truth |
| audit / evidence | `AuditProjection`;`EvidenceLinkage`;`AuditAppendRecord` | `domain` | 只拥有projection / linkage,不拥有source audit / evidence body |
| handoff / authenticity | `EvidenceIndexInputView`;`ReportHandoffRecord`;`AuthenticityHint`;`HandoffLifecycleRecord` | `contracts` + `domain` | immutable body-free input;Delivered不是verdict / signoff |
| retention / replay / no-write | `RetentionMarker`;`ActiveReferenceProtection`;`ReplayScope`;`NoWriteViolation` | `domain` | 不执行source cleanup / repair |
| read / diagnostic / gap | `ReadVisibilityState`;`DiagnosticScope`;`DiagnosticRequestContext`;`DiagnosticSummary`;`GapState`;`DegradedOutputState` | `domain` | Query context transient;gap不补造成功 |
| peripheral / export | `ExternalAuditExportPreparation`;`PeripheralDeliveryState`;delivery record | `domain` | external product不是truth owner |
| reference / maintenance | `ReferenceSnapshotState`;`ProjectionMaintenanceState`;`ReplayCoordinationState`;`RollupRebuildState` | `domain` | Fresh / Resolved只描述本地derived state |
| operation / visibility | typed operation namespace;`ObservationOperationContext`;`ObservationVisibilityDecision`;application result carriers | `application` | route静态映射;Query result一次完整构造 |
| idempotency / result | `ObservationIdempotencyScope`;`ObservationIdempotencyReservation`;`StoredObservationResult` | `application` | actor-scoped reservation;exact immutable replay |
| outbox / external effect | `ObservationOutboxRecord`;payload snapshot;five stable token families | `application` | snapshot / intent先提交;exact historical binding |
| Job coordination | execution plan / item / outcome;claim / fence;`ObservationJobReportDraft` | `application` | immutable work set;terminal report封存plan |
| runtime / entry | `BuiltObservabilityRuntime`;locator-free entry slices；two registrars；finite handler catalogs；opaque registered sets | `infra` + entry | complete-or-error + all-or-nothing registration；entry carrier/handle不是业务状态机 |

#### 6.2.1 Finite support type 与 external consumer ref 索引

Current `04` Step07 的 definition/use 审计确认以下类型会同时进入 protocol、config、idempotency或external token，因此在本详细设计中固定唯一代码 owner。配置、transport和实现 boundary 不得建立平行 enum / alias：

| Type | Unique owner / planned file | Exact contract |
|---|---|---|
| `SchemaVersion` | `contracts::metadata`;`crates/contracts/src/metadata.rs` | enum仅`V1`；wire token `v1`；P0 binary-supported set=`{V1}` |
| `SourceFamilyKind` | `contracts::metadata`;same file | `Bus`;`SourceOwner`;`Identity`;`Governance`;`Artifact`;`Runtime`;`Sandbox`;`Archive`;`ReportConsumer` |
| `ObservationProducerFamily` | `contracts::metadata`;same file | 与source family同名的9个variant，但表示authenticated envelope producer，类型不可互转 |
| `ReportConsumerRef` | `contracts::refs`;`crates/contracts/src/refs.rs` | transparent `BodyFreeRef` newtype；report / archive logical consumer identity |
| `PeripheralConsumerRef` | `contracts::refs`;same file | transparent `BodyFreeRef` newtype；product-neutral peripheral consumer identity |
| `ObservationQueryOperation` | `application::idempotency`;`crates/application/src/idempotency.rs` | 14个exact Query variant，实际enum见§12.2；Query不可reserve |

```rust
pub enum SchemaVersion {
    V1,
}

pub enum SourceFamilyKind {
    Bus,
    SourceOwner,
    Identity,
    Governance,
    Artifact,
    Runtime,
    Sandbox,
    Archive,
    ReportConsumer,
}

pub enum ObservationProducerFamily {
    Bus,
    SourceOwner,
    Identity,
    Governance,
    Artifact,
    Runtime,
    Sandbox,
    Archive,
    ReportConsumer,
}

pub struct ReportConsumerRef(pub BodyFreeRef);
pub struct PeripheralConsumerRef(pub BodyFreeRef);
```

Finite enum使用exact lowercase `snake_case` wire token：`v1`以及`bus`、`source_owner`、`identity`、`governance`、`artifact`、`runtime`、`sandbox`、`archive`、`report_consumer`。禁止case-fold alias、numeric alias、unknown passthrough、`Other`或first-variant default。`SchemaVersion::V1`与`DigestProfileVersion(1)`、`StoreSchemaRevision`、`ObservationSourceVersionRef`互不替代。两个consumer ref复用`BodyFreeRef`非空/body-free校验，但不得携带endpoint、topic、path、credential、provider/report/evidence body、真实run ID、verdict或signoff，也不得互相包装转换。

### 6.3 Trait / Port / Adapter 索引

除 service façade 和 runtime builder外,所有业务 I/O trait均由 `application` 定义、`infra` 实现。`contracts`、`domain` 和三个 entry crate不定义 port。

| Port 类别 | 正式 trait / surface | 实现方 / 使用规则 |
|---|---|---|
| service façade | `ObservationTruthWriteService`;`ObservationReadService`;`ObservationInboundEventService`;`ObservationMaintenanceService`;`ObservationPublicationService` | application实现;entry只调用对应façade |
| transaction | `ObservationUnitOfWork`;`ObservationUnitOfWorkManager` | infra实现;accepted mutation使用;Query不begin |
| time / identity / context | `ClockPort`;`IdGeneratorPort`;`ObservationOperationContextFactory` | infra实现;entry不得拼ID或logical context |
| intake / signal | `ObservationIntakeRepository`;`CorrelationSignalRepository` | versioned read/save/list + append-only record |
| audit / handoff | `AuditEvidenceRepository`;`ReportHandoffRepository`;`PeripheralDeliveryRepository` | immutable evidence input、versioned state、lifecycle append |
| retention / maintenance | `RetentionGuardRepository`;`ReferenceMaintenanceRepository`;`ObservationJobReportRepository` | CAS、target binding、report terminal seal |
| projection | `ObservationProjectionSourceReader`;`ObservationProjectionMembershipPlanner`;`ObservationProjectionStore` | bounded capture、exact membership、atomic composite replace |
| idempotency / result | `ObservationIdempotencyRepository`;`ObservationStoredResultRepository` | atomic reserve;result-before-complete |
| outbox / Job execution | `ObservationOutboxRepository`;`ObservationJobExecutionRepository` | immutable snapshot;plan / claim / fence |
| body-free resolver | `ObservationSourceSummaryResolver`;`RuntimeSandboxSummaryResolver`;`GovernanceArtifactEvidenceResolver`;`SubjectObservationResolver` | infra返回formal `SafeResolution<T>` |
| publication / delivery | `ObservationEventPublisher`;`ReportHandoffDeliveryPort`;`PeripheralExportDeliveryPort` | stable token、phase probe、formal outcome |
| availability | `AdapterAvailabilityProbe` | infra构造family / exact-binding safe snapshot |
| runtime assembly | `ObservabilityRuntimeBuilder` | infra定义并实现;返回完整runtime或startup error |
| entry registration technical seam | `InboundConsumerRegistrar`;`JobScheduleRegistrar`;finite handler/catalog；opaque registered sets | infra定义shape与registrar implementation；worker/jobs实现handler；不属于application port/public protocol |

Adapter实现按同一 port contract 分为 in-memory / durable store与 fake / controlled / endpoint / disabled external mode。具体产品、DDL、transport route和credential不进入本索引。

### 6.4 API / Protocol 索引

| 协议族 | 数量 | 正式名称 | Schema / flow 入口 |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Command | 16 | `SubmitObservationMaterial`;`RecordSafetyDisposition`;`BindCorrelationContext`;`RecordSafeSignal`;`AppendAuditProjection`;`LinkBodyFreeEvidence`;`PrepareReportHandoff`;`EvaluateAuthenticityHint`;`SetRetentionMarker`;`ProtectActiveReference`;`DefineReplayScope`;`RecordNoWriteViolation`;`RecordGapState`;`PrepareExternalAuditExport`;`RegisterReferenceSnapshot`;`UpdateReferenceSnapshotState` | §7.2;§8.2;Step 08 §7.5 |
| Query | 14 | `GetObservationReceipt`;`GetIntakeStatus`;`GetSafeSignal`;`GetSignalRollup`;`GetAuditTimeline`;`GetEvidenceIndexInput`;`GetReportHandoff`;`GetRetentionProtection`;`GetObservationReadModel`;`GetDiagnosticView`;`GetGapStatus`;`GetPeripheralExportView`;`GetReferenceSnapshotView`;`GetRebuildProgress` | §7.3;§8.3;Step 08 §7.6 |
| Inbound Consumer | 9 | `ConsumeBusObservationMaterial`;`ConsumeSourceAuditMaterial`;`ConsumeIdentityObservationContext`;`ConsumeGovernanceAuditContext`;`ConsumeArtifactEvidenceContext`;`ConsumeRuntimeSignalSummary`;`ConsumeSandboxSignalSummary`;`ConsumeArchiveHandoffFeedback`;`ConsumeReportConsumerFeedback` | §7.4;§8.4;Step 08 §7.7 |
| Outbound Event | 12 | `ObservationReceiptChanged`;`SafetyDispositionChanged`;`SafeSignalRecorded`;`AuditProjectionAppended`;`EvidenceLinkageChanged`;`ReportHandoffChanged`;`RetentionMarkerChanged`;`NoWriteViolationRecorded`;`GapStateChanged`;`ReferenceSnapshotChanged`;`DerivedProjectionChanged`;`PeripheralDeliveryChanged` | §7.5;§8.5;Step 08 §7.8 |
| Operations Job | 9 | `PublishObservationOutbox`;`RebuildObservationReadModels`;`RebuildSignalRollups`;`RefreshReferenceSnapshots`;`ScanObservationGaps`;`CoordinateObservationReplay`;`PrepareReportHandoffDelivery`;`PrepareExternalAuditExport`;`RebuildPeripheralViews` | §7.6;§8.6;Step 08 §7.9 |

五族合计 60 个 public protocol surface。Command 与 Operations Job 中的 `PrepareExternalAuditExport` 必须依赖 family discriminator与typed operation做静态映射,不能按名称字符串合并。

### 6.5 状态 Owner 索引

| 状态族 | 正式 state owner | 数量 | 矩阵入口 |
|---|---|---:|---|
| observation truth / safety | `ObservationReceiptState`;`SafetyDispositionState`;`CorrelationContextState`;`SafeSignalState`;`AuditProjectionState`;`EvidenceLinkageState` | 6 | §9.2;Step 10 §9 |
| handoff / retention / gap | `ReportHandoffState`;`HandoffReadinessState`;`AuthenticityHintState`;`RetentionMarkerState`;`ActiveReferenceProtectionState`;`ReplayScopeState`;`NoWriteViolationState`;`GapLifecycleState`;`DegradedOutputKind` | 9 | §9.3;Step 10 §10 |
| read / reference / maintenance | `SignalRollupState`;`ReadVisibilityKind`;`DiagnosticFreshnessState`;`ReferenceSnapshotStateKind`;`ProjectionMaintenanceStateKind`;`ReplayCoordinationKind`;`RollupRebuildKind` | 7 | §9.4;Step 10 §11 |
| propagation / idempotency / report | `PeripheralDeliveryKind`;`ExportPreparationState`;`OutboxPublicationState`;`IdempotencyReservationState`;`JobReportState` | 5 | §9.5;Step 10 §12 |

以上合计 27 个正式 lifecycle / persisted state owner。`ObservationJobPlanItemState` 是 application technical coordination state,按 immutable plan + claim / fence + CAS 实施,但不计入 27。`ObservationCommandOutcome`、`ObservationConsumerOutcome`、`ObservationJobOutcome`、`EntryDisposition`、availability snapshot、record / ref / cursor均是一次性结果或技术 carrier,不得实现为额外 lifecycle authority。

### 6.6 索引使用与停审规则

1. 找到类型或 trait 后先按 owner进入 §5,再按本章指向的 calibration读取完整字段 / signature;不得只依据索引补实现。
2. 协议名称存在但DTO字段、target constructor或missing behavior无法闭合时,回 Step 08,不得在handler私补 optional field。
3. 状态名称存在但trigger、from/to、terminal或reserved口径不清时,回 Step 10;当前 reserved transition一律返回 typed rejection。
4. Port名称存在但函数、version、UoW或formal outcome不足时,回 Step 07 / 11 / 13;adapter不得扩展trait绕过设计。
5. 实现需要第 61 个协议、第 28 个正式state owner或新的业务port时,这是设计缺口,必须暂停并回报。

## 7. API / Command / Query / Event / Job 协议契约

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_08_protocol_contracts.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议按 Step 08 §7.4 的 shared helper、§7.5~§7.9 的五族 schema、§7.10 的 DTO-to-target闭环和 §7.11~§7.12 的 error / idempotency / public surface审计继续阅读。正式正文保留全部60个surface、字段组、target与missing behavior;逐字段Rustdoc卡片留在该中间产物。

### 7.1 Shared Protocol Helper

五类协议都使用 route-neutral finite name wrapper。`ObservationCommandName`、`ObservationQueryName`、`ObservationInboundConsumerName`、`ObservationOutboundEventName`、`ObservationJobName` 和 `ObservationProtocolSurfaceRef` 不得包含 HTTP path、topic、handler名或产品 binding。Entry必须用 total static map同时校验 family、name、concrete body和typed `ObservationOperationName`;禁止 trim / case-fold / free-text parse。

#### 7.1.0 Finite vocabulary decode boundary

§6.2.1 是finite type和consumer ref的定义索引；本章只固定协议decode顺序。Inbound header必须先验证exact `SchemaVersion::V1`、route-bound `ObservationProducerFamily`和optional source-version producer/source equality，再解析typed payload。`SourceFamilyKind`随后按§7.4 compatibility table验证。Unknown schema、producer mismatch或incompatible source family不得fallback、换route、创建reservation或写local marker。

`ReportConsumerRef` / `PeripheralConsumerRef` decoder必须先构造valid `BodyFreeRef`再包装exact owner。即使inner value相同，两类ref也不能在Command、Consumer、catalog、token或digest中互换；raw destination / credential / product config不得进入protocol。

#### 7.1.1 Command wrapper

```rust
pub struct ObservationCommandMetadata {
    pub idempotency_key: IdempotencyKey,
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>,
    pub request_digest: RequestDigest,
    pub requested_at: ObservedAt,
}

pub struct ObservationCommandRequest<T> {
    pub command_name: ObservationCommandName,
    pub metadata: ObservationCommandMetadata,
    pub body: T,
}

pub struct ObservationCommandResponse<T> {
    pub result_ref: Option<ObservationProtocolResultRef>,
    pub command_name: ObservationCommandName,
    pub outcome: ObservationCommandOutcome,
    pub result: Option<T>,
    pub changed_refs: Vec<BodyFreeRef>,
    pub outbox_refs: Vec<OutboxRecordRef>,
    pub gap_refs: Vec<GapStateRef>,
    pub error: Option<ObservationProtocolErrorSurface>,
}
```

`ObservationCommandOutcome` exact variants为 `Accepted`、`DuplicateReplayed`、`Rejected`、`Conflict`、`Delayed`、`Quarantined`。Accepted / committed Quarantined才可有新 stored result;Duplicate复用原result且不追加outbox;pre-UoW Rejected / Conflict / no-write Delayed没有虚构result ref。

#### 7.1.2 Query wrapper

```rust
pub struct ObservationQueryMetadata {
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>,
    pub visibility_scope_ref: VisibilityScopeRef,
    pub consistency: ObservationConsistencyHint,
    pub requested_at: ObservedAt,
}

pub struct ObservationQueryRequest<T> {
    pub query_name: ObservationQueryName,
    pub metadata: ObservationQueryMetadata,
    pub body: T,
}
```

Single response为 `ObservationQueryResponse<T> { query_name, surface, body: Option<T> }`;paged response为 `ObservationPublicPage<T> { surface, page, items }`。Page request使用 opaque optional cursor + positive limit;page info包含 optional next cursor、`has_more`和visible `returned_count`。Repository page helper不得直接成为public DTO。

`ObservationQuerySurface` 必须一次包含:

| Field | Exact surface | 关键不变量 |
|---|---|---|
| `visibility` | `Visible`;`Restricted`;`NotVisible`;`Blocked`;`Degraded` | not-visible不等于missing |
| `freshness` | `Fresh`;`Stale { marker_ref }`;`Rebuilding { progress_ref }`;`Unknown` | marker / progress来自persisted projection |
| `degraded` | optional `DegradedSurface` | 与visibility内degraded一致 |
| `availability` | `Available`;`Disabled`;`Unavailable`;`Failed` | unavailable不映射为not-found |
| `missing` | optional `NotFound`;`UnresolvedReference`;`MissingLinkage` | visible empty page时为None |
| `rebuild` | optional target / progress / cursor surface | Query不得触发同步rebuild |

#### 7.1.3 Event / Job wrapper

```rust
pub struct ObservationInboundEventEnvelope<T> {
    pub source_event_ref: SourceEventRef,
    pub source_ref: ObservationSourceRef,
    pub source_version_ref: Option<ObservationSourceVersionRef>,
    pub producer_family: ObservationProducerFamily,
    pub schema_version: SchemaVersion,
    pub dedup_key: IdempotencyKey,
    pub occurred_at: ObservedAt,
    pub trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>,
    pub payload: T,
}
```

Payload不得重复 envelope field。Missing required field / unsupported schema在payload parse前拒绝;source version缺失保持None,不得以`occurred_at`猜顺序。

Outbound envelope包含 outbox / event refs、event name、schema version、committed subject、tagged committed cursor、optional trace、stored time和typed payload。`ObservationOutboundEventPayloadSnapshot`还必须包含 snapshot ref、exact serialized body-free bytes和payload digest;publisher不得重建。

```rust
pub struct ObservationJobMetadata {
    pub job_execution_ref: JobExecutionRef,
    pub idempotency_key: IdempotencyKey,
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub trace_ref: Option<TraceCorrelationRef>,
    pub requested_at: ObservedAt,
}
```

Job request为name + metadata + input;response为required result ref、name、outcome、optional output、report和optional top-level error。Job outcome exact variants为 `Completed`、`PartiallyCompleted`、`FailedRetryable`、`FailedPermanent`、`Blocked`、`DuplicateReplayed`。Report surface只含report ref、body-free changed / failed / gap / progress refs和safe summary ref,不是run result、evidence alias、verdict或signoff。

#### 7.1.4 Public error surface

`ObservationProtocolErrorSurface` exact fields是 route-neutral `surface_ref`、typed `code`、body-free `issue_ref`、optional committed `gap_ref`和derived `retryable`。Error code inventory:

```text
MissingRequiredField, InvalidReference, ActorNotAllowed,
BodyFreeBoundaryViolation, IdempotencyConflict, NotVisible,
StaleProjection, DependencyUnavailable, UnsupportedSchemaVersion,
NoWriteGuardViolation, InvalidRequest, TargetNotFound,
InvalidStateTransition, PolicyRejected, VersionConflict,
ConsistencyFailure, CommitOutcomeUnknown, ExternalDeliveryFailure
```

`code`只由Step 12 typed mapper产生,`issue_ref`不是真实 evidence / run / acceptance identity。`retryable`只由正式 recovery class推导;ProbeBeforeRetry和ManualIntervention在尚未完成分类时必须为false。

### 7.2 Command 协议

Command 是唯一同步修改 observation-owned fact / marker / history / outbox / stored result的public family。所有body都不得携带raw log / metric / trace、source audit / evidence / artifact body、identity / runtime body、archive package或product config。

| # | Command / Request fields | Result DTO | Target / exact behavior | Missing / forbidden behavior |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `SubmitObservationMaterial`: source ref/family、submission purpose、optional safe summary / redaction marker | `ObservationReceiptCommandResult` | construct receipt + disposition + intake record;time / IDs由ports | source/purpose缺失reject;summary unresolved delayed;body quarantine |
| 2 | `RecordSafetyDisposition`: receipt ref、intended state、redaction marker、optional sanitized summary / quarantine reason | `SafetyDispositionCommandResult` | versioned disposition transition + intake record | missing receipt reject;invalid transition reject;forbidden body quarantine |
| 3 | `BindCorrelationContext`: receipt / source refs、optional trace / causation / seed | `CorrelationContextCommandResult` | construct / transition correlation + link record | missing receipt reject;opaque IDs不推导业务truth;partial显式 |
| 4 | `RecordSafeSignal`: context ref、signal kind、summary ref、optional runtime ref / rollup window | `SafeSignalCommandResult` | safe signal + optional rollup / correlation record | missing context reject;summary missing delayed;raw signal body reject |
| 5 | `AppendAuditProjection`: subject / context / source-audit refs、safe action summary、optional visibility | `AuditProjectionCommandResult` | append observation-owned projection + audit record | subject/source audit不得从body推导;missing summary delayed / gap |
| 6 | `LinkBodyFreeEvidence`: projection / boundary refs、digest summary、evidence purpose | `EvidenceLinkageCommandResult` | linkage + audit append record | invalid owner reject;missing digest delayed;body input quarantine |
| 7 | `PrepareReportHandoff`: handoff scope、complete `EvidenceIndexInputView`、consumer、optional visibility | `ReportHandoffCommandResult` | revalidate constituents,immutably save complete input,create handoff / lifecycle | ref-only / mismatch reject;blocked visibility保留;不生成verdict |
| 8 | `EvaluateAuthenticityHint`: handoff / evidence-input refs、canonical gap refs、evidence origin | `AuthenticityHintCommandResult` | create / transition hint + lifecycle record | unknown / unproven origin -> insufficient / placeholder;不伪造alias |
| 9 | `SetRetentionMarker`: protected ref、purpose、optional hold / release reason | `GuardCommandResult` | marker + retention record | ambiguous intent reject;active protection conflict;不执行cleanup |
| 10 | `ProtectActiveReference`: protected / consumer refs、typed reason | `GuardCommandResult` | protection canonical consumer set + record | missing owner reject;consumer不是truth owner |
| 11 | `DefineReplayScope`: canonical target refs、allowed effect、no-write boundary ref、purpose | `GuardCommandResult` | replay scope / record | empty / source-write target reject或blocked |
| 12 | `RecordNoWriteViolation`: trigger context、forbidden target、typed reason | `GuardCommandResult` | violation + append-only record | target缺失reject;attempted source write始终不执行 |
| 13 | `RecordGapState`: source ref、gap kind、optional degraded reason、limited-consumption flag | `GapStateCommandResult` | gap + optional degraded state / record | missing source/kind reject;not-visible不当missing;不补成功 |
| 14 | `PrepareExternalAuditExport`: export scope、consumer、existing export view ref、visibility | `ExternalAuditExportCommandResult` | local preparation + optional delivery marker | view missing reject/missing;blocked visibility无external call;不拥有audit truth |
| 15 | `RegisterReferenceSnapshot`: subject、optional safe summary、freshness、optional source version | `ReferenceSnapshotCommandResult` | create snapshot / refresh record | subject missing reject;no version显式unversioned/degraded;不存body |
| 16 | `UpdateReferenceSnapshotState`: snapshot ref、new state、optional safe summary、reason、optional source version | `ReferenceSnapshotCommandResult` | versioned snapshot transition / refresh record | missing snapshot reject;older不回退;uncomparable stale/degraded |

Command response result DTO必须暴露typed primary refs与current public state,不能返回domain-private object。`GuardCommandResult`的primary / state refs仍为body-free public carrier,具体state owner通过command family与changed refs确定。Accepted transaction返回的changed / outbox / gap refs必须等于实际committed set;Duplicate返回原集合,Conflict不泄漏winner material。

### 7.3 Query 协议

Query 只读取 committed observation fact、projection、view、marker、history和maintenance state。14个Query都不创建idempotency reservation,不保存read access、projection、snapshot、gap或outbox,也不在miss / stale / unavailable时触发refresh / rebuild。

| # | Query / exact request fields | Response view / identity | Read source | Empty / missing / visibility / freshness behavior |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `GetObservationReceipt`: `receipt_ref` | `ObservationReceiptView`;identity=receipt ref | intake repository | absent -> NotFound;not-visible -> body None;source may be restricted |
| 2 | `GetIntakeStatus`: intake scope + page | page of `IntakeStatusItemView`;identity=receipt ref | receipt + safety indexes | visible empty page valid;safety absent means pending,not missing |
| 3 | `GetSafeSignal`: optional signal ref or context ref + optional page | `SafeSignalProjectionView` or page;identity=signal ref | signal repository / projection | selector must be unambiguous;blocked -> no body;empty context page valid |
| 4 | `GetSignalRollup`: optional window ref or scope + page | `SignalRollupView`;identity=window ref | rollup repository / marker | stale / rebuilding explicit;invalid dual selector reject |
| 5 | `GetAuditTimeline`: subject ref + page | page of `AuditTimelineEntryView`;identity=projection ref | audit timeline read port | not-visible entry redacted / omitted,not missing;empty visible valid |
| 6 | `GetEvidenceIndexInput`: evidence-index scope + optional handoff ref | `EvidenceIndexInputView`;generated preview input ref | evidence / audit / gap reads | Query preview never saves;empty linkage valid only with exact gap / visibility semantics |
| 7 | `GetReportHandoff`: handoff ref | `ReportHandoffView`;identity=handoff ref | handoff repository | missing -> NotFound;blocked / degraded readiness explicit |
| 8 | `GetRetentionProtection`: protected ref | `RetentionProtectionView`;identity=protected ref | retention / protection repository | no marker is unmarked / missing by formal scope;active conflict refs preserved |
| 9 | `GetObservationReadModel`: projection scope + optional page | `ObservationReadModel`;generated stable view ref | projection store | no lookup -> missing;stale / rebuilding body controlled by consistency hint |
| 10 | `GetDiagnosticView`: request-context ref + canonical projection scope | `DiagnosticView`;generated stable view ref | diagnostic composite store | request ref only correlates this read;scope alone selects projection;corrupt bundle typed failure |
| 11 | `GetGapStatus`: optional gap ref or source ref + optional page | `GapStatusView`;identity=gap ref | gap repository / projection | selector unambiguous;missing gap NotFound;Suppressed仍不是Resolved |
| 12 | `GetPeripheralExportView`: consumer ref + projection scope | `DashboardAlertExportView`;generated consumer+scope identity | peripheral projection store | disabled / unavailable explicit;not-visible no body;no external call |
| 13 | `GetReferenceSnapshotView`: optional snapshot ref or subject ref | `ReferenceSnapshotView`;identity=snapshot ref | reference / projection store | unresolved / stale / unavailable explicit;selector required and unambiguous |
| 14 | `GetRebuildProgress`: maintenance target ref | `RebuildProgressView`;generated target identity | progress + maintenance / target binding | no progress -> exact missing / not-started surface;broken linkage consistency error |

Public view required field groups:

| View family | Required fields | Replacement / body-free invariant |
|---|---|---|
| receipt / intake | receipt/source refs、admission / optional safety state、received time、optional gap | no source body;receipt identity stable |
| signal / rollup | signal/window refs、kind / state、context / summary refs、count、freshness | summary ref only;no raw log / metric / trace |
| audit / evidence | projection/source-audit/linkage refs、state、visibility;input scope + canonical linkage/audit/gap refs | not-visible preserved;input preview no persistence |
| handoff / retention | handoff / consumer / input / hint refs、state/readiness;protected / marker / protection / gap refs | no verdict;active protection visible |
| `ObservationReadModel` | stable read-model + marker refs、canonical scope、canonical receipt/signal/audit refs、visibility/freshness | first create generates refs;replacement preserves refs |
| `DiagnosticView` | stable view/scope/marker refs、fresh immutable summary ref、domain / projection freshness、visibility/gaps | replacement preserves view/scope/marker and atomically replaces summary pointer |
| `DashboardAlertExportView` | stable view/marker、consumer、scope、read-model、optional diagnostic/gap、visibility/freshness | product-neutral;consumer feedback不写fact |
| gap / reference / progress | owner ref、stable marker、owner state / safe refs / target / rebuild surface | state owner remains domain;view marker stable |

`GetDiagnosticViewInput` 必须由entry lossless包含operation context、request-context ref、scope、visibility scope、consistency和requested time。Read service不得从global context猜这些字段。所有public projection identity只在lookup不存在的first create由`IdGeneratorPort`生成;replacement保留identity和marker,Query绝不生成或写入它们。

### 7.4 Inbound Consumer 协议

所有Consumer共用§7.1.3 envelope。Envelope validation先于payload parse和idempotency;payload不得重复event/source/version/schema/dedup/time/trace字段。Outcome exact variants为 `Accepted`、`Duplicate`、`Delayed`、`Rejected`、`Quarantined`、`DeadLettered`、`UnsupportedSchema`、`NoOp`。

Consumer operation到producer family是compile-time total static map；配置只能绑定transport/schema/actor，不能改变第二列：

| Consumer operation | Required producer | Payload / derived source family |
|---|---|---|
| `ConsumeBusObservationMaterial` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Bus` | explicit family仅允许`Bus`、`SourceOwner`、`Identity`、`Governance`、`Artifact`、`Runtime`、`Sandbox`中已被safety allowlist启用者 |
| `ConsumeSourceAuditMaterial` | `ObservationProducerFamily::SourceOwner` | explicit family仅允许`Governance`、`Artifact`、`Runtime`、`Sandbox`中已启用者 |
| `ConsumeIdentityObservationContext` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Identity` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::Identity` |
| `ConsumeGovernanceAuditContext` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Governance` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::Governance` |
| `ConsumeArtifactEvidenceContext` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Artifact` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::Artifact` |
| `ConsumeRuntimeSignalSummary` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Runtime` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::Runtime` |
| `ConsumeSandboxSignalSummary` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Sandbox` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::Sandbox` |
| `ConsumeArchiveHandoffFeedback` | `ObservationProducerFamily::Archive` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::Archive` |
| `ConsumeReportConsumerFeedback` | `ObservationProducerFamily::ReportConsumer` | derived `SourceFamilyKind::ReportConsumer` |

Validation固定为route/operation -> header required fields -> binary/root/binding schema intersection -> producer static map -> optional source-version equality -> typed payload/source-family compatibility -> route-bound actor/digest/event identity -> atomic reserve。任一步失败都不得按payload、transport、topic或consumer name重路由；producer mismatch属于protocol/binding violation，source-family mismatch只能拒绝/隔离，不能标记accepted truth。

| # | Consumer / payload required field group | Local target | Missing / order / body behavior |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `ConsumeBusObservationMaterial`: source family、purpose、optional safe summary / redaction marker | receipt、safety disposition、intake record | missing summary resolver/delayed;raw body quarantine;duplicate original receipt |
| 2 | `ConsumeSourceAuditMaterial`: source-audit / subject refs、optional context、safe action summary、source family | audit projection / append record / gap | missing context gap/delayed;source audit body reject;older version no write |
| 3 | `ConsumeIdentityObservationContext`: subject ref、optional safe summary、freshness | reference snapshot / refresh record | no summary -> stale/unresolved;invalid owner reject;identity body forbidden |
| 4 | `ConsumeGovernanceAuditContext`: governance evidence ref、digest、visibility | evidence boundary snapshot / reference / gap | not-visible may commit explicit surface;body / decision truth quarantine |
| 5 | `ConsumeArtifactEvidenceContext`: artifact evidence ref、digest、purpose、visibility | linkage input / audit record / snapshot | digest missing delayed;artifact/evidence body quarantine |
| 6 | `ConsumeRuntimeSignalSummary`: runtime ref、safe signal summary、kind、optional context | safe-signal input / correlation record / snapshot | context absent partial/gap;raw runtime/log/metric/trace reject |
| 7 | `ConsumeSandboxSignalSummary`: sandbox ref、optional receipt、safe summary、optional safety state | safety / signal input / snapshot | absent receipt may no-op/snapshot;unsafe body quarantine |
| 8 | `ConsumeArchiveHandoffFeedback`: archive handoff / local handoff refs、delivery result、optional safe summary | handoff lifecycle / local delivery marker | unknown handoff delayed/dead-letter;feedback body forbidden;absence no outcome proof |
| 9 | `ConsumeReportConsumerFeedback`: consumer、optional delivery ref、delivery result、optional gap kind | peripheral delivery / record / gap | unknown delivery delayed;gap may be accepted;consumer truth不反写 |

Consumer receipt mapping:

| Outcome | Required / forbidden fields | Durable / outbox behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `Accepted` | committed result ref;no error;exact changed/gap refs | may append event snapshot only for committed local change |
| `Duplicate` | original result ref;no new changed refs / error | no mutation / outbox |
| `Delayed` | typed error;result ref only when formal delayed marker committed | no truth event unless explicit local marker committed |
| `Rejected` | no result;typed error;changed empty | no outbox |
| `Quarantined` | committed result + quarantine ref + boundary error | only body-free quarantine / gap event |
| `DeadLettered` | committed marker requires result + dead-letter ref + error | body-free dead-letter event only |
| `UnsupportedSchema` | no result;typed error;payload unparsed | no stale marker / outbox |
| `NoOp` | stored result when idempotency replay required;changed empty | no outbox |

Producer ordering只能比较同 producer + source 的`ObservationSourceVersionRef`:Older不写;Equal + same digest为duplicate / no-op;Equal + different digest是conflict;Newer仍需local CAS;Uncomparable形成stale / degraded / reject,不以occurred time决胜。

### 7.5 Outbound Event 协议

Outbound Event只能来自accepted UoW中已形成的committed object / state / record。Application同时冻结event name、exact `effect_binding_ref`、subject、typed payload bytes、digest、schema、tagged cursor和trace;public payload不暴露binding。Publication failure只更新marker,不回滚owner fact。

| # | Event / exact payload fields | Committed source | Forbidden content / claim |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `ObservationReceiptChanged`: receipt/source refs、admission state、optional disposition | committed receipt | raw material |
| 2 | `SafetyDispositionChanged`: disposition/receipt refs、safety state、redaction marker | committed disposition | forbidden-body evidence |
| 3 | `SafeSignalRecorded`: signal ref/kind、context、optional rollup、safe summary | committed safe signal | raw log / metric / trace |
| 4 | `AuditProjectionAppended`: projection/subject/source-audit refs、state、visibility | committed audit projection | source audit body / business verdict |
| 5 | `EvidenceLinkageChanged`: linkage/boundary refs、state、digest、visibility | committed linkage | evidence body / alias claim |
| 6 | `ReportHandoffChanged`: handoff/consumer refs、state/readiness、optional hint | committed handoff / lifecycle | final verdict / signoff |
| 7 | `RetentionMarkerChanged`: protected ref、optional marker / protection、optional state | committed retention / protection | source cleanup completed claim |
| 8 | `NoWriteViolationRecorded`: violation/context/forbidden-target refs、state | committed violation | compensation source write |
| 9 | `GapStateChanged`: gap/source refs、kind/state、optional degraded ref | committed gap / degraded state | synthetic source material |
| 10 | `ReferenceSnapshotChanged`: snapshot/subject refs、state、optional safe summary | committed snapshot | external body / lifecycle truth |
| 11 | `DerivedProjectionChanged`: target、optional maintenance ref、freshness、optional progress | committed maintenance / progress | new business truth / false Fresh |
| 12 | `PeripheralDeliveryChanged`: delivery/consumer refs、state、optional preparation | committed delivery / export state | consumer / external audit truth |

Stored snapshot rules:

| Rule | Exact contract |
|---|---|
| identity | event ref / outbox ref / payload snapshot ref一一对应且由ID port生成 |
| subject | envelope committed subject == snapshot subject == accepted change subject |
| cursor | observation / reference namespace保留tag;与same-UoW source index / stale marker一致 |
| bytes / digest | redaction-first exact serialized bytes + digest immutable;schema与event name固定 |
| binding | application builder input冻结exact historical binding;不进入public serialized payload |
| publisher | 只读stored snapshot;不查询current owner / projection重建 |
| retry | same binding/token/bytes;ambiguous先probe;Failed不回Pending |

### 7.6 Operations Job 协议

| # | Job / exact input fields | Exact output field group | Target / missing / duplicate behavior |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `PublishObservationOutbox`: optional cursor、positive limit、canonical event filter | scanned / published / retryable / failed outbox refs | immutable eligible plan;invalid limit reject;duplicate不relist / republish |
| 2 | `RebuildObservationReadModels`: target、canonical non-empty scopes、optional replay scope、diagnostic visibility、optional minimum cursor | rebuilt read-model / diagnostic refs、maintenance ref | complete bounded capture;bad target/scope/replay/fence -> reject/blocked/failed item;不truncate |
| 3 | `RebuildSignalRollups`: rollup scope、optional signal cursor、canonical window refs | rebuilt window refs、rebuild ref、safe signal count | only stored SafeSignal;incomplete cursor不Fresh;duplicate不rebuild |
| 4 | `RefreshReferenceSnapshots`: refresh scope、freshness policy ref、canonical snapshot refs | refreshed / stale / unresolved snapshot refs | formal resolver outcome;unavailable partial/retryable;no external body |
| 5 | `ScanObservationGaps`: scan scope、canonical expected source refs、visibility scope | scan record、opened / updated / suppressed gap refs | empty expected set scans scope;no synthetic material;no-basis gap不close |
| 6 | `CoordinateObservationReplay`: replay scope、target、no-write context | coordination / execution record refs、coordination state | scope must beApproved and target-covered;guard failure Blocked;no source repair |
| 7 | `PrepareReportHandoffDelivery`: handoff scope / record / consumer refs | handoff / preparation refs、handoff state | missing/mismatch blocked/retryable;prepare/deliver same historical binding;no verdict |
| 8 | `PrepareExternalAuditExport`: export scope、consumer、preparation refs | preparation/delivery refs、formal delivery result | typed Job family maps to delivery callable;missing preparation failed;no audit conclusion |
| 9 | `RebuildPeripheralViews`: canonical consumer scopes、optional minimum cursor | rebuilt view / delivery refs、progress ref | complete committed source capture;empty/incomplete failed item;no core truth write |

Job metadata key + canonical input digest绑定整个start / item / finalize lifecycle。`job_execution_ref`和`requested_at`不进入digest。Start提交immutable plan + complete config snapshot + Draft report;resume不得重新list、改scope、改config或改binding。Terminal duplicate读取exact stored report/result,不获取new claim、不执行item / external call。Public report changed / failed / gap / progress集合必须等于durable item outcomes的lossless fold。

### 7.7 DTO 构造与协议闭环

| Closure gate | 正式规则 |
|---|---|
| field source | request / envelope / job required field来自public DTO;generated ref/time来自ID / Clock;resolved summary来自body-free port |
| target construction | 16 Command、9 Consumer、9 Job均能构造§5 owner对象 / application plan;缺字段不由默认值补齐 |
| Query construction | 14 Query均有exact request / input / view / page / marker / missing行为;zero-write |
| outbound construction | 12 Event均由committed typed object / record构造exact snapshot;不从current mutable truth重建 |
| idempotency | Command / Consumer / Job有typed operation + actor + key + digest;Query排除 |
| error | code来自typed layer;carrier result/error组合符合outcome;不解析message |
| truth boundary | 所有60个surface都不保存forbidden body、不反写source truth、不生成真实run/evidence/verdict/signoff |

实现发现任一DTO无法lossless构造目标字段、public secondary type引用domain-private类型、Query缺少body-none语义或event缺少committed snapshot来源时,必须回Step 08,不得在handler / adapter私补。

## 8. 逐接口函数级处理流

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_09_function_flows.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 09 §7.3~§7.7 的五类shared template、§8~§12的逐协议flow卡和代表性伪代码、§13的60-surface覆盖与cross-flow audit。正文保留可直接实现的调用顺序、事务切面、write set和关键分支;逐函数长伪代码留在中间产物。

### 8.1 Shared Flow Discipline

| Flow family | Entry | Application façade | 写事务 | 固定结果边界 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Command | API handler | truth-write | accepted / formal committed marker使用single UoW | exact result + history / outbox / stale / stored replay |
| Query | API handler | read | 无 | complete query surface;zero write |
| Consumer | worker handler | inbound event | accepted local mutation使用single UoW | exact receipt + local marker / snapshot / outbox |
| Outbound publication | worker / Job entry | publication | start plan、per-item finalize、terminal report分阶段短UoW | stored snapshot only;publication marker |
| Maintenance Job | Job entry | maintenance | start、每item、failure accounting、finalize分别短UoW | immutable plan + fenced outcomes + terminal report |

所有entry先做name/body / schema / metadata映射,再调用façade;不得直接访问port。所有domain mutation前必须有typed object + policy call;不得在application直接改字段。所有save existing owner必须使用同一flow刚读取的`expected_version`。

### 8.2 Command Accepted Flow

Accepted或正式 committed quarantine / blocked marker 的不可重排顺序:

```text
static route + metadata/body validation
-> canonical digest + typed operation context
-> begin one UoW
-> reserve_or_load(operation, actor, key, digest)
-> versioned load / body-free resolver / policy prerequisites
-> domain factory + transition + validate record inputs
-> stage owner / state / immutable input
-> plan exact projection membership updates from before+after roots
-> assign exactly one ObservationCursor
-> construct and append cursor-bound native history / audit records
-> record committed source/membership/positions
-> resolve exact outbound binding and construct/append immutable payload snapshot
-> mark exact affected views stale with same tagged cursor
-> save exact replay surface
-> mark idempotency Completed
-> commit
```

Replay / Conflict / InFlight在domain / resolver调用前回滚incoming UoW。Replay随后读取并校验exact stored result;missing / wrong result是consistency defect,不能重跑。任何mandatory owner、history、index、cursor、snapshot、stale marker、result或complete写失败都回滚整个accepted UoW。

| Command flow | Versioned load / resolve | Domain call / staged owner | Outbound / stale | 关键异常 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SubmitObservationMaterialFlow` | source safe summary;existing source uniqueness | receipt receive + disposition evaluate + admission transition + decision | receipt;optional safety event;intake views | unavailable delayed;forbidden body formal quarantine only |
| `RecordSafetyDispositionFlow` | receipt + current disposition | safety transition;optional receipt transition + decision | safety;optional receipt event | missing receipt / invalid transition rollback |
| `BindCorrelationContextFlow` | receipt + current context by receipt | context factory / bind + link record | affected diagnostic / derived stale | rejected/quarantined receipt不创建Bound context |
| `RecordSafeSignalFlow` | context;optional runtime/sandbox summary;rollup | signal record + rollup accept/stale + link record | safe-signal;derived stale | rollup CAS失败回滚signal |
| `AppendAuditProjectionFlow` | context;optional linkage basis | projection create / restrict / append + record | audit event;timeline/evidence stale | missing context delayed / gap;restricted不当missing |
| `LinkBodyFreeEvidenceFlow` | projection + body-free evidence resolution | candidate + link/body-block/not-visible/stale + audit record | linkage event;handoff/evidence index stale | provider body拒绝;digest missing delayed |
| `PrepareReportHandoffFlow` | every input linkage/projection/gap/visibility constituent | immutable full evidence input save;handoff draft + readiness transition + lifecycle | handoff event | preview不可信;ref-only / mismatch不提交 |
| `EvaluateAuthenticityHintFlow` | handoff + immutable input + links / gaps | hint real/placeholder/insufficient;attach + lifecycle | handoff event | 没有formal real basis不得confirm |
| `SetRetentionMarkerFlow` | marker by protected ref + active protection | hold / release-candidate / conflict + record | retention event;handoff/read stale | active consumer阻断release;不cleanup |
| `ProtectActiveReferenceFlow` | current protection / marker | canonical attach consumer + retention record | retention event | expired / concurrent set按CAS和policy处理 |
| `DefineReplayScopeFlow` | target ownership + no-write / retention basis | define + approve / block + execution record | derived maintenance event / stale | source-write target blocked并可记录violation |
| `RecordNoWriteViolationFlow` | exact trigger / target | detect + block / escalate + record | violation event | record持久化失败仍不放行write |
| `RecordGapStateFlow` | existing gap / degraded state + classification inputs | open / acknowledge / mitigate / close + record | gap event;affected views stale | close必须有observation-side basis;不修source |
| `PrepareExternalAuditExportFlow` | existing peripheral view + visibility / consumer match | local export draft + prepare / block + delivery marker | peripheral event | 本Command不external deliver;blocked无call |
| `RegisterReferenceSnapshotFlow` | subject uniqueness;optional body-free resolver result | pending + formal resolved/stale/unresolved state + refresh record | reference event with Reference cursor | no version不以time补;raw body拒绝 |
| `UpdateReferenceSnapshotStateFlow` | snapshot version + source version compare | formal transition + refresh record | reference event / affected stale with Reference cursor | Older no write;Uncomparable不覆盖current |

`ProjectionMembershipPlanner.plan_updates`必须读取当前UoW的staged + committed relations,并接收before + after relation anchors;合法`memberships=[]`表示full withdrawal。Planner不分配cursor / 写index;实际写入只在sole cursor分配后发生。

### 8.3 Query Read-only Flow

```text
static query route + metadata / selector / page validation
-> canonical query digest + for_query context
-> lossless concrete input including visibility / consistency / requested_at
-> read committed repository / projection / history / report
-> evaluate visibility and persisted freshness / availability
-> assemble exact view or page
-> return complete Query surface
```

| Query flow | Primary read | Specific assembly / branch |
|---|---|---|
| receipt / intake | intake receipt / safety indexes | missing vs visible pending;page stable |
| safe signal / rollup | signal / rollup repositories | selector validation;stale / rebuilding marker |
| audit timeline | audit timeline page | per-entry visibility redaction / omission;empty visible valid |
| evidence input | linkage page + timeline + gaps | create preview identity in memory only;no save / alias |
| handoff / retention | handoff / immutable input / protection reads | readiness / conflict / gap exact surface |
| observation read model | canonical projection lookup | body gate by consistency hint;no inline rebuild |
| diagnostic | atomic diagnostic composite + progress / target linkage | request-context仅correlation;scope selects;corruption typed failure |
| gap / peripheral / reference / progress | owner + projection / marker reads | missing、not-visible、disabled、unavailable、stale、rebuilding保持distinct |

Query不得调用`begin`、save、append、replace、mark stale、refresh、reserve idempotency或publisher。`RequireFresh`遇到Stale / Rebuilding / Unknown时body=None且保留exact surface;`AllowStale` / `BestEffort`只能返回已有committed body,不能构造placeholder。

### 8.4 Inbound Consumer Flow

```text
validate required envelope + schema before payload parse
-> static consumer mapping + canonical digest
-> build secondary identity (consumer, producer, source_event_ref)
-> begin UoW + atomic reserve_or_load
-> enforce typed source-version ordering
-> resolve safe body-free prerequisites
-> construct / transition local owner, marker, snapshot or gap
-> assign Observation cursor or Reference cursor by write family
-> source index + optional outbox snapshot + stale marker
-> save exact consumer receipt / stored result + complete
-> commit, then runtime ack / retry / dead-letter mapping
```

| Consumer flow | Local accepted write | Distinct branch |
|---|---|---|
| bus material | receipt / disposition / decision | safe summary missing delayed;body quarantine |
| source audit | audit projection / record / gap | correlation missing delayed / gap;no source audit body |
| identity context | reference snapshot / refresh | unresolved explicit;no identity truth |
| governance context | body-free evidence snapshot / gap | not-visible可accepted;no Governance decision write |
| artifact context | linkage input / snapshot / record | missing digest delayed;body quarantine |
| runtime signal | safe signal input / correlation / snapshot | partial context explicit;no execution verdict |
| sandbox signal | safety / signal marker或no-op snapshot | no receipt不伪造receipt;unsafe quarantine |
| archive feedback | handoff lifecycle / local delivery marker | unknown handoff delayed/dead-letter;absence无证明力 |
| report-consumer feedback | peripheral delivery / record / gap | unknown delivery delayed;不反写consumer truth |

Ack failure发生在local commit后时不得rollback;redelivery依赖secondary event identity找到original reservation并replay receipt。Unsupported schema不解析payload、不mark stale。Older source version不写owner / snapshot / outbox;`occurred_at`不参与winner判定。

### 8.5 Outbound Append 与 Publication Flow

Accepted source flow中的outbox helper依次:

1. 从当前staged / committed owner或record构造exact typed payload。
2. 按event name从injected safe catalog解析唯一`effect_binding_ref`;missing / duplicate / capability mismatch使accepted UoW失败。
3. 使用same-UoW tagged cursor构造event、payload snapshot和outbox marker。
4. serialization / digest / append完成后才能保存stored result并commit。

Publication flow与owner transaction分离:

```text
start UoW freezes eligible Pending / typed RetryableFailed snapshots into plan
-> acquire global Outbox work claim + fresh fence per item
-> verify plan binding == stored snapshot binding
-> publish(stable publication token, exact stored snapshot) outside DB UoW
-> short finalize UoW registers fence and CAS marks Published / Failed / DeadLettered
-> terminal report/result/idempotency finalize
```

Publication success不等于downstream consumed。Payload corrupt / missing不得查询owner重建。External result unknown先用same token probe;Published probe只做local finalize,NotPublished + formal abort proof才可policy-controlled same-token retry,Unknown / Unsupported停止自动发送。

### 8.6 Operations Job Flow

所有9个Job使用相同的staged execution:

| Phase | Atomic content | Resume / failure rule |
|---|---|---|
| entry | validate name/body/metadata;digest excludes requested time / execution ref | invalid input before valid report -> protocol error,不造report |
| start UoW | reservation、one execution、immutable canonical plan + config snapshot、Draft report、target binding / progress或external intent basis | same-digest Active -> in-flight;terminal -> replay;partial mismatch -> manual |
| item claim | global typed work key、fresh monotonically increasing fence | stale / expired claimant不能写item或failure classification |
| item work | immutable plan item + versioned committed inputs;external call在DB UoW之外 | no rescan / scope growth / current-config replacement |
| item UoW | register fence、CAS owner/derived state、structured item outcome、Draft report update | failure先rollback item,再独立accounting UoW |
| finalize UoW | no Planned / Running;canonical fold全部outcomes;target / marker fence;terminal report + stored result + complete | known finalize failure只重做finalize;unknown probe report/result/reservation |

| Job flow | Item source / local effect | Completion guard / boundary |
|---|---|---|
| publish outbox | immutable stored snapshots / publication marker | stable token + fence;no payload rebuild |
| rebuild read models | complete bounded source capture -> atomic read-model + diagnostic replacement | every bound scope classified;target aggregate recapture;all success markers未被higher stale watermark越过 |
| rebuild rollups | stored Recorded SafeSignal to fixed captured cursor | incomplete source不能Fresh;不读raw metric/trace |
| refresh references | immutable snapshot items + formal resolver outcomes | Invalid / Unresolved / Unavailable可为exact item outcome;不改external lifecycle |
| scan gaps | expected refs / committed projections -> gap / status | no safe basis不close;missing source不补造 |
| coordinate replay | Approved scope + retention / no-write guards -> coordination / record | effect仅observation-derived;Blocked保留violation / gaps |
| handoff delivery | immutable input + prepare intent / token + delivery intent / token | same historical binding;receipt后local finalize;无verdict |
| external export delivery | immutable preparation / view / consumer + symmetric intents | no current view/package/target substitution;无audit truth |
| rebuild peripheral views | committed product-neutral sources -> view / progress | visibility / retention / no-write fail保留old view stale |

Projection rebuild每个scope的capture与atomic replace必须在同一个item UoW,并验证source read fence。Source set超限 / 不完整、timestamp / position未知或fence不可建立时整个item失败,不得分页截断后标Fresh。First create才生成view / scope / marker refs;replacement保留identity并使用loaded expected version。

### 8.7 External Effect Split Transaction

Handoff / export的prepare和deliver各自遵守:

```text
phase-local UoW commits immutable intent + binding + material digest + token
-> close database transaction
-> external prepare/deliver call with exact token/material
-> if ambiguous, same-token phase probe
-> short local finalize UoW reloads owner/report/claim and commits formal result
```

Preparation token binding来自Job start config snapshot;delivery token从matching preparation / intent复制同一binding。Known external success + local finalize failure只允许finalize-only;Unknown / Unsupported不得换token、binding、target或重发。External receipt只表达transport phase fact,不等于accepted / verdict / signoff。

### 8.8 Flow Coverage 与停审规则

| Family | 正式surface | Flow coverage | 结论 |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Command | 16 | 16 | complete |
| Query | 14 | 14 | complete |
| Inbound Consumer | 9 | 9 | complete |
| Outbound payload append | 12 | 12 | complete |
| Operations Job | 9 | 9 | complete |
| shared template | 5 | 5 | complete |

实现若需要重排accepted write、在Query写入、在publisher重建payload、在Job resume重新list或在external call期间持有长事务,必须停止并回Step 09 / 11 / 13,不能作为adapter优化。

## 9. 状态机与转换矩阵

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_10_state_matrix.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 10 §6~§8 的状态主语筛选与通用规则、§9~§12 的27个逐状态完整from/to矩阵、§13~§15 的一次性结果与forbidden transition。正文保留全部variant、当前trigger、terminal、reserved和副作用边界;逐转换前置条件与ASCII图留在中间产物。

### 9.1 通用状态规则

1. 状态名和variant必须逐字使用本章定义,不得引入`Healthy`、`Done`、`Retryable`等口语别名。
2. Domain transition只更新对象并返回decision / append-only record;application在accepted UoW内保存owner、record、outbox、stale和result。
3. Existing object transition必须使用versioned load给出的`expected_version`;非法 / terminal / reserved失败不得保存owner、history、outbox或stale marker。
4. Query只投影状态,不推进任何state;external health snapshot也不自动推进domain / delivery state。
5. Terminal recovery创建新对象、scope、execution、delivery或preparation,除非矩阵明确允许同对象恢复。
6. 标为reserved的转换当前没有protocol / flow / callable,必须返回`ReservedTransition`,不得在实现中补入口。
7. 同名`Pending`、`Failed`、`Blocked`、`Delivered`只在各自owner内成立,不得跨owner自动传播。

### 9.2 Observation Truth / Safety

| # | State owner / exact variants | Initial与当前callable迁移 | Terminal / reserved | 关键副作用与边界 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `ObservationReceiptState`: `Received`;`Accepted`;`Rejected`;`Quarantined`;`Degraded`;`Superseded` | factory -> Received;Received/Degraded -> Accepted;Received/Degraded/Quarantined -> Rejected;Received/Degraded -> Quarantined;Received -> Degraded | Rejected、Superseded terminal;Accepted/Degraded/Quarantined -> Superseded reserved | Accepted需Safe/Redacted disposition;每次变更返回intake record;不保存body |
| 2 | `SafetyDispositionState`: `Pending`;`Safe`;`Redacted`;`Rejected`;`Quarantined` | factory -> Pending;Pending -> Safe/Redacted/Rejected/Quarantined;Quarantined -> Rejected | Safe、Redacted、Rejected terminal | Safe/Redacted需body-free summary且forbidden flag false;quarantine无下游eligibility |
| 3 | `CorrelationContextState`: `Unbound`;`Bound`;`Partial`;`Invalid` | factory -> Unbound;Unbound/Partial -> Bound;Unbound/Bound -> Partial;nonterminal -> Invalid;Bound -> Bound可append runtime link | Invalid terminal | source必须匹配receipt;opaque trace / causation不升级为business relation truth |
| 4 | `SafeSignalState`: `Candidate`;`Recorded`;`Suppressed`;`Stale` | factory -> Candidate;Candidate/Stale -> Recorded;Recorded -> Stale;Candidate/Recorded/Stale -> Suppressed | Suppressed terminal | Recorded才可正常rollup / event;Stale保留summary;不读取raw signal body |
