| 5 | `AuditProjectionState`: `PendingAppend`;`Appended`;`VisibilityRestricted`;`Suppressed` | factory -> PendingAppend;PendingAppend/Restricted -> Appended;PendingAppend/Appended -> Restricted;attach gap可state-preserving | Suppressed terminal且进入Suppressed reserved | append-only record与projection同UoW;不保存 / 修改source audit truth |
| 6 | `EvidenceLinkageState`: `Candidate`;`Linked`;`BodyBlocked`;`NotVisible`;`Stale` | factory -> Candidate;Candidate/Stale/NotVisible -> Linked;Candidate -> BodyBlocked;Candidate/Linked/Stale -> NotVisible;Linked/NotVisible -> Stale | BodyBlocked terminal | NotVisible不是missing;Linked需body-free policy;状态变更使handoff/evidence view stale |

### 9.3 Handoff / Retention / Gap

| # | State owner / exact variants | Initial与当前callable迁移 | Terminal / reserved | 关键副作用与边界 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 7 | `ReportHandoffState`: `Draft`;`Prepared`;`Delivered`;`Failed`;`Cancelled` | factory -> Draft;Draft/Failed -> Prepared;Prepared -> Delivered/Failed;Draft -> Failed;attach hint可state-preserving | Delivered、Cancelled terminal;Draft/Prepared/Failed -> Cancelled reserved | Prepared需readiness Ready/Degraded;Delivered仅delivery fact,非signoff |
| 8 | `HandoffReadinessState`: `PendingEvidence`;`Ready`;`Blocked`;`Degraded` | draft -> PendingEvidence;policy可从任一非final evaluation替换为Ready/Blocked/Degraded | 无跨请求terminal;每次accepted reevaluation仍需versioned handoff | 只由complete immutable input、visibility、gap、retention、no-write共同决定;Query不保存reevaluation |
| 9 | `AuthenticityHintState`: `Unassessed`;`RealEvidenceLinked`;`PlaceholderDetected`;`Insufficient` | factory -> Unassessed;Unassessed/Insufficient -> RealEvidenceLinked/PlaceholderDetected;Unassessed -> Insufficient | RealEvidenceLinked、PlaceholderDetected terminal | real需formal body-free evidence origin;Insufficient可新basis复评;不生成alias/run/verdict |
| 10 | `RetentionMarkerState`: `Unmarked`;`ActiveHold`;`ReleaseEligible`;`Released`;`Conflict` | factory -> Unmarked;Unmarked/ReleaseEligible/Conflict -> ActiveHold;Unmarked/ActiveHold/Conflict -> ReleaseEligible;nonterminal -> Conflict;archive hint可state-preserving | Released terminal;ReleaseEligible -> Released reserved | Active protection优先;ReleaseEligible不是cleanup;Released也不表示source已删除 |
| 11 | `ActiveReferenceProtectionState`: `Unprotected`;`Protected`;`Expired`;`Released`;`Conflicted` | factory -> Unprotected;Unprotected/Expired/Conflicted/Protected -> Protected;Protected -> Expired/Conflicted;Unprotected/Expired/Conflicted -> Released | Released terminal | consumer set非空时release -> RetentionConflict;release不删除protected source |
| 12 | `ReplayScopeState`: `Defined`;`Approved`;`Blocked`;`Completed`;`Cancelled` | factory -> Defined;Defined -> Approved;Defined/Approved -> Blocked/Cancelled;Approved -> Completed | Blocked、Completed、Cancelled terminal | Approved只允许observation-derived effect;retry需新scope;no source repair |
| 13 | `NoWriteViolationState`: `Detected`;`Blocked`;`Escalated`;`Closed` | factory -> Detected;Detected -> Blocked/Escalated;Blocked -> Escalated/Closed;Escalated -> Closed | Closed terminal | attempted write在Detected前后都不得执行;只保存local record / diagnostic |
| 14 | `GapLifecycleState`: `Open`;`Acknowledged`;`Resolved`;`Suppressed` | factory -> Open;Open -> Acknowledged;mitigate保持/进入Acknowledged;Open/Acknowledged/Suppressed -> Resolved | Resolved terminal;Open/Acknowledged -> Suppressed和Suppressed -> Open reserved | Resolved只说明observation-side basis闭口;Suppressed不是Resolved;不补source material |
| 15 | `DegradedOutputKind`: `None`;`Active`;`Blocked` | policy/factory -> None;None -> Active;None/Active -> Blocked;Active/Blocked恢复必须创建new policy-evaluated None replacement | Blocked对当前对象terminal | 不in-place隐藏reset;Blocked无替代success body;Query只读 |

### 9.4 Read / Reference / Maintenance

| # | State owner / exact variants | Initial与当前callable迁移 | Terminal / reserved | 关键副作用与边界 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 16 | `SignalRollupState`: `Pending`;`Fresh`;`Stale`;`Rebuilding`;`Failed` | factory -> Pending;Pending accept保持Pending;Fresh accept -> Stale;Pending/Stale/Rebuilding -> Fresh on complete seal;Fresh/Stale/Failed -> Rebuilding;Rebuilding -> Failed | 无永久terminal;Failed需formal new rebuild attempt | 只聚合stored Recorded SafeSignal;完整target cursor前不得Fresh |
| 17 | `ReadVisibilityKind`: `Visible`;`Restricted`;`NotVisible`;`Blocked` | policy creates Visible/NotVisible;Visible -> Restricted;Visible/Restricted/NotVisible -> Blocked;新request新evaluation | 每个request evaluation terminal,不跨context复用 | authorization / existence不由本状态拥有;NotVisible不映射Missing;zero-write |
| 18 | `DiagnosticFreshnessState`: `Fresh`;`Stale`;`Partial`;`Unavailable` | assembler -> Fresh/Partial/Unavailable;Fresh/Partial -> Stale;maintenance replacement从Stale/Partial/Unavailable生成new state | 无terminal;恢复只经maintenance replacement | freshness owner在DiagnosticSummary;view只投影;Query不repair |
| 19 | `ReferenceSnapshotStateKind`: `Pending`;`Resolved`;`Stale`;`Unresolved`;`Invalid`;`Unavailable` | factory -> Pending;non-Invalid + valid summary -> Resolved;Resolved/Unavailable -> Stale;Pending/Resolved/Stale/Unavailable -> Unresolved;non-Invalid -> Invalid;Pending/Resolved/Stale/Unresolved -> Unavailable | Invalid terminal,恢复需new snapshot | Resolved只是local safe snapshot;subject不变;older source version不得覆盖 |
| 20 | `ProjectionMaintenanceStateKind`: `Fresh`;`Stale`;`Rebuilding`;`Failed` | successful baseline / complete -> Fresh;missing factory -> Stale;Fresh/Failed -> Stale;Stale -> Rebuilding;Rebuilding -> Fresh/Failed | 无terminal | Fresh需全部bound member、target fence和marker成立;Query不能start |
| 21 | `ReplayCoordinationKind`: `Pending`;`Coordinating`;`Blocked`;`Completed`;`Failed` | factory -> Pending;Pending -> Coordinating/Blocked;Coordinating -> Blocked/Completed/Failed | Blocked、Completed、Failed terminal for execution | retry新execution;只协调derived/reference effect;record / report不表示source repaired |
| 22 | `RollupRebuildKind`: `Pending`;`Running`;`Completed`;`Failed`;`Cancelled` | factory -> Pending;Pending -> Running;Running -> Completed/Failed | Completed、Failed、Cancelled terminal;Pending -> Cancelled reserved | Running只读saved SafeSignal;Completed需full fixed cursor并seal rollup |

### 9.5 Propagation / Idempotency / Report

| # | State owner / exact variants | Initial与当前callable迁移 | Terminal / reserved | 关键副作用与边界 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 23 | `PeripheralDeliveryKind`: `Pending`;`Prepared`;`Delivered`;`Failed`;`Blocked`;`Cancelled` | factory -> Pending;Pending/Failed/Blocked -> Prepared;Prepared -> Delivered/Failed/Blocked;Pending/Prepared/Failed -> Blocked | Delivered、Cancelled terminal;nonterminal -> Cancelled reserved | exact consumer/view与body-free receipt;Delivered不反写consumer / observation truth |
| 24 | `ExportPreparationState`: `Draft`;`Prepared`;`Blocked`;`Delivered`;`Failed` | factory -> Draft;Draft/Failed/Blocked -> Prepared;Draft/Prepared/Failed -> Blocked;Prepared -> Delivered/Failed | Delivered terminal | no final conclusion field;Blocked无adapter call;Delivered非external audit truth |
| 25 | `OutboxPublicationState`: `Pending`;`Published`;`Failed`;`DeadLettered` | accepted append -> Pending;Pending -> Published/Failed/DeadLettered;Failed -> Published/Failed/DeadLettered via same-token eligible retry | Published、DeadLettered terminal;Failed不得回Pending | 只发布immutable snapshot;failure不rollbackowner;expected version + claim/fence |
| 26 | `IdempotencyReservationState`: `Reserved`;`Completed` | atomic absent -> Reserved;acquired writer result-before-complete -> Completed | Completed terminal | incoming same digest Reserved -> InFlight;Completed -> Replay;different digest -> Conflict;这些不是durable variants |
| 27 | `JobReportState`: `Draft`;`Completed`;`PartiallyCompleted`;`FailedRetryable`;`FailedPermanent`;`Blocked` | factory -> Draft;complete item fold后Draft ->任一terminal outcome | 除Draft外全部terminal | DuplicateReplayed不改stored state;terminal report/result/complete same UoW;不是验收report |

### 9.6 Technical Job Item Coordination

`ObservationJobPlanItemState`不计入27个正式owner,但必须作为application technical store中的明确状态实现:

| From | To | Guard / outcome rule |
|---|---|---|
| factory | `Planned` | canonical unique work key、immutable input digest / observed version;outcome None |
| `Planned` | `Running` | report Draft + Active fresh claim / monotonic fence;outcome仍None |
| `Running` | `Succeeded` | local effect与item/plan/report在同fenced UoW成功;failure refs empty / reason None |
| `Running` | `FailedRetryable` | effect UoW rollback;typed failure outcome;report仍Draft |
| `Running` | `FailedPermanent` | deterministic / permanent typed outcome |
| `Running` | `Blocked` | current policy / visibility / retention / no-write guard;protected effect未执行 |
| `Running` | `SkippedTerminal` | probe验证same work identity/token/material已有equivalent durable terminal fact |
| `FailedRetryable` | `Running` | immutable input不变、report仍Draft、fresh claim/fence;previous attempt保留append-only record |

`Succeeded`、`FailedPermanent`、`Blocked`、`SkippedTerminal`不可重进Running。Terminal report提交后整个plan封存。Finalize不得看到Planned / Running,每个finalizable item必须有compatible outcome且report恰好等于canonical fold。

### 9.7 Reserved 与 Forbidden Transition

当前必须拒绝的reserved phase:

- Receipt supersede。
- Audit projection suppress。
- Report handoff cancel。
- Retention marker release。
- Gap suppress / unsuppress。
- Rollup rebuild cancel。
- Peripheral delivery cancel。

此外,任何terminal回退、Completed reservation回Reserved、Published / DeadLettered回Pending、Delivered回Prepared、Invalid snapshot复活、Placeholder hint改写为Real、Suppressed signal重新Recorded均为forbidden。需要恢复时按owner创建新对象 / execution / preparation,不得修改history。

### 9.8 跨状态副作用闭环

| 状态变化族 | 必须同UoW保存 | 禁止副作用 |
|---|---|---|
| receipt / safety / correlation / signal | owner + native decision/link record + source index + applicable outbox/stale/result | raw body、business relation / execution truth |
| audit / evidence / handoff | owner + append/lifecycle record + immutable input where applicable + outbox/stale | source audit/evidence body、verdict/signoff |
| retention / replay / no-write / gap | owner + protection/execution/violation/gap record + marker/report | cleanup、source repair、synthetic success |
| reference / maintenance / rollup | snapshot/derived state + refresh/progress/report + exact fence | external lifecycle write、raw metric/trace、core truth mutation |
| peripheral / outbox | preparation/delivery/publication marker + formal receipt/failure/report | consumer truth write、payload rebuild、owner rollback |
| idempotency / Job report | exact stored result before completion;terminal report与item fold一致 | duplicate rerun、terminal rewrite |

每个状态测试至少覆盖factory、合法迁移、非法from/to、terminal、reserved、state-preserving operation、side-effect pairing和failure no-side-effect。完整逐转换前置条件与typed error回指Step 10 §9~§12。

## 10. 数据持久化、事务与一致性契约

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_11_persistence_transaction_consistency.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 11 §8~§10 的逐对象 ownership、logical store 与 repository 函数语义,§11~§12 的 version / cursor 与逐 flow transaction ordering,§13~§16 的 consistency invariant、failure recovery 和 logical schema notes。正文保留实现 physical adapter 和 fake / durable parity 所需的 key、version、write order、fence、recovery 与禁止行为;物理产品 DDL 和逐 store 诊断过程不在本文锁定。

本章只定义本仓 logical persistence contract,不选择数据库产品或伪造已经存在的迁移。所有 store 都只承载 observation-side truth、projection、reference、marker、history 或 technical coordination;external source body、raw log / metric / trace、source audit body、evidence / artifact body、identity / governance / runtime / archive 业务 truth 永不进入本仓持久化。

### 10.1 数据所有权实现表

| 数据对象 / 对象族 | Owner / Repository | 合法写入方 | 主要读取方 | 一致性与边界 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ObservationReceipt`;`SafetyDisposition` | `domain`;`ObservationIntakeRepository` | intake Command / Consumer | intake Query、correlation / signal、projection | owned mutable truth;admission与safety/decision同UoW;forbidden body不可序列化 |
| `CorrelationContext`;`SafeSignal`;`SignalRollupWindow` | `domain`;`CorrelationSignalRepository` | correlation / signal flow、rollup Job | signal / rollup / diagnostic Query与rebuild | owned safe observation truth / derived state;opaque correlation不升级为业务truth;rollup cursor单调 |
| `AuditProjection`;`EvidenceLinkage` | `domain`;`AuditEvidenceRepository` | audit / evidence Command与Consumer | timeline、evidence、handoff、diagnostic | owned observation projection / body-free relation;不拥有source audit或evidence body |
| immutable `EvidenceIndexInputView` | `contracts`;`ReportHandoffRepository` | `PrepareReportHandoff` accepted UoW | handoff、hint、delivery、report Query | Query只生成preview;Command逐项重读后append exact snapshot;提交后不可replace |
| `ReportHandoffRecord`;readiness;`AuthenticityHint` | `domain`;`ReportHandoffRepository` | handoff / hint Command、delivery Job / feedback Consumer | handoff Query、delivery、report surface | owned handoff / hint fact;不生成verdict、signoff、真实run id或evidence alias |
| `RetentionMarker`;`ActiveReferenceProtection`;`ReplayScope`;`NoWriteViolation`;`GapState`;`DegradedOutputState` | `domain`;`RetentionGuardRepository` | guard Command / Consumer / Job | retention、replay、diagnostic、handoff | owned local marker / violation / explainability truth;release不删除source;no-write marker失败也不放行forbidden write |
| `ReferenceSnapshotState`;`ProjectionMaintenanceState`;`ReplayCoordinationState`;`RollupRebuildState` | `domain`;`ReferenceMaintenanceRepository` | reference Command / Consumer / maintenance Job | guards、Query、projection、operations | local body-free reference / derived coordination;只最终一致,永不反写source truth |
| `ExternalAuditExportPreparation`;`PeripheralDeliveryState` | `domain`;`PeripheralDeliveryRepository` | export Command / Job / feedback Consumer | peripheral Query、report、operations | owned preparation / delivery marker;Delivered不是外部结论或验收 |
| concrete transition / lifecycle / refresh / execution records | owning family repository | accepted transition或fenced Job item | timeline、diagnostic、operations | append-only且与被解释的state同UoW;不替代current truth |
| `ReadAccessRecord` | phase-reserved logical store | 当前无合法同步writer | future explicit audit Consumer | 14 Query零写;未新增独立异步flow前禁止启用 |
| six public projection families与diagnostic composite | `ObservationProjectionStore` | projection / peripheral / refresh / gap Job | Query、handoff、report | versioned atomic replace;body、lookup、dependency、freshness同UoW;不反写owner truth |
| `ObservationOutboxRecord` + payload snapshot | `ObservationOutboxRepository` | accepted mutation append;publication Job marker update | publisher、operations | record + immutable snapshot与owner change同UoW;publisher只读stored bytes |
| idempotency reservation + `StoredObservationResult` | idempotency / stored-result repositories | Command / Consumer / Job | duplicate / recovery | actor-scoped atomic reserve;result先于complete;exact protocol surface immutable replay |
| Job plan / claim / report / external intent | job execution / report repositories | staged Job start / item / finalize | resume、duplicate、operations | immutable plan/config/work identity;global claim + monotonic fence;terminal report封存plan |
| cursor、source membership、scope position、lookup / dependency index | infra persistence support | UoW / projection adapters | repositories、Query、Job | technical truth;只由typed refs和committed cursor维护,不是业务truth |
| external source / provider / adjacent repository body | 无 owner | 无合法writer | 无合法reader | 仅允许body-free ref、safe summary、digest、visibility、freshness和typed outcome |

### 10.2 Logical store / collection / projection 契约

以下名称是logical store,不是物理表名。`repository_version`表示`ObservationRepositoryVersion`;`append-only`表示当前没有update/delete API;retention marker也不授权物理删除。

#### 10.2.1 Owned mutable truth / state

| Logical store | PK / logical unique | 关键索引 / stable order | Version / 核心规则 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `observation_receipts` | PK `receipt_ref`;current source binding唯一 | source / purpose / state;`received_at + ref` | `repository_version`;create / CAS update |
| `safety_dispositions` | PK `disposition_ref`;unique `receipt_ref` | receipt / state / forbidden flag / safe summary ref | `repository_version`;不得有raw material列 |
| `correlation_contexts` | PK `context_ref`;unique current `receipt_ref` | receipt / source / trace / state | `repository_version`;typed opaque refs only |
| `safe_signals` | PK `signal_ref` | context / kind / state / runtime safe ref;ref order | `repository_version`;safe summary only |
| `signal_rollup_windows` | PK `window_ref`;unique `(scope,window_kind)` | scope / state / source cursor | `repository_version`;cursor不回退 |
| `audit_projections`;`evidence_linkages` | projection PK;linkage PK;linkage unique `(projection,boundary,purpose)` | subject / source audit / context;projection / purpose / state | each `repository_version`;body-free refs / digest only |
| `report_handoff_records`;`authenticity_hints` | handoff PK;hint PK + unique current handoff | scope / consumer / readiness / input ref;hint state / origin | each `repository_version`;handoff state/readiness共一version |
| `retention_markers`;`active_reference_protections` | marker / protection PK;each unique current protected ref | protected ref / state / consumer membership | each `repository_version`;release前重查active set |
| `replay_scopes`;`no_write_violations` | scope / violation PK | allowed effect / state;trigger / target / state | `repository_version`;no source-repair authority |
| `gap_states`;`degraded_output_states` | gap / degraded PK | source / kind / state / cross-ref | each `repository_version`;cross-ref必须双向一致 |
| `reference_snapshot_states` | PK `snapshot_ref`;unique current `subject_ref` | source family / state / safe summary | `repository_version`;body-free snapshot only |
| `projection_maintenance_states` | PK `maintenance_ref`;unique current `target_ref` | target / state / progress | `repository_version`;target identity不可拼接 |
| `replay_coordination_states`;`rollup_rebuild_states` | coordination / rebuild PK | scope / no-write guard;window / state / cursor | each `repository_version`;one local execution fact |
| `external_audit_export_preparations`;`peripheral_delivery_states` | preparation PK;delivery PK + unique current preparation | consumer / view / visibility / state | each `repository_version`;marker不包含external body |

#### 10.2.2 Append-only history

| Logical store | Record / uniqueness | 关键索引 | 写入规则 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `intake_decision_records`;`correlation_link_records` | typed `record_ref` PK | receipt / context、change kind、recorded time、cursor | receipt / correlation transition同UoW |
| `audit_append_records`;`handoff_lifecycle_records` | typed `record_ref` PK | projection / handoff、kind、preparation / receipt ref | audit/linkage或handoff local transition同UoW |
| `retention_change_records`;`no_write_violation_records`;`gap_transition_records` | typed `record_ref` PK | owner ref、kind、actor / target、recorded time | owner transition同UoW;violation + record为atomic composite |
| `peripheral_delivery_records`;`reference_refresh_records` | typed `record_ref` PK | delivery / snapshot、outcome kind、cursor | delivery / reference update同UoW |
| `projection_maintenance_records`;`gap_scan_records`;`replay_execution_records` | typed `record_ref` PK | target / scope / report、kind、recorded time | fenced item / finalize UoW append;不声明source repaired |
| `read_access_records` | typed `record_ref` PK | request context / visibility / recorded time | reserved;同步Query不得写 |

Append-only duplicate PK必须返回conflict / invariant,不能upsert overwrite。字段只能来自当前transition、operation context、typed owner和`ClockPort`;adapter不得查询external body补齐。物理retention需要未来独立设计,当前无delete callable。

#### 10.2.3 Derived projection

| Logical projection / sidecar | PK / unique lookup | Version / replace | 不变量 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `observation_read_models` | generated view ref;unique canonical `ObservationProjectionScope` | one composite `repository_version` | first create生成view + freshness marker;replacement保留identity并原子替换body / dependency / lookup / watermark |
| `diagnostic_views`;`diagnostic_scopes`;`diagnostic_summary_snapshots` | stable view / scope refs;fresh summary ref per replacement;unique canonical scope | owning diagnostic composite version | view / scope / current-summary pointer / dependency / freshness原子;request context不落盘;old summary immutable |
| `gap_status_views`;`reference_snapshot_views` | owner ref PK;one freshness marker per view | versioned replace | marker identity稳定,body和dual watermark同UoW |
| `peripheral_export_views` | generated view ref;unique consumer + canonical scope | versioned replace | consumer lookup / dependency / visibility / freshness同UoW |
| `rebuild_progress_views` | generated progress ref;unique target lookup | versioned replace | ref lookup与target lookup必须命中同一row;target fence为`ByMaintenanceTarget` |
| `projection_freshness_markers` | marker PK;unique view ref | adapter-internal monotonic replace | observation / reference applied与stale watermark独立且均不回退 |
| `projection_dependency_index`;`projection_lookup_index` | unique source-view;unique kind-canonical scope | 与view同UoW replace | 只指向已存在view;missing row不授权拼接view ref |

#### 10.2.4 Reference / idempotency / outbox / Job technical stores

| Logical store | PK / unique | Mutability | 核心字段与规则 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `reference_scope_index` | unique `(snapshot_ref,scope discriminator,scope ref)` | snapshot / relation UoW内维护 | formal source family / subject / target relation;不扫描body或ref字符串 |
| `maintenance_target_scope_bindings` | unique target;unique target + canonical member scope | first bind immutable | same canonical set retry为no-op,different set conflict;member不得是aggregate target scope |
| `projection_source_records` | unique typed `ProjectionSourceRef` | accepted source UoW upsert | typed item、stable first `source_observed_at`、last tagged cursor;membership空仍保留row |
| `projection_source_scope_index` | unique `(canonical member scope,source ref,role)` | exact-membership replace | added / retained / removed全部推进member与bound-target position/revision |
| `projection_scope_positions` | unique canonical scope | dual cursor + revision monotonic | target aggregate为all member max;first bind禁止zero / epoch默认 |
| `evidence_index_input_snapshots` | PK `input_ref` | immutable append | scope、canonical linkage / projection / gap refs、visibility;handoff保存前逐项验证 |
| `observation_idempotency_reservations` | PK idempotency ref;unique `(operation,actor,key)` | Reserved -> Completed only | request digest、optional inbound identity、stored result pointer |
| `observation_inbound_event_identities` | unique `(consumer operation,producer family,source event ref)` | immutable secondary binding | dedup-key drift仍定位原reservation |
| `stored_observation_results` | PK result ref;unique idempotency ref | immutable append | operation / actor / digest / public ref / result kind / schema / exact serialized surface / digest |
| `observation_outbox_records`;`observation_outbox_payload_snapshots` | outbox PK;unique event;unique snapshot;one-to-one | marker versioned CAS;snapshot immutable | event / subject / tagged cursor / payload digest / schema / exact historical `effect_binding_ref`必须相等 |
| `observation_job_execution_plans` | plan PK;unique execution;unique idempotency | plan/config/work identity immutable;item CAS | complete `JobExecutionConfigSnapshot`与digest进入plan digest;resume不重新list或读current config |
| `observation_execution_claims` | active execution唯一;active global typed work key唯一 | acquire / renew / release CAS | acquire产生严格更大fencing token;commit再次验证Active owner + token |
| `observation_job_report_drafts` | report PK;unique local job ref | Draft versioned;terminal immutable | report sets / reason必须是plan item current outcome的canonical lossless fold |
| `observation_external_effect_intents` | intent PK;typed semantic token唯一 | token fields immutable;phase marker CAS | exact binding + local refs + body-free material digest;Publication token落在outbox snapshot |
| observation / reference cursor sequence | namespace + monotonic value | each accepted UoW assign once | transaction ref、opaque value、committed visibility;namespace不可混用 |

### 10.3 Repository 与 UoW 函数语义

所有不带UoW的`get/find/list/page`只读committed row。`get/find`缺失返回`Ok(None)`,`list/page`缺失返回empty page;由application决定映射Missing、Gap、create或error。page必须稳定排序并使用opaque page cursor,page cursor不携带secret/body且绝不充当version。

| Port / exact callable group | 作用 | Lock / transaction | 返回 / 失败 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ObservationUnitOfWorkManager::{begin,commit,rollback}` | 创建、原子公开、丢弃staged write set | one flow phase one UoW;entry不得直接调用 | begin失败零durable effect;known commit failure不返回accepted;unknown走probe |
| `ObservationUnitOfWork::{transaction_ref,assign_observation_cursor,assign_reference_cursor}` | 绑定transaction identity并分配唯一tagged order | truth/history staged后、index/outbox/stale/result前;同UoW只能选一个namespace | second incompatible assign为invariant;rollback cursor不可被visible row引用 |
| `ObservationIntakeRepository` | receipt / safety versioned get/find/list/save;`append_intake_decision` | save使用CAS;decision与owner transition同UoW | absent与conflict分离;forbidden serialization fail closed |
| `CorrelationSignalRepository` | correlation / signal / rollup versioned read/save/list;`append_correlation_record` | existing row必须versioned load;rollup cursor不回退 | CAS / cursor regression rollback UoW |
| `AuditEvidenceRepository` | projection / linkage get/list/save;`append_audit_record`;`page_audit_timeline` | logical unique + CAS;timeline read-only | body-boundary / dangling relation为typed invariant error |
| `ReportHandoffRepository` | evidence input get/save;handoff / hint get/find/list/save;`append_lifecycle_record` | immutable input先于handoff save;handoff/readiness one CAS | duplicate input不同内容、readiness mismatch或dangling ref冲突 |
| `PeripheralDeliveryRepository` | preparation / delivery get/find/save;`append_delivery_record` | local finalize短UoW + versioned guard | external result不得越过state / token / visibility guard |
| `RetentionGuardRepository` | retention / protection / replay / violation / gap / degraded get/list/save与append record | release前同UoW重查protection;violation + record atomic | CAS / protection / no-write / state invariant |
| `ReferenceMaintenanceRepository` | snapshot / maintenance / coordination / rebuild get/list/find/save;target scope get/bind;refresh / maintenance append | binding first-create + aggregate init同start UoW;reference update CAS | same set no-op,different set conflict;malformed index为consistency error |
| `ObservationJobReportRepository::{get_report_with_version,find_report_by_job,save_report}` | Draft / terminal report | every update exact version + current fence | terminal rewrite、fold mismatch、missing plan拒绝 |
| `ObservationProjectionSourceReader::capture` | complete bounded body-free source set + dual upper position + scope read fence | same UoW later used byreplace;无external call / persistence | incomplete / oversized / unknown dependency / missing position不截断,整个item失败 |
| `ObservationProjectionMembershipPlanner::plan_updates` | 从staged + committed formal relation计算全部exact post-commit membership | accepted UoW、cursor assignment前;只规划不写store | malformed / unbounded / incomplete closure使accepted mutation rollback |
| `ObservationProjectionStore::{record_committed_sources,resolve_affected_views,mark_views_stale}` | 写typed source / exact memberships / positions并标既有view stale | cursor后同accepted UoW;stale按namespace单调 | empty membership是full withdrawal;不得拼view ref或丢cursor tag |
| `ObservationProjectionStore` view `get/page/replace` callables | read / atomic replace observation、diagnostic、gap、peripheral、reference、progress composite | Query read-only;replace使用source position + expected version + read fence | any member / pointer / lookup / CAS mismatch回滚whole item |
| `ObservationIdempotencyRepository::{reserve_or_load,load_by_scope,load_by_inbound_event,mark_completed}` | actor-scoped atomic reserve与completion | reserve在mutation UoW起点;complete在result save之后 | absent Acquired;same digest Reserved InFlight;Completed Replay;different digest Conflict |
| `ObservationStoredResultRepository::{save_result,get_result}` | immutable exact replay surface | save与complete同UoW | completed reservation + missing / wrong result为consistency failure,不得重建 |
| `ObservationOutboxRepository::{append,list_eligible_with_payload,mark_published,mark_failed,mark_dead_letter}` | atomic record/snapshot append、eligible scan、publication CAS | append与source change同UoW;marker在fenced short UoW | missing/corrupt snapshot不从current truth重建;Failed不回Pending |
| `ObservationJobExecutionRepository::{get_plan,find_plan_by_execution,save_plan,acquire_execution_claim,acquire_item_claim,renew_claim,release_claim,register_fence}` | immutable plan、item CAS、global claim / fence | external call外无DB transaction;每次mutation注册并commit-check current fence | stale / wrong owner / non-Active token零写入并返回`ExecutionFenceConflict` |

Repository公共写语义:

1. `expected_version = None`只表示create-if-absent;PK或logical unique已存在即conflict,不覆盖。
2. `expected_version = Some(v)`必须执行compare-and-swap;affected rows不为1时整个UoW回滚。
3. `ObservationRepositoryVersion`只能来自同repository的`Versioned<T>` read,不得使用cursor、timestamp、row id、event sequence、page token、constant或external version。
4. append-only只接受当前UoW构造的typed record;duplicate identity不可upsert。
5. composite write必须由adapter保证原子,包括violation + record、outbox record + snapshot、projection body + lookup + dependency + freshness。
6. in-memory fake必须与durable adapter同样执行unique、CAS、rollback visibility、monotonic cursor、stable page、fence与exact snapshot,不得用`HashMap::insert`覆盖掩盖冲突。

### 10.4 Version、cursor 与 identity 规则

| Authority | 类型 / 来源 | 可以决定 | 禁止替代 |
|---|---|---|---|
| optimistic concurrency | `ObservationRepositoryVersion` from versioned repository read | existing mutable row CAS | observation/reference cursor、source version、timestamp、page token |
| accepted observation order | `ObservationCursor` from current UoW | observation-side source position、outbox / stale / record ordering | version、ID、trace ref、clock |
| reference-only order | `ReferenceCursor` from current UoW | reference snapshot / stale position | observation cursor裸值或external source version |
| external source order | trusted `ObservationSourceVersionRef` within same producer/source comparator namespace | Consumer older/equal/newer guard | occurred time、local clock、schema、repository version |
| projection snapshot proof | `ProjectionSourcePosition` + scope-bound read fence | captured set在replace commit仍有效 | claim token或单个row version |
| execution ownership | durable claim + strictly monotonic `ObservationFencingToken` | current worker有权提交item / report / marker | lease expiry本身、process mutex、source fence |
| semantic identity | exact typed ref / canonical typed scope / typed work key | unique lookup、membership、plan completeness | debug string、hash、JSON、path、topic、free text |

一个UoW不得同时分配observation与reference cursor;混合truth change使用observation namespace。cursor可由transactional sequence或counter row实现,允许rollback留下不可见gap,但不得复用gap。所有visible outbox / stale / progress row引用的cursor必须绑定同一`ObservationTransactionRef`。

`record_committed_sources`逐source执行:验证typed item/ref/role/scope与namespace;读取current source和旧membership;保留首次`source_observed_at`;写current source row;原子计算added / retained / removed;推进全部touched member scope positions / revisions;重算所有受影响immutable target binding的aggregate position / revision;commit前校验source row、exact membership、member和target position一致。retained membership也推进revision;empty membership删除全部旧membership但保留source row,表示validated full withdrawal。

### 10.5 Transaction boundary 与 accepted 写序

| 场景 | Begin / commit | 同UoW必须完成 | 主要回滚条件 |
|---|---|---|---|
| accepted Command | metadata validation后begin;exact result + reservation complete后commit | reservation、owner/state、history、cursor、source index、conditional outbox snapshot、stale、result、complete | missing / policy / transition / CAS / cursor / index / outbox / stale / result / commit known failure |
| accepted local Consumer | envelope/schema gate后begin;stored receipt + complete后commit | local fact/snapshot/history、tagged cursor、conditional outbox/stale、result、complete | body boundary、source-version、relation、CAS、index / result failure |
| unsupported / rejected Consumer | 通常不开UoW | none;只有Step 12正式定义durable rejection时才独立短UoW | 不解析unsupported payload,不写normal truth |
| projection / rollup / gap / reference item | immutable plan item + global claim后short UoW | current fence、one item effect、history/view/progress、item outcome、Draft report | guard / version / capture / source fence / execution fence / CAS / report |
| outbox publication item | external call后short finalize UoW | current fence、publication marker、item outcome、Draft report | token / snapshot / probe / fence / CAS / commit |
| Job start | canonical bounded work-set已确定后begin | reservation、execution/plan/config、binding、Draft report、maintenance/progress where applicable | reserve / binding / policy / capture / CAS / create |
| Job finalize | all item finalizable + execution claim后begin | target recapture、terminal maintenance/progress、terminal report、stored result、complete | completeness / freshness / target fence / report fold / result / commit |
| handoff / export intent | local guards accepted后short UoW | immutable prepare/deliver intent + exact binding + plan progress | state / visibility / retention / no-write / token / fence |
| handoff / export local finalize | formal external outcome后short UoW | local prepared/delivered/failed marker、history、conditional outbox、plan/report | token / binding / fence / CAS / invariant / commit |
| Query | no write UoW | nothing | read / map error;不得产生hidden repair |

Accepted Command与单事务Consumer的固定顺序不可重排:

```text
validate metadata / envelope and normalize logical digest
begin UoW
reserve_or_load(operation + actor + key + digest + optional inbound identity)
branch Acquired / Replay / Conflict / InFlight before resolver or domain transition
versioned load local owners and required body-free snapshots
run policy guards and domain transition
stage owner/post-state with expected version
plan exact post-commit source memberships from staged + committed typed relations
assign exactly one observation or reference cursor
construct and append mandatory cursor-bound native history / audit record
record current sources, exact memberships, member/target positions and revisions
construct and append exact immutable outbox record + payload snapshot only when a canonical event exists
resolve existing affected views and mark them stale with the same tagged cursor
save exact StoredObservationResult replay surface
mark reservation Completed with that result ref
commit and return the exact committed surface
```

Planner只输出无cursor计划;cursor assignment前不得构造或append任何需要 committed cursor 的history/audit、source position、outbox或stale marker。Domain可在此之前验证record所需body-free输入,但正式record必须由分配后的tagged cursor构造。Outbox append必须从injected binding catalog解析exact `OutboundEvent(event_name)`的唯一Publication binding并把`effect_binding_ref`固化进snapshot;raw endpoint/topic/credential不进入UoW。没有canonical outbound payload的accepted flow明确保持empty outbox refs。任何mandatory index / history / stale / result失败均回滚owner change;event publication不在该transaction内。

### 10.6 Consumer 与 Publisher 一致性

九类Consumer只保存local fact / marker / snapshot。每个accepted Consumer在transition前比较同producer + source的`ObservationSourceVersionRef`:older为no-write stale / duplicate;equal必须digest compatible;newer才允许CAS;unknown只能返回协议定义的degraded / unknown-order。不得用`occurred_at`、clock、schema、cursor或repository version猜顺序。

Consumer duplicate先由actor-scoped reservation或source-event secondary identity定位原reservation,rollback当前UoW,再读取exact stored `ObservationConsumerReceipt`;不得解析payload、调用resolver或重新写local state。其forbidden write始终包括source business truth、source body以及相邻仓lifecycle。

Publisher ordering:

```text
start UoW: reserve -> list eligible Pending / retryable Failed rows with exact snapshot
           -> freeze record/snapshot/version/binding/token into immutable plan
           -> persist config snapshot + Draft report -> commit
item:      load exact plan item -> acquire global Outbox(outbox_ref) claim
           -> reload and verify stored snapshot / digest / version / binding
           -> probe stable token when prior outcome is unknown
           -> publish exact stored payload outside any DB transaction
finalize:  short UoW reload outbox / plan / report -> register fence
           -> CAS Published / Failed / DeadLettered + exact item outcome -> commit
```

`ObservationPublicationToken(effect_binding_ref,event_ref,outbox_ref,payload_digest,schema_version)`跨attempt稳定且只能从stored snapshot复制。`Failed`不回`Pending`;same execution resume读原plan,new execution只可由eligible selector选取typed retryable failure。external success而local finalize失败时只允许same-token finalize / probe;不得blind republish、rollback original owner、重建payload、改event ref或切换current binding,也不得声称cross-system exactly-once。

### 10.7 Staged Operations Job 一致性

Job必须拆为互不嵌套的start、item、failure-accounting、finalize短UoW。Start成功前不能claim item或调用external adapter。

| Phase | 固定持久化动作 | 完成条件 / 禁止行为 |
|---|---|---|
| Start | reserve;derive one execution;freeze complete bounded work-set + `JobExecutionConfigSnapshot`;create immutable plan / Planned items / Draft report;target Job first-bind canonical scopes并初始化aggregate position;start maintenance/progress | binding、plan、config、report、maintenance/progress同UoW;resume不得重新list、改work set或读取current config |
| Item preflight | load exact plan item/config;terminal item no call;acquire global typed work claim;reload current owner;check planned identity/version、source version、retention、visibility、no-write | external call在UoW外且只用stable token / frozen binding |
| Item commit | short UoW reload plan/owner/report/binding;register fence;recheck guards;capture complete source;assemble detached objects;replace one scope;save one compatible item outcome + Draft report | source read fence、claim fence、row version三者同时有效;一个item不得推进whole target Fresh |
| Failure accounting | effect UoW先rollback;在valid claim下新开short UoW;只保存typed failed/gap outcome与Draft report | 不写view / progress / source;earlier committed items不概念回滚 |
| Finalize | execution claim;load plan/report/binding;register fence;verify无Planned/Running且每item有structured outcome;report等于canonical fold;target recapture;保存terminal maintenance/progress/report/result/complete | all member markers + target fence通过才Fresh;known finalize failure只重做finalize;unknown先probe |

Plan work-set、typed work key、planned digest / observed version、config snapshot和plan digest不可replace。每次claim reacquire产生更大token;renew不得降低;release / expiry不授权旧token。terminal report、stored result与Completed reservation同UoW提交后封存plan,任何item不得重开。report只保存local affected / failed / gap / progress refs及typed reason,不保存final verdict、验收signoff、真实evidence alias、测试结果或伪造run id。

### 10.8 External effect、Query 与 projection 边界

Handoff / export的prepare与deliver各有独立immutable intent。intent在external call前提交,包含exact `effect_binding_ref`、kind所需local refs和body-free material digest;delivery复制preparation binding。external call期间无本地transaction;local finalize重新versioned load并校验token、binding、state、readiness、visibility、retention、no-write和current fence。Unknown / Unsupported probe不能当negative;local finalize failure不授权再次deliver;resume不得从current evidence/view重建material或切换destination。

14个Query只进行committed read、visibility / freshness / availability评估和DTO assembly。它们不得begin UoW、reserve idempotency、append history / outbox、save result、refresh reference、mark stale、start rebuild或修复index。`ObservationConsistencyHint`只决定已合法取得的committed body是否可返回,不改变visibility、freshness、availability、rebuild或持久化状态。

Projection capture / replace遵守:

1. `capture(scope,uow)`在同一item UoW读取complete bounded source set、canonical target/window、observation/reference upper position和scope read fence;不完整、超限或未知dependency不能truncate后标Fresh。
2. replace保存body、version、lookup、dependency、applied dual watermark为one atomic composite;diagnostic additionally inserts new immutable summary并原子替换stable scope body / current pointer。
3. `mark_views_stale`只推进tagged cursor对应namespace;replace遇到并发更高stale watermark必须保留,旧snapshot不能清除另一namespace或更新后的stale信号。
4. Fresh要求observation与reference各自`applied >= stale`且maintenance为Fresh;跨namespace裸值不可比较。
5. persisted diagnostic `Rebuilding { progress_ref }`必须同时命中exact progress ref row、same target lookup、embedded progress/freshness、same-target Rebuilding maintenance和覆盖requested scope的immutable binding;任一missing / mismatch是consistency error,Query不猜target、不丢rebuild surface、不在线修复。
6. first target binding、all-member aggregate init、maintenance Rebuilding、target progress和Draft report同start UoW;same-UoW target capture必须read-your-writes。Finalize按immutable binding逐项核对report classification。

### 10.9 一致性策略与 cross-store invariants

| 场景 | 一致性策略 | Commit / recovery authority |
|---|---|---|
| owner + native history + result | local strong consistency | accepted UoW全有或全无;runtime log / metric / span不参与commit |
| owner -> outbox | transactional outbox + at-least-once external delivery | immutable snapshot + exact binding同UoW;publication marker独立CAS |
| owner / reference -> projection stale | local strong marker,asynchronous rebuild | source membership / position + stale同UoW;Job按capture / fence replace |
| projection body / sidecars | local atomic composite + optimistic CAS | any member failure rollbackwhole replacement;old complete bundle保留 |
| handoff / export | stable intent + split transaction + probe | intent先提交,external call无DB tx,local finalize由token / fence / CAS决定 |
| duplicate / out-of-order input | actor-scoped idempotency + source-version monotonicity | exact stored surface replay;不同digest conflict |
| Query | committed read + explicit degraded surface | zero write;corruption fail closed而非在线repair |

必须恒真的正式不变量:

| ID | Invariant | Enforced at |
|---|---|---|
| `PCI-OBS-001`~`003` | accepted receipt的safety存在且Safe/Redacted;receipt-disposition双向ref一致;SafeSignal context存在、非Invalid且body boundary通过 | intake / signal UoW + repository validation |
| `PCI-OBS-004`~`006A` | rollup cursor不回退且只聚合stored SafeSignal;audit / evidence refs body-free且logical relation唯一;handoff input immutable且constituents在accepted validation存在 | rollup / audit / handoff UoW |
| `PCI-OBS-007`~`009` | handoff state/readiness共version且不与block冲突;hint双向ref一致且placeholder不原地升级;retention Released前active set为空 | domain guard + CAS |
| `PCI-OBS-010`~`013` | replay只作用derived/reference且不声称source repaired;violation + record同生共灭;gap-degraded双向一致;reference snapshot body-free且refresh ref有效 | composite repository / item UoW |
| `PCI-OBS-014`;`014A` | projection body/version/lookup/dependency/freshness同UoW;diagnostic view/scope/summary/current pointer/dual watermark ref和scope恒一致 | projection adapter + commit validation |
| `PCI-OBS-014B`~`014F` | target canonical member set immutable;Fresh需all-member marker + target fence;source current row/exact membership/stable time/tagged cursor一致;first bind aggregate正确初始化;Rebuilding progress-maintenance-binding链不分叉 | binding / source-index / rebuild UoW + Query integrity read |
| `PCI-OBS-015`;`015A` | observation/reference applied / stale watermarks各自只前进;consistency hint无persistent landing且只控制body return | projection adapter / Query mapper |
| `PCI-OBS-016`;`016A` | outbox record、event、subject、snapshot、tagged cursor、payload字段相等;snapshot固化exact historical effect binding | accepted append |
| `PCI-OBS-017`;`017A` | publication只改marker不改snapshot/source;stable token的binding / event / outbox / digest / schema与snapshot一致,unknown先probe | publication preflight + short UoW |
| `PCI-OBS-018`~`018B` | reservation unique `(operation,actor,key)`且Completed pointer完整compatible;source-event identity唯一绑定reservation;trusted source version仅在same comparator namespace单调 | atomic reserve / Consumer guard |
| `PCI-OBS-019`~`019B` | one execution one immutable plan/report/reservation;report lossless fold item outcome;global work claim唯一且fence单调;plan持久化完整config snapshot与binding | start / item / finalize + claim store |
| `PCI-OBS-020`;`020A` | external receipt / preparation只作body-free local fact;每个effect call前已有matching immutable intent和binding,delivery继承preparation binding | intent / lifecycle store + preflight |
| `PCI-OBS-021` | one UoW不同时分配observation与reference cursor;混合change使用observation | UoW adapter |
| `PCI-OBS-022` | external body、secret、credential、raw signal、evidence body在serialization前拒绝或quarantine | boundary validator + row mapper |

### 10.10 Failure recovery 与持久化红线

| Failure family | Durable state | 允许恢复 | 禁止行为 |
|---|---|---|---|
| validation / unsupported before begin | none | exact rejection / unsupported surface | parse unsupported body或写normal marker |
| idempotency Conflict / InFlight / Replay | old reservation/result unchanged | conflict;delayed;exact stored replay | overwrite digest、第二次mutation、rerun resolver / Job |
| missing / policy / transition / optimistic conflict | only staged reservation or concurrent winner | rollback;reload / state-change handling | last-write-wins或synthetic external truth |
| history / cursor / index / outbox / stale / result failure before commit | all accepted writes staged only | rollbackwhole UoW | commit untraceable owner、publish without cursor、complete missing result |
| known commit failure | confirmed no committed transaction | typed dependency recovery | return accepted |
| commit outcome unknown | may be committed | probe exact actor-scoped reservation + stored result / marker first | blind retry或以missing cursor推断abort |
| publisher retryable / unknown / corrupt payload | source + immutable outbox already committed | same-token retry when formal;unknown probe;corrupt dead-letter / consistency | Failed回Pending、rollback owner、rebuild bytes、new token / binding |
| projection incomplete / read-fence / CAS / item failure | source committed;old projection remains | rollback item;separate fenced failure accounting;fresh recapture | partial bundle、truncate、clear newer stale、反写source |
| target binding / aggregate / finalize mismatch | old binding / item progress may exist | conflict / explicit partial or failed report;finalize-only retry | mutate member set、fabricatezero position、mark target Fresh |
| execution fence conflict | old claimant无authority | rollback;reload plan / current claim / item outcome;fresh claim only | reuse stale token或按lease expiry认定success |
| reference unavailable / Invalid | old body-free snapshot may exist | persist formal unavailable/stale outcome where flow allows;Invalid恢复建new snapshot | copyexternal body或Invalid原地Resolved |
| handoff/export external success + local finalize failure/unknown | intent + exact token;可能有receipt | same-token finalize-only;probe local then external | immediate redelivery、rebuildmaterial、switch destination |
| Job item / finalize failure | earlier item commits + Draft report | classify failed item;retry finalize after completeness audit | rerunterminal item或概念回滚earlier item |
| completed reservation missing result / corrupt index/composite/linkage | inconsistent committed state | fail closed + operations diagnostic / manual intervention | 从current truth重建结果或Query在线repair |
| no-write marker persistence failure | forbidden effect仍未执行 | return typed failure and operations signal when possible | 放行attempted source write或伪造marker已保存 |
| cursor gap after rollback | no visible row references skipped value | continue next monotonic value | reuse gap或据此声称data loss |

以下实现一律无效:`expected_version = Some(1)`、cursor / timestamp充当version、existing update使用`None`静默upsert、fake map overwrite、publisher读取current truth重建payload、external call期间持有长transaction、一个transaction包整个Job、Job duplicate重新scan、Query refresh / repair、missing historical binding fallback到current route、retention marker触发source delete。发现需要这些行为时必须停止实施并回到Step 11 / 12 / 13校准,不能作为adapter优化。

## 11. 错误模型、异常分支与恢复口径

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_12_error_recovery.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 12 §7~§9 的错误原则、exact enum与逐协议public mapping,§10~§13 的异常分支、恢复、写入和outcome语义,§14~§15 的24个consistency defect与反模式。正文保留实现typed mapper、failure branch、recovery decision和fake / durable error parity所需的完整名称;HTTP / RPC数字、retry次数 / backoff、runtime ack策略和telemetry名称不在本章定义。

错误控制流只允许匹配typed enum、state与formal outcome。message、debug string、driver code文本、provider response或issue ref都不是分类authority。Public / durable surface只披露typed code与body-free issue / gap / subject / progress / report / outbox / trace ref,不得暴露SQL、stack、path、secret、raw payload或external body。

### 11.1 错误分层与设计原则

```text
contracts constructor / envelope validation -> ProtocolError
domain factory / policy / transition          -> DomainError
application orchestration + ports + UoW       -> ApplicationError
api mapping                                   -> ApiError -> public surface
worker mapping                                -> WorkerError -> receipt / publication result
durable report mutation                       -> JobError -> ApplicationError::Job
infra private cause                           -> typed ApplicationError at port boundary
```

| Layer | Owner / 可见错误 | 必须表达 | 禁止事项 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `contracts` | `ProtocolError` | malformed ref / envelope / route-body / schema / page | repository、network、domain state或retry策略 |
| `domain` | `DomainError` | object invariant、policy、scope、boundary、transition | adapter、UoW、transport数字、backoff |
| `application` | `ApplicationError`;`JobError` | orchestration、port、transaction、consistency、external finalize | raw driver / provider cause、free-text classification |
| `api` | `ApiError` | protocol/application到route-neutral public error的lossless mapping | 直接泄漏domain / infra detail |
| `worker` | `WorkerError` | envelope、application、ack / dead-letter / publication finalize failure | unsupported payload继续解析 |
| `jobs` | existing protocol/application/report types | runner input、report outcome和exit classification | 另建竞争性public job error enum |
| `infra` | private error only | 在port boundary立即映射typed application error / formal outcome | private cause穿透、持久化或驱动domain mutation |

通用原则:

1. Query missing、not-visible、stale、rebuilding、disabled、unavailable和degraded通常是正常read surface;只有malformed input、read port failure或persisted invariant defect进入error。
2. 普通rejected / invalid / conflict / unsupported / not-visible分支不得append正常history / outbox或mark projection Fresh。只有正式Command本身拥有quarantine、gap、no-write等local marker时才可提交negative fact。
3. Forbidden body只能产生body-free quarantine / gap / decision marker;raw body本身在任何错误分支都不落盘。
4. Duplicate只replay exact stored result / receipt / report;missing或wrong-kind stored surface是consistency defect,不重跑body。
5. Projection、reference、publication、handoff、export与Job恢复只修改本仓derived / marker / report,永不修复或反写source truth。
6. Commit / external outcome unknown必须先probe;在无法证明未提交或未交付前不得重执行。
7. Transient、state/input change、reload、finalize-only、probe和manual必须分开;`retryable=true`不等于授权立即循环。

### 11.2 Exact error type 契约

#### 11.2.1 Contracts、Domain 与 Job error

```rust
pub enum ProtocolError {
    InvalidRef,
    InvalidEnvelope,
    RouteBodyMismatch,
    UnsupportedSchemaVersion,
    InvalidPageCursor,
}

pub enum DomainError {
    MissingRequiredReference,
    ScopeMismatch,
    InvalidStateTransition,
    ReservedTransition,
    PolicyRejected,
    ReadNotAllowed,
    SafetyBoundaryViolation,
    BodyFreeBoundaryViolation,
    AuthenticityBoundaryViolation,
    ReferenceBoundaryViolation,
    ReplayBoundaryViolation,
    CorrelationConflict,
    ReferenceConflict,
    RetentionConflict,
    GapInvariantViolation,
    RollupInvariantViolation,
    RollupIncomplete,
    HandoffNotReady,
    MaintenanceIncomplete,
}

pub enum JobError {
    MissingReportReference,
    InvalidReportTransition,
    ScopeClassificationConflict,
    ReportInvariantViolation,
}
```

所有`ProtocolError`都在write UoW前产生。除`UnsupportedSchemaVersion`在producer改变schema前属于`DoNotRetrySameInput`,其余属于`RetryAfterInputChange`。`DomainError`只能由factory / policy / transition产生且失败时不得留下部分mutation。`JobError`不直接进入public protocol;application按发生阶段映射failed / blocked / conflict / manual。

#### 11.2.2 Application error

```rust
pub enum ApplicationError {
    InvalidRequest,
    InvalidPageCursor,
    UnsupportedSchemaVersion,
    OwnedStateNotFound,
    Domain(DomainError),
    IdempotencyConflict,
    IdempotencyInFlight,
    CompletedReservationResultMissing,
    StoredResultKindMismatch,
    InvalidStateTransition,
    ReservedTransition,
    OptimisticConflict,
    ExecutionFenceConflict,
    RepositoryUnavailable,
    ReferenceUnavailable,
    ResolverUnavailable,
    PublisherUnavailable,
    DeliveryUnavailable,
    AdapterDisabled,
    SerializationFailed,
    CursorAllocationFailed,
    CommitFailed,
    CommitOutcomeUnknown,
    RollbackFailed,
    OutboxInvariantViolation,
    OutboxPayloadMissing,
    OutboxPayloadCorrupt,
    ProjectionAssemblyFailed,
    ProjectionScopeMismatch,
    ProjectionFreshnessMarkerMismatch,
    ProjectionIndexCorrupt,
    RebuildProgressLinkMissing,
    RebuildMaintenanceLinkMissing,
    RebuildTargetBindingMissing,
    MaintenanceTargetMissing,
    MaintenanceTargetBindingMissing,
    MaintenanceTargetBindingConflict,
    ReplayScopeMissing,
    JobReportMissing,
    MaintenanceIncomplete,
    EvidenceIndexInputMismatch,
    DiagnosticCompositeCorrupt,
    PersistenceInvariantViolation,
    ExternalDeliveryFailed,
    ExternalFinalizeUnknown,
    NoWritePersistenceFailed,
    Job(JobError),
}
```

Variant名称是控制流authority。Optional issue、subject、gap、report或trace refs由surrounding context承载,不改变classification。Raw message、SQL code、stack、path和external body不是enum field。

#### 11.2.3 Entry 与 adapter mapping error

```rust
pub enum ApiError {
    Protocol(ProtocolError),
    Application(ApplicationError),
    ResponseMappingFailed,
}

pub enum WorkerError {
    Protocol(ProtocolError),
    Application(ApplicationError),
    AckFailed,
    DeadLetterFailed,
    PublicationFinalizeFailed,
}
```

`ResponseMappingFailed`不能降级成partial success或触发mutation rerun。`AckFailed`不回滚已提交local truth,redelivery必须走duplicate replay。`DeadLetterFailed`不复制raw payload。`PublicationFinalizeFailed`不授权重新publish。

| Infra-private cause | Required mapping | 禁止映射 |
|---|---|---|
| row not found | caller-context `OwnedStateNotFound` / formal missing | `RepositoryUnavailable` |
| CAS affected rows = 0 | `OptimisticConflict` | generic persistence failure |
| stale claim / fencing token | `ExecutionFenceConflict` | optimistic conflict或repository unavailable |
| temporary store / resolver / publisher / delivery unavailable | corresponding unavailable variant | domain policy rejection |
| deterministic serialization / schema / digest mismatch | serialization / corruption variant | retryable unavailable |
| backend proves transaction aborted | `CommitFailed` | accepted outcome |
| timeout / disconnect after commit send | `CommitOutcomeUnknown` | known failure |
| invalid credential / configuration | `AdapterDisabled`或manual configuration defect | raw provider message |
| formal external failure outcome | `ExternalDeliveryFailed`或publication marker outcome | repository failure |

### 11.3 Recovery classification

```rust
pub enum ObservationRecoveryClass {
    DoNotRetrySameInput,
    RetryAfterInputChange,
    RetryAfterStateChange,
    RetryAfterReload,
    RetryAfterDependencyRecovery,
    RetryFinalizeOnly,
    ProbeBeforeRetry,
    ManualIntervention,
}
```

| Recovery class | Public `retryable` | 允许动作 | 禁止动作 |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `DoNotRetrySameInput` | false | return exact rejection / conflict / blocked | loop same request |
| `RetryAfterInputChange` | false | caller修正typed metadata/body-free input/ref后new attempt | 静默补齐或规范化forbidden / missing data |
| `RetryAfterStateChange` | false | 等待formal owner/reference/policy/config/claim state变化 | timer-only blind retry |
| `RetryAfterReload` | true | rollback,重新读`Versioned<T>`,重新跑全部guard | reuse old expected version |
| `RetryAfterDependencyRecovery` | true | dependency确实恢复后new attempt | fabricate dependency outcome |
| `RetryFinalizeOnly` | true | 复用durable preparation / receipt / item classification,只重做local finalize | 再次external call或重做completed item |
| `ProbeBeforeRetry` | false | probe reservation/result/marker/plan或exact external token | 假定committed / aborted / delivered |
| `ManualIntervention` | false | fail closed,保留body-free refs并暴露operations defect | 从current truth重建immutable history / payload / result |

主要variant mapping:

| Internal variants / family | Recovery class |
|---|---|
| `InvalidRequest`;`InvalidPageCursor` | `RetryAfterInputChange` |
| `UnsupportedSchemaVersion`;`IdempotencyConflict`;reserved / deterministic invalid transition | `DoNotRetrySameInput` |
| `IdempotencyInFlight`;owned state/reference missing;policy/readiness block;`AdapterDisabled` | `RetryAfterStateChange` |
| `OptimisticConflict`与并发型reference / retention / binding conflict | `RetryAfterReload` |
| `ExecutionFenceConflict` | `RetryAfterStateChange`;先reload plan / claim / item / report,旧token不可复用 |
| repository / resolver / publisher / delivery unavailable;known transient cursor allocation | `RetryAfterDependencyRecovery` |
| external success + known local finalize failure | `RetryFinalizeOnly` |
| `CommitOutcomeUnknown`;`ExternalFinalizeUnknown` | `ProbeBeforeRetry` |
| missing / wrong stored result、rollback unknown、deterministic serialization、outbox / projection / diagnostic / persistence corruption、`NoWritePersistenceFailed` | `ManualIntervention` |
| `MaintenanceIncomplete` | valid pending state用`RetryAfterStateChange`;persisted impossible classification用manual |

### 11.4 Public error surface 与 carrier 不变量

`ObservationProtocolErrorCode`是唯一public error code owner,exact variants为:

```rust
pub enum ObservationProtocolErrorCode {
    MissingRequiredField,
    InvalidReference,
    ActorNotAllowed,
    BodyFreeBoundaryViolation,
    IdempotencyConflict,
    NotVisible,
    StaleProjection,
    DependencyUnavailable,
    UnsupportedSchemaVersion,
    NoWriteGuardViolation,
    InvalidRequest,
    TargetNotFound,
    InvalidStateTransition,
    PolicyRejected,
    VersionConflict,
    ConsistencyFailure,
    CommitOutcomeUnknown,
    ExternalDeliveryFailure,
}
```

`ObservationProtocolErrorSurface`必须包含canonical `surface_ref`、code、body-free `issue_ref`、optional existing `gap_ref`和由`ObservationRecoveryClass`推导的`retryable`。Mapper不得创建 / 关闭gap,不得把runtime evidence、run、acceptance或telemetry identity填入issue ref。

| Public code family | Default recovery / semantic class | 特殊约束 |
|---|---|---|
| missing / invalid field/ref/request | input change / invalid request | real owner missing使用`TargetNotFound`,不伪装malformed input |
| `ActorNotAllowed`;`NotVisible` | state change / forbidden | Query通常返回normal visibility surface |
| `BodyFreeBoundaryViolation` | input change | forbidden body已持久化则升级manual containment |
| `IdempotencyConflict` | no same-input retry / conflict | same digest duplicate无error |
| `DependencyUnavailable` | dependency recovery / unavailable | typed in-flight例外为state change且retryable=false |
| `StaleProjection` | state change | 只能走独立maintenance protocol,Query不inline retry |
| `UnsupportedSchemaVersion`;`NoWriteGuardViolation` | no same-input retry | no-write persistence失败仍不得放行原write |
| `InvalidStateTransition`;`PolicyRejected` | no same-input或state change / semantic rejection | state/policy变化后才是new attempt |
| `VersionConflict` | reload / conflict | execution fence例外为state/claim change,不是CAS retry |
| `ConsistencyFailure` | manual / internal consistency | pre-save bad input不得映射成consistency |
| `CommitOutcomeUnknown` | probe / indeterminate | retryable=false直到probe完成 |
| `ExternalDeliveryFailure` | formal outcome决定dependency/manual | external success + local failure改用finalize-only / probe |

| Carrier | `error=None` | `error=Some` | `result_ref`规则 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Command | `Accepted`;`DuplicateReplayed` | `Rejected`;`Conflict`;`Delayed`;`Quarantined` | committed Accepted/Quarantined与Duplicate为`Some`;pre-UoW reject/conflict/delayed为`None` |
| Consumer receipt | `Accepted`;`Duplicate`;`NoOp` | `Delayed`;`Rejected`;`Quarantined`;`DeadLettered`;`UnsupportedSchema` | committed Accepted/Quarantined/stored NoOp与Duplicate为`Some`;pre-UoW / delayed为`None` |
| Job response | `Completed`;`DuplicateReplayed`;可由report完整解释的partial | top-level reason required的partial / failed / blocked | 所有response必须已有compatible result/report pair;否则不构造response |

一个domain object state名为`Rejected`不等于Command outcome `Rejected`:若intake / safety negative decision作为本仓owned fact成功提交,operation仍是`Accepted`或formal committed `Quarantined`。同理`ObservationJobOutcome`不是`JobReportState`,duplicate不得改写terminal report,terminal `FailedRetryable`也不自动授权sealed execution继续mutation。

### 11.5 Command 与 Query mapping

| Command internal branch | Outcome / public code | Recovery | Durable effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| invalid envelope / missing metadata / route-body / digest | Rejected;missing / invalid request | input change | no UoW / no write |
| malformed / wrong-owner ref | Rejected;`InvalidReference` | input change | none |
| required owner row missing | Rejected;`TargetNotFound` | state/input change by source | staged reservation rollback |
| invalid / reserved transition | Rejected;`InvalidStateTransition` | no same-input retry | rollback;no normal outbox |
| policy / readiness reject | Rejected;`PolicyRejected` | state change | rollback unless formal marker flow owns fact |
| actor / visibility denied | Rejected;actor / not-visible code | state change | no hidden audit write |
| forbidden body | Quarantined或Rejected;boundary code | input change / manual if persisted | only formal body-free quarantine family may commit |
| ownership write attempt | Rejected;`NoWriteGuardViolation` | no same-input retry | attempted write blocked;only formal local violation flow may write |
| completed duplicate | `DuplicateReplayed`;no error | exact replay | current UoW rollback;zero new write |
| same digest Reserved | Delayed;typed in-flight via dependency code | state change;retryable=false | rollback before resolver / domain |
| same key different digest | Conflict;`IdempotencyConflict` | no same-input retry | old reservation/result unchanged |
| optimistic CAS conflict | Conflict;`VersionConflict` | reload | rollback;no last-write-wins |
| temporary dependency unavailable | Delayed;`DependencyUnavailable` | dependency recovery | no synthetic gap unless separate flow owns it |
| accepted-UoW known failure | no success response | exact dependency / consistency code | whole UoW rollback |
| commit unknown | no accepted/rejected claim;`CommitOutcomeUnknown` | probe | no second mutation / compensation event |
| completed result missing / wrong kind | no replay response;`ConsistencyFailure` | manual | fail closed;do not reconstruct |

Query `Ok(ObservationQueryResult<T>)`正常承载not found / empty、not-visible / blocked、stale / rebuilding / unknown freshness、disabled / unavailable with valid persisted surface。Invalid request/page/ref、repository无法形成合法read、dangling lookup/index/composite/progress linkage才返回entry error。

| Query branch | Public behavior | Recovery / write rule |
|---|---|---|
| canonical lookup absent / empty page | normal missing / visible empty | zero write |
| not-visible / blocked | body none + exact visibility | zero write;不扩大授权 |
| stale / rebuilding | exact marker / validated progress;body按hint | separate maintenance only;不wait、不start/finalize Job |
| disabled / unavailable | exact availability | dependency/config change;不enable / refresh |
| repository unavailable | semantic dependency error | dependency recovery |
| lookup -> missing body/marker | `ConsistencyFailure` | manual;no placeholder / repair |
| diagnostic scope/view/summary/progress/maintenance/binding mismatch | matching typed corruption -> `ConsistencyFailure` | body unavailable,zero write |
| `RequireFresh` + stale或BestEffort无allowed degraded body | body none,保留真实surface | 不补default / Fresh |

### 11.6 Consumer、Publisher 与 Operations Job mapping

| Consumer branch | Outcome / error | Write / ack boundary |
|---|---|---|
| malformed envelope | Rejected;missing / invalid request | no payload parse / no local mutation |
| unsupported schema | `UnsupportedSchema`;`UnsupportedSchemaVersion` | no payload parse / stale / outbox;runtime policy later |
| same digest duplicate | `Duplicate`;no error | rollback current UoW,exact stored receipt;ack without reapply |
| same digest Reserved | Delayed;typed in-flight | no parse/apply;runtime policy later,无immediate loop |
| key digest conflict / source-event key drift mismatch | Rejected或Quarantined;idempotency conflict | original reservation wins;never second execution |
| older / equal source version | NoOp / Duplicate / Delayed by flow | no local regression;不按clock/schema/cursor猜顺序 |
| raw / forbidden body | Quarantined;boundary code | only body-free quarantine marker may commit |
| resolver / repository temporary unavailable | Delayed;dependency unavailable | no fabricated snapshot |
| formal unresolved / not-visible outcome | Accepted / NoOp / Delayed by flow | may commit explicit local snapshot/gap if formal flow owns it |
| accepted local mutation | Accepted | ack only aftercommit;outbox usesstored snapshot |
| valid no state change | NoOp | no hidden write except formal stored receipt contract |
| permanent safe-envelope defect | DeadLettered only afterformal marker commit | raw payload不进入dead-letter marker |

Publisher failure只推进publication marker与fenced Job report,不回滚source owner。Temporary publisher failure -> `Failed`;formal permanent -> `DeadLettered`;missing / corrupt snapshot -> consistency/manual且绝不重建;CAS conflict reload winner;success后local marker known failure -> finalize-only;unknown -> probe marker + provider token;duplicate publication Job只replay report,不重新list。

| Job flow family | Retryable / partial | Permanent / blocked / manual | Recovery boundary |
|---|---|---|---|
| publication | publisher unavailable;item CAS / fence conflict | payload missing/corrupt;formal permanent failure | per-item marker;duplicate report replay |
| projection / peripheral rebuild | temporary store / CAS / source fence conflict | incomplete/oversized capture;corrupt index/composite;binding mismatch | failed item rollback + separate accounting;old view保留 |
| rollup | repository unavailable;CAS conflict | rollup invariant / unbounded incomplete source | retain old rollup;never raw metric/trace |
| reference refresh | resolver unavailable | forbidden body、invalid subject、deterministic mapping defect | formal Unresolved / Unavailable可作为valid item outcome |
| gap scan | store / projection unavailable | gap/degraded invariant、synthetic source attempt | preserve existing gap;no material fabrication |
| replay coordination | temporary derived dependency unavailable | replay / no-write / retention block;corrupt scope | no source repair;only approved derived effects |
| handoff / export delivery | adapter temporary;known finalize failure | readiness/no-write block、input mismatch、formal permanent external failure | intent committed first;external call outsideUoW;same token only |

| Job branch | Public outcome | Durable report rule |
|---|---|---|
| all items + finalize complete | `Completed` | `JobReportState::Completed` |
| successes and exact failed items coexist | `PartiallyCompleted` | exact failed / gap / affected refs |
| transient execution outcome | `FailedRetryable` | terminal report不授权same execution继续;new call经formal plan/claim gate |
| deterministic / permanent | `FailedPermanent` | typed reason required |
| policy / visibility / retention / no-write block | `Blocked` | local marker/report only |
| terminal duplicate | `DuplicateReplayed` | original report不变 |
| terminal report/result missing或malformed | no replay outcome | consistency/manual;不重跑body |

Handoff / export的`Prepared` / `Delivered`只表示transport lifecycle。External temporary failure保留preparation并等待dependency recovery;permanent failure只保存body-free failure / receipt;external success + known local finalize failure只做same-token finalize;unknown同时probe local marker/result与external token。任何external receipt中的verdict、signoff、evidence body、真实run id或真实evidence alias必须作为boundary violation拒绝,不能落盘或升级为truth。

### 11.7 异常分支处理表

| 场景 | 检测位置 | 处理方式 | Durable audit / event |
|---|---|---|---|
| route/body mismatch、missing metadata、invalid ref/page/schema/digest | entry / contracts | exact `ProtocolError`;write façade前停止 | none |
| adapter disabled before operation | application availability guard | family-specific delayed / blocked / availability | none;不自动改配置 |
| duplicate / conflict / in-flight | reservation repository | rollback;replay / conflict / wait | no new history/event/result |
| owner missing / domain reject / reserved transition | versioned load / domain guard | rollback;typed target / policy / transition code | ordinary rejection none;formal marker flow exception |
| mandatory history / planner / cursor / index / outbox / stale / result / complete failure | accepted UoW | rollbackwhole UoW | none;不得留下partial accepted fact |
| commit known failed | UoW manager | only afterbackend proves abort,return typed failure | none |
| commit / rollback unknown | UoW manager | indeterminate;probe or manual | no compensation event / certainty claim |
| Query missing / invisible / stale / unavailable | read service / mapper | normal exact surface | none |
| Query persisted mismatch | repository / linkage validator | typed consistency failure,body unavailable | none;no repair |
| Consumer ack failure after commit | worker runtime | `AckFailed`;local result remains | no rollback;redelivery duplicate |
| Publisher payload defect / external failure | preflight / publisher | no publish or marker-only failure | valid short UoW marker/report only |
| Job duplicate / in-flight | start gate | report replay or already-running;no new list / plan | none |
| Job item capture / assembly / fence failure | item phase | rollback effect;separate fenced failure accounting if authority remains | report classification only |
| stale worker loses fence | commit validation | rollback;reload/wait;stale worker不得写failure classification | none from stale worker |
| finalize incomplete / conflict | finalize guard / CAS | explicit partial/failed target;finalize-only reload | report/progress only whenvalid UoW commits |
| external call temporary / permanent / unknown | stable intent boundary | typed failed marker;or exact-token probe;Unknown / Unsupported stop | local body-free lifecycle/report only |
| external success + local finalize fail | short finalize UoW | known -> finalize-only;unknown -> probe | no Delivered/event/result untilfinalize commit |
| no-write marker persistence fails | violation repository | attempted forbidden effect remains blocked | no normal event;marker不得伪造 |

### 11.8 Recovery decision 与 ownership

Recovery decision固定顺序:

```text
classify exact typed error / formal outcome
determine whether requested local mutation committed,known-aborted,or indeterminate
if indeterminate:probe durable local identity and exact external token where applicable
determine whether immutable result / plan / intent / payload exists and is compatible
select exactly one ObservationRecoveryClass
apply only the action owned by that class and recovery owner
```

| Recovery target | Allowed owner / effect | 禁止效果 |
|---|---|---|
| Command retry / replay | application + reservation/result repos;exact replay或reload后new accepted UoW | bypass policy、overwrite winner、reuse stale version |
| Consumer retry | worker + inbound service;redeliver envelope,duplicate replay | parse unsupported/raw body或second execution |
| Outbox publication | publication service;stored snapshot + marker CAS | rebuild payload、change binding、source rollback |
| Projection / read model | maintenance service;rebuildderived bundle / progress / report | source truth write或Query repair |
| Reference snapshot | refresh Job + resolver;new safe snapshot / refresh record | external body copy或alter external owner |
| Handoff / export | maintenance + delivery port;prepare/deliver/finalize local marker | claim verdict / signoff、blind redelivery |
| Job item / finalize | exact immutable plan + fresh durable claim;resume pending item或finalize | regenerate plan / config、rerunterminal item |
| Consistency defect | operations/manual path to be bound later;inspect body-free refs,formal repair only | ad hoc data edit被表示成normal success |

| Failure truth | Recovery | 禁止 |
|---|---|---|
| optimistic conflict | rollback + reload current version/state | same-version retry |
| execution fence conflict | reload current claim/plan/item/report;fresh claim afterstate allows | treat as row CAS或reuse token |
| commit unknown | probe reservation/result/owner/outbox marker | blind rerun accepted mutation |
| projection item failed | old bundle stays;separate failure accounting | partial view / target Fresh |
| Job finalize known failed | retry finalize aftercompleteness audit | rerunitems |
| Job finalize unknown | probe report/result/reservation | assume terminal / nonterminal |
| reference formal Unresolved/Unavailable | explicit refresh afterstate/dependency change | external body copy |
| reference Invalid | create new snapshot underformal protocol | in-place Invalid -> Resolved |
| external temporary failure | retain intent/preparation;dependency recovery | source rollback / switch binding |
| external outcome unknown | exact stable-token probe | new token、new material、blind retry |
| external success + local finalize known fail | finalize-only with exact receipt/preparation | redelivery |
| forbidden body already persisted | fail closed + immediate manual containment | normal read/export/publish |

### 11.9 Error branch write 与 event gate

| Branch | Local durable write | Outbox / event | Stored replay |
|---|---|---|---|
| invalid request/ref/page/route、unsupported schema | none | none | none |
| ordinary Command domain/policy rejection | none;staged reservation rollback | none | none |
| accepted negative intake decision | owned receipt/safety/decision/history | exact body-free changed event only if flow maps one | yes |
| accepted quarantine / gap / no-write / blocked marker | only exact owning object + history | only matching formal event | yes whenaccepted UoW commits |
| duplicate / in-flight / digest conflict / optimistic conflict | none in incoming UoW | none | duplicate reads original only |
| temporary dependency failure beforeaccepted write | none by default | none | none |
| formal reference Unresolved / Unavailable | snapshot + refresh record | reference changed event ifdefined | Consumer / Job result |
| known accepted-UoW failure | rollbackall staged | none | none |
| commit unknown | no compensation write | none | none untilprobe |
| publisher failure | publication marker + fenced plan/report only | no new source event | publication report/result |
| projection item failure | separate fenced plan/report classification | no projection-changed event | Draft report |
| projection item success | complete composite + item/report classification | derived event only ifformal mapping exists | Draft report |
| external delivery failure | local lifecycle failure + fenced plan/report | matching lifecycle event ifcommitted | Job report/result |
| external success + finalize failure | no Delivered marker untilcommit | none | no terminal result |
| consistency defect found by Query | none | none | none |
| no-write marker save failure | no marker guaranteed;attempted write blocked | none | none |

```text
normal outbound event allowed
  iff owning local mutation + mandatory history + source index + immutable outbox snapshot
      + exact stored result + idempotency completion all commit in the formal UoW

failure / diagnostic event allowed
  iff an existing formal flow owns that body-free marker / history / event

otherwise
  no durable event
```

不得新增generic `ErrorOccurred` durable event。该事件若未来需要,必须重新定义owner、protocol、schema、transaction、retention、consumer与测试,不能由adapter或telemetry shortcut补出。

### 11.10 Consistency defect catalog

| Defect ID | Detection invariant | Internal error / public behavior | Recovery |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ECD-OBS-001` | completed reservation指向existing result row | `CompletedReservationResultMissing`;ConsistencyFailure,no replay | manual;never reconstruct |
| `ECD-OBS-002` | stored result operation / kind / digest与reservation compatible | `StoredResultKindMismatch`;ConsistencyFailure | manual;never reconstruct |
| `ECD-OBS-003` | outbox row恰有one immutable payload snapshot | `OutboxPayloadMissing`;no publish | manual;never current-truth rebuild |
| `ECD-OBS-004` | payload digest / schema / event / subject / cursor与outbox匹配 | `OutboxPayloadCorrupt` / `OutboxInvariantViolation`;dead-letter / consistency | manual;never publish corrupt bytes |
| `ECD-OBS-005` | projection lookup命中matching view/marker | scope / freshness mismatch;Query consistency | manual/formal rebuild afterrepair |
| `ECD-OBS-006` | projection source record的item / ref / owner一致 | `ProjectionIndexCorrupt`;rebuild item failed | manual |
| `ECD-OBS-007` | exact membership与scope positions / revisions一致 | `ProjectionIndexCorrupt`;old view stale / unavailable | manual |
| `ECD-OBS-008` | diagnostic view/scope/summary/current pointer同一composite | `DiagnosticCompositeCorrupt`;body unavailable | manual,no-write |
| `ECD-OBS-009` | diagnostic Rebuilding ref命中exact progress row | `RebuildProgressLinkMissing`;Query consistency | manual;不猜progress |
| `ECD-OBS-010` | progress命中same-target Rebuilding maintenance | `RebuildMaintenanceLinkMissing`;Query consistency | manual;不猜maintenance |
| `ECD-OBS-011` | progress target命中覆盖requested scope的immutable binding | `RebuildTargetBindingMissing`;Query consistency | manual;不猜target / binding |
| `ECD-OBS-012` | one target的canonical scope binding immutable | `MaintenanceTargetBindingConflict`;conflict / blocked | formal new target only |
| `ECD-OBS-013` | first-bind aggregate等于all member positions | `PersistenceInvariantViolation`;start / finalize failed | manual data repair;不fabricate zero |
| `ECD-OBS-014` | report scope恰好分类一次 | `JobError::ScopeClassificationConflict`;Job consistency | manual / finalize audit |
| `ECD-OBS-015` | terminal report匹配exact stored result和reservation | result / report consistency;no replay | manual;不重跑Job body |
| `ECD-OBS-016` | evidence input ref命中exact immutable constituents | `EvidenceIndexInputMismatch`;handoff reject / consistency | input correction pre-save;manual ifpersisted |
| `ECD-OBS-017` | reference view匹配snapshot与marker | persistence invariant;Query consistency | manual/formal refresh afterrepair |
| `ECD-OBS-018` | one UoW one tagged cursor namespace | persistence invariant;commit rejected | manual adapter defect |
| `ECD-OBS-019` | rollback / commit outcome可分类 | `RollbackFailed` / `CommitOutcomeUnknown`;indeterminate | probe/manual |
| `ECD-OBS-020` | durable/public surface没有forbidden body | domain boundary / persistence invariant;fail closed | immediate manual containment |
| `ECD-OBS-021` | reservation scope / inbound identity只映射one compatible result/execution | idempotency/result consistency;no second writer | manual |
| `ECD-OBS-022` | plan/report/reservation identity、digest和item classification lossless compatible | persistence / report invariant;failed permanent/manual | never regenerate plan |
| `ECD-OBS-023` | current writer持有authoritative Active claim + token | stale -> `ExecutionFenceConflict`;persisted stale write -> consistency | stale rollback/reload;persisted defect manual |
| `ECD-OBS-024` | external marker/receipt/material匹配committed immutable intent token | persistence invariant / `ExternalFinalizeUnknown` | exact-token probe/manual;never rebuild material |

Defect ID只是设计反查标识,不是runtime evidence alias、acceptance ID、database PK或automatic telemetry name。Runtime只可通过既有body-free issue / gap / report fields暴露安全引用。

### 11.11 Redaction 与错误反模式
