错误上下文可包含public code、canonical protocol surface ref、`ReasonRef`、existing `GapStateRef`、typed subject/progress/report/outbox refs、recovery-derived bool和trusted trace correlation。不得包含raw request/event、raw log/metric/trace/evidence、external response、SQL/driver message、stack、path、credential、endpoint、provider payload、伪造evidence alias / run id / test result / acceptance / signoff / verdict。

以下实现无效:

- 返回generic `Failed`或解析message决定retry / mapping。
- 把所有Query missing映射成error,或在Query inline repair / refresh / rebuild。
- 对`CommitOutcomeUnknown`设置`retryable=true`或直接重跑mutation。
- 使用旧version重试optimistic conflict,或把stale execution fence当普通CAS。
- 从current truth重建missing stored result / outbox payload / external material。
- 默认另开transaction保存普通rejection“用于审计”。
- 在quarantine / dead-letter / error中保存raw body。
- Consumer commit前ack,或ack失败后回滚local truth。
- Publish / delivery failure后回滚source truth,或external success + local failure后立即redeliver。
- 将probe `Unknown` / `Unsupported`当作NotDelivered。
- last item成功就标whole target Fresh,忽略failed / stale members与target fence。
- 把resolver call failure转换成formal `Invalid` snapshot。
- Duplicate时编辑terminal Job report,或由`FailedRetryable`自动调度sealed execution。
- no-write marker失败后放行attempted write。
- 将Delivered解释为acceptance / signoff,或在本章发明HTTP/RPC数字与retry/backoff参数。

## 12. 并发、幂等与重入保护

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_13_concurrency_idempotency.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 13 §8~§17 的primitive、逐资源race、operation scope、canonical digest和16/9/9 key矩阵,§18~§24 的immutable Job plan、claim / fence、outbox与handoff / export重入,§25 的完整并发测试索引。正文保留实现application reservation、repository CAS、global work ownership和external effect去重所需的可计算identity、algorithm与failure surface;retention时长、lease / heartbeat、retry / backoff / exhaustion和adapter timeout留给§13与`04-配置设计`。

本章区分四类不可互换的identity:application reservation解决logical request replay;business / storage unique key解决canonical object identity;global work key + claim解决worker ownership;stable external token解决target-side effect去重。任何一类都不能代替另一类,也不能据此宣称cross-system exactly-once。

### 12.1 并发原则与控制 primitive

| Primitive | 适用资源 | 成功 / 冲突语义 | 禁止替代 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ObservationIdempotencyReserveOutcome` | Command / Consumer / Job logical operation | exactly one `Acquired`;otherwise exact `Replay` / `Conflict` / `InFlight` | plain unique exception + current truth lookup |
| `ObservationRepositoryVersion` CAS | existing mutable owner / view / report / marker | loaded version one winner;loser `OptimisticConflict` | cursor、source version、timestamp、claim token、hard-coded value |
| create-if-absent + formal unique | current owner / binding first create | one canonical current identity;loser unique / policy conflict | stored duplicate replay |
| append-only identity | history、result、snapshot、intent record | one immutable append;duplicate ref / content mismatch conflict | update old row |
| tagged committed cursor | source index与dual watermark | same namespace monotonic;older no-op / invariant | row version、source version、global order claim |
| scope read fence | projection source-set | capture与replace commit scope revision一致 | last read timestamp或execution claim |
| immutable target / work-set binding | staged Job | same execution exact canonical items / config | resume时重新list、改scope或current config |
| durable execution/item claim + fence | nonterminal Job与propagation item | one Active owner;acquire token严格递增;stale claimant zero-write | long transaction、process mutex、lease time本身 |
| stable external token + probe | publication / handoff / export | target识别same logical effect;existing receipt / formal probe | random attempt ID、Job run ref、current route |

| Resource family | 并发写入方 | Conflict identity / control | Loser behavior |
|---|---|---|---|
| receipt / safety / correlation / signal / rollup | Command与Bus / Runtime / Sandbox Consumer、rollup Job | operation reserve + current unique + loaded row version | replay / conflict / rollback;不建second current owner |
| audit / evidence | Command与source / Governance / Artifact Consumer | body-free semantic unique + CAS | duplicate relation不append;digest mismatch conflict |
| handoff / hint / export / delivery | Command、feedback Consumer、delivery Job | reservation + owner CAS + immutable input / token | terminal不改写;不重复prepare / deliver |
| retention / protection / replay / no-write / gap | Command与guard / maintenance Job | current unique + CAS + same-UoW guard recheck | stale release / close rollback;source write始终blocked |
| reference snapshot | Command、Consumer、refresh Job | subject-current unique + source-version guard + local CAS | older no-write;local loser reload |
| source membership / scope position | accepted source mutations | same accepted UoW exact replacement + tagged cursor | whole accepted mutation rollback |
| public projection / diagnostic composite | rebuild Job与accepted stale mutation | scope read fence + view CAS + monotonic watermarks | item rollback;old complete view保留且newer stale wins |
| target binding / maintenance / progress | parallel Job start / item / finalize | immutable canonical binding + aggregate revision + fence + CAS | same binding reuse;different binding conflict;不得Fresh |
| Draft / terminal report | item worker、accounting、finalizer、resume | report version + execution fence + mutually exclusive item outcome | reload remaining work;terminal never edited |
| outbox marker + snapshot | accepted writer与publisher workers | immutable append;global outbox claim + marker version | truth不回滚;one marker winner |

Query重复或并发读取不是idempotent replay。14个Query每次读取调用时的current committed authorized surface,允许两次结果不同,但始终不reserve、save result、append history、mark stale、refresh、rebuild、repair或advance cursor。

### 12.2 Typed operation namespace 与 atomic reservation

`ObservationOperationName`必须是finite `Command(ObservationCommandOperation)`、`Query(ObservationQueryOperation)`、`InboundConsumer(ObservationInboundConsumerOperation)`或`Job(ObservationJobOperation)`。Public route-neutral name只能通过compile-time / static table lossless映射到exact variant;unknown name、family/body mismatch或Command / Job同名前缀混用在UoW前返回`InvalidRequest`。

`ObservationQueryOperation` 的actual enum由`application::idempotency`唯一拥有；配置和API route复用同一类型：

```rust
pub enum ObservationQueryOperation {
    GetObservationReceipt,
    GetIntakeStatus,
    GetSafeSignal,
    GetSignalRollup,
    GetAuditTimeline,
    GetEvidenceIndexInput,
    GetReportHandoff,
    GetRetentionProtection,
    GetObservationReadModel,
    GetDiagnosticView,
    GetGapStatus,
    GetPeripheralExportView,
    GetReferenceSnapshotView,
    GetRebuildProgress,
}
```

Public `ObservationQueryName`必须total static映射到上方14个variant；禁止local config enum、string parsing或unknown alias。Query operation进入context/digest/entry enablement，但`idempotency_key=None`且repository拒绝reserve。

```rust
pub struct ObservationIdempotencyScope {
    pub operation_name: ObservationOperationName,
    pub actor_ref: ActorSafeRef,
    pub idempotency_key: IdempotencyKey,
}

pub struct ObservationInboundEventIdentity {
    pub consumer: ObservationInboundConsumerOperation,
    pub producer_family: ObservationProducerFamily,
    pub source_event_ref: SourceEventRef,
}

pub enum IdempotencyReservationState {
    Reserved,
    Completed,
}

pub enum ObservationIdempotencyReserveOutcome {
    Acquired(ObservationIdempotencyReservation),
    Replay { idempotency_ref: IdempotencyRef, result_ref: StoredObservationResultRef },
    Conflict { idempotency_ref: IdempotencyRef },
    InFlight { idempotency_ref: IdempotencyRef },
}
```

| Rule | Exact contract |
|---|---|
| logical unique | canonical `(operation_name,actor_ref,idempotency_key)`;enum family + variant均保留 |
| required family | Command、Inbound Consumer、Job必须有key;Query必须`None`且repository拒绝reserve |
| effective actor | API用authenticated actor;Consumer用stable system principal;Job用operator / system actor;不得用process / pod / peer text |
| same raw key | different operation或actor合法,不得串result或conflict |
| event secondary unique | Consumer additionally unique `(consumer,producer_family,source_event_ref)` -> original idempotency ref |
| digest authority | local canonicalizer从validated typed input生成;transport digest只能做constant-time equivalent validation |
| durable state | only `Reserved -> Completed`;`Replay` / `Conflict` / `InFlight`是incoming classification,不得持久化回row |

| Existing row | Incoming digest | Atomic outcome | Service action |
|---|---|---|---|
| absent | valid | `Acquired(new Reserved)` | only branch allowed to enter mutation / Job start |
| Reserved | same | `InFlight` | rollback incoming UoW;no body / candidate work;state-change recovery |
| Reserved | different | `Conflict` | rollback;old row unchanged |
| Completed + compatible result | same | `Replay` | rollback incoming UoW;read exact stored surface |
| Completed | different | `Conflict` | no old result exposure as incoming result |
| Completed + missing / incompatible result | same | typed consistency error | manual;never rerun or reconstruct |

Consumer secondary identity collision必须在同一atomic reserve中返回original Replay / InFlight / Conflict,不得创建alias reservation、result或接受dedup-key drift产生second execution。

Stored replay顺序固定为:确认reservation仍Completed且pointer一致;逐项核对result idempotency ref、operation、actor、digest;核对expected result kind与schema decoder;核对serialized surface digest;body-free decode并拒绝raw / credential / verdict / signoff字段。任一失败映射`CompletedReservationResultMissing`、`StoredResultKindMismatch`或`PersistenceInvariantViolation`,不得回查current truth。

### 12.3 Stable digest 规范

| Input kind | Canonical encoding |
|---|---|
| enum | stable variant discriminator,不能用display text |
| typed ref / newtype | owner type discriminator + validated opaque value |
| `Option<T>` | explicit `Absent` / `Present(value)`;missing不等于empty / default |
| ordered list | only when protocol says order is semantic,保留validated order |
| set-like list | validate then sort / deduplicate by typed canonical bytes |
| bool / number | fixed canonical representation;无locale / display drift |
| body-free snapshot | exact typed refs / states / digest markers;不读取或hash external body |
| version | only protocol semantic source version / explicitly supplied expected version;repository version不来自request |

| Global include | Global exclude |
|---|---|
| operation family + exact variant;effective actor | raw idempotency key本身 |
| route-bound refs、intent、state、reason、scope、safe marker | request / received / current time、metadata trace、request / message ID |
| Consumer producer、source event/ref、schema、optional source version、typed payload | `occurred_at`、bus offset、ack、delivery attempt |
| Job exact scope/cursor/filter/target/consumer fields与config snapshot | execution/run ref、scheduler attempt、retry counter、claim/fence token |
| body-level semantic trace only when DTO formally owns it | telemetry trace/span、adapter endpoint/product/secret/raw response |

Digest schema独立version且fake / durable / entry对same schema必须产生identical canonical bytes。算法或serializer演进不得使retention window内的reservation无法replay。`requested_at`不是幂等窗口,不进入任何Job key/digest;reservation retention由配置承接,但不能改变same-key / same-digest语义。

### 12.4 Command 幂等键与 digest

16个Command raw key均来自`ObservationCommandMetadata.idempotency_key`;logical key为`Command(exact variant) + effective actor + raw key`。下表字段是在全局operation / actor之外的stable body material。

| Command | Stable digest fields | Canonical / boundary detail |
|---|---|---|
| `SubmitObservationMaterial` | source ref/family、purpose、safe summary、redaction marker | Options explicit;never hash source body |
| `RecordSafetyDisposition` | receipt、disposition state、redaction marker、safe summary、quarantine reason | summary/reason Options independent |
| `BindCorrelationContext` | receipt、body semantic trace、causation、source、correlation seed | metadata trace excluded |
| `RecordSafeSignal` | context、signal kind、summary、runtime signal、rollup window | optional runtime/window explicit;no raw telemetry |
| `AppendAuditProjection` | subject、context、source audit、action summary、visibility | exact typed visibility;no audit body |
| `LinkBodyFreeEvidence` | projection、boundary、digest summary、purpose | owner discriminator preserved |
| `PrepareReportHandoff` | scope、complete evidence-index input、consumer、visibility | canonical linkage/audit/gap sets;encode input ref/scope/visibility |
| `EvaluateAuthenticityHint` | handoff、input ref、gap set、evidence origin | origin is hint basis,not verdict |
| `SetRetentionMarker` | protected ref、purpose、hold reason、release reason | conflicting intents rejected beforedigest |
| `ProtectActiveReference` | protected ref、consumer ref、reason | consumer boundary ref不变成truth owner |
| `DefineReplayScope` | canonical target set、allowed effect、constraint ref、purpose | non-empty;source-repair effect rejected |
| `RecordNoWriteViolation` | trigger、attempted target、reason | attempted body excluded |
| `RecordGapState` | source、gap kind、degraded reason、limited-consumption bool | false distinct from missing |
| `PrepareExternalAuditExport` | export scope、consumer、view、visibility | external target config excluded |
| `RegisterReferenceSnapshot` | subject、safe summary、freshness、optional source version | version must match producer/source namespace |
| `UpdateReferenceSnapshotState` | snapshot、state、safe summary、reason、optional source version | source version does not replace local row version |

Duplicate Replay返回original exact response、result ref、outcome、result、changed / outbox / gap refs和error surface;entry只把outcome映射为`DuplicateReplayed`,不得生成新refs。普通pre-UoW rejection无reservation / result;只有正式accepted negative marker / quarantine flow才可持久化replay surface。Business unique conflict不能从current row伪装duplicate success。

### 12.5 Inbound Consumer key、dedup 与 source order

九个Consumer raw key来自envelope `dedup_key`,effective actor是route-bound stable system principal。Global digest为exact consumer + actor + producer + source event/ref + schema + optional source version + typed payload。Dedup key、occurred time、metadata trace、offset、message ID、attempt与ack state全部排除。

Secondary identity中的producer必须与§7.4 static map、validated binding、envelope和optional source-version marker exact相等：Bus、SourceOwner、Identity、Governance、Artifact、Runtime、Sandbox、Archive、ReportConsumer依次对应九个Consumer。Mismatch在canonical digest / reserve前拒绝；不能用payload `SourceFamilyKind`替代producer，也不能以同一个source event在另一producer namespace建立alias reservation。

| Consumer | Payload stable fields | Local conflict / duplicate rule |
|---|---|---|
| `ConsumeBusObservationMaterial` | source family、purpose、safe summary、redaction marker | current receipt/safety;Replay or older no-write |
| `ConsumeSourceAuditMaterial` | source audit、subject、context、action summary、source family | semantic projection relation;older不得append |
| `ConsumeIdentityObservationContext` | subject、safe summary、freshness | current subject snapshot;uncomparable显式degraded |
| `ConsumeGovernanceAuditContext` | governance evidence ref、digest、visibility | same ref different digest conflict / quarantine |
| `ConsumeArtifactEvidenceContext` | artifact evidence ref、digest、purpose、visibility | linkage/snapshot CAS;older不覆盖visibility |
| `ConsumeRuntimeSignalSummary` | runtime signal、summary、kind、context | duplicate不建new signal;older不倒退snapshot |
| `ConsumeSandboxSignalSummary` | sandbox signal、receipt、summary、safety state | terminal safety不被old feedback覆盖 |
| `ConsumeArchiveHandoffFeedback` | archive handoff、handoff、delivery result、summary | old feedback不把Delivered退回Failed/Pending |
| `ConsumeReportConsumerFeedback` | consumer、delivery、delivery result、gap kind | old feedback不倒退delivery / reopen newer-closed gap |

```rust
pub struct ObservationSourceVersionRef {
    pub producer_family: ObservationProducerFamily,
    pub source_ref: ObservationSourceRef,
    pub version_token: OpaqueSourceVersionToken,
}
```

Source version只能来自trusted producer / resolver。默认只可比较equality;只有adapter声明并验证typed monotonic comparator时才能得到Older / Equal / Newer;different producer/source是Uncomparable。Equal + same digest为duplicate/no-op,equal + different digest为producer inconsistency;Older不写snapshot / projection / normal outbox;Newer仍必须通过local CAS与state matrix。Missing / Uncomparable必须保留explicit degraded / freshness evidence,绝不能按`occurred_at`、clock、schema、repository version、cursor或digest猜newer。

Unsupported schema在payload parse和reserve前返回,当前不创建durable reservation / result。Consumer Replay必须在rollback incoming UoW后读取exact `ObservationConsumerReceipt`,不得再次parse payload、call resolver或执行state/outbox effect。

### 12.6 Operations Job key 与 immutable plan

九个Job raw key来自`ObservationJobMetadata.idempotency_key`;logical key为`Job(exact variant) + effective actor + raw key`。Execution ref只关联one accepted local execution/report,不进digest、不充当key、也不是真实external run ID。

| Job | Stable request digest | Canonical work-set / global key |
|---|---|---|
| `PublishObservationOutbox` | cursor、limit、event filter | start冻结eligible exact outbox/snapshot;`Outbox(outbox_ref)` |
| `RebuildObservationReadModels` | target、canonical scopes、replay scope、diagnostic visibility、source cursor | one item per bound scope;`ProjectionScope(scope)` |
| `RebuildSignalRollups` | scope、signal cursor、window set | explicit / expanded windows frozen;`SignalRollup(window_ref)` |
| `RefreshReferenceSnapshots` | scope、freshness policy、snapshot set | expanded ref + observed version frozen;`ReferenceSnapshot(snapshot_ref)` |
| `ScanObservationGaps` | scan scope、expected source set、visibility scope | exact source candidates;`GapSource(source_ref)` |
| `CoordinateObservationReplay` | replay scope、target、no-write guard | Approved observation-side targets only;`ReplayTarget(target_ref)` |
| `PrepareReportHandoffDelivery` | handoff scope/ref、consumer | exact input + prepare/deliver intent;`ReportHandoff(handoff_ref)` |
| `PrepareExternalAuditExportDelivery` | export scope、consumer、preparation | exact view / preparation / intent;`ExternalExport(preparation_ref)` |
| `RebuildPeripheralViews` | consumer scope set、source cursor | exact view candidates;`PeripheralScope(scope_ref)` |

`limit`、request cursor和explicit source cursor影响candidate set,必须进digest;reserve后不得静默截断。Terminal duplicate在任何candidate read前replay report。Same digest nonterminal只按persisted plan做fenced resume;input/scope/config变化产生different digest并Conflict。需要从current state重新计算时必须new key + new execution,不得编辑old plan/report。

Plan exact objects:

| Object | Immutable / mutable contract |
|---|---|
| `ObservationJobWorkKey` | finite 9 variants,global across executions;canonical bytes sorted unique |
| `ObservationJobPlanItem` | immutable work key、planned input digest、optional observed row version;only state/outcome CAS mutable |
| `ObservationJobPlanItemOutcome` | canonical affected / failed / gap / progress refs、typed reason、outcome digest |
| `ObservationJobExecutionPlan` | plan/execution/idempotency refs、operation、request digest、complete config snapshot、plan digest、items |
| `ObservationExecutionClaim` | execution、optional global work key、strictly monotonic token、Active / Released / Expired |

`plan_digest`覆盖operation、request digest、complete canonical config snapshot和每个item的work key / planned digest / observed version,排除worker、clock、attempt与claim token。Plan work-set、config、planned input immutable;missing / corrupt snapshot或unknown digest profile是manual consistency failure,不能读current config补造。

Item outcome规则:Planned initial outcome None;Running不可finalize;Succeeded requires no failed refs/reason;FailedRetryable / FailedPermanent / Blocked require typed reason;SkippedTerminal must reference verified equivalent durable owner / marker。Finalize requires no Planned / Running and report sets/reason equal canonical lossless fold of every current item outcome。Terminal report + stored result + Completed reservation提交后seal whole plan。

### 12.7 Claim、fencing、item 与 finalize algorithm

| Claim rule | Exact contract |
|---|---|
| execution ownership | same execution one Active owner;resume requires prior durable Released / Expired,not local-clock inference |
| item ownership | unique by global typed work key,not only execution + key |
| token | every acquire strictly larger;renew never lowers;old token never becomes valid again |
| commit validation | every item / report / marker UoW calls `register_fence`;commit rechecks Active owner + current token |
| external effect | claim prevents local dual worker only;external call still requiresstable token / probe |
| fake parity | fake simulates acquire / expiry / reacquire monotonicity and commit-time stale rejection |

Item algorithm:

```text
load immutable plan + Draft report and verify operation/request/plan digests
skip terminal Succeeded / FailedPermanent / Blocked / SkippedTerminal
allow FailedRetryable only while report Draft and typed recovery/policy permits
acquire global item claim and fresh fencing token
reload local owner;recheck planned identity/version,source order,state,retention,visibility,no-write
perform external call outside DB UoW with stable token when applicable
begin short UoW;reload plan/owner/report/binding versions;register current fence
commit exactly one compatible local effect + item outcome + report classification by CAS
release claim best-effort after commit;release failure does not undo commit
on unknown commit probe item/owner/report/marker before reacquire
```

CAS / fence conflict先rollback。只有仍持有valid authority的separate failure-accounting UoW可写failed classification;stale worker不得记录自己的failure。若other execution已完成same global work,本item仅在验证equivalent durable terminal outcome后可`SkippedTerminal`,不得复制other execution result或声称本worker执行成功。

| Existing execution | Resume action | Forbidden |
|---|---|---|
| terminal report + Completed reservation | exact report replay | claim / list / item / finalize |
| Draft + Active claim | `InFlight` / already-running | second owner |
| Draft + Released/Expired claim + compatible plan | fresh execution fence;only unclassified / eligible retryable items | regenerate current candidate list |
| Draft + plan/report digest mismatch | consistency/manual | patch either side |
| no Planned/Running + valid outcomes + Draft | finalize-only under fresh fence | rerun terminal items |
| unclassified after crash | probe owner/report/marker;execute only with no durable outcome + fresh claim | assume rollback from lease expiry |

Finalize fixed order:acquire execution claim;begin;load plan/report/binding/maintenance/progress versions;register fence;validate all outcome digests and canonical report fold;recheck target/member freshness and target fence;transition Draft to one terminal;save report byversion/fence;save exact JobReport stored result;mark reservation Completed;commit。Known finalize failure只重做finalize;unknown按§12.9 probe,不得重做item。

### 12.8 Outbox 与 external effect 重入

Outbox `Failed`永不回`Pending`。Eligible selector显式接受`Pending`与policy-eligible typed retryable `Failed`;claim期间保持原state,success -> Published,failure CAS更新classification,exhaustion -> DeadLettered。Permanent Failed、Published和DeadLettered不eligible,未来恢复需要formal protocol。

`ObservationPublicationToken(effect_binding_ref,event_ref,outbox_ref,payload_digest,schema_version)`从accepted immutable snapshot复制并跨all attempt恒定。Publisher必须支持`Published(receipt)` / `NotPublished` / `Unknown` / `Unsupported` probe outcome或formal Unsupported。Known failure可按policy same-token retry;existing / success receipt只做local mark;success + local rollback先probe;Unknown / Unsupported停止;payload / binding missing或mismatch无external call且不得fallback current route。

```rust
pub enum PublicationProbeOutcome {
    Published(PublicationReceipt),
    NotPublished,
    Unknown,
    Unsupported,
}

pub enum ExternalPreparationProbe<T> {
    Prepared(T),
    NotPrepared,
    Unknown,
    Unsupported,
}

pub enum ExternalDeliveryProbe<T> {
    Delivered(T),
    NotDelivered,
    Unknown,
    Unsupported,
}
```

Publisher、handoff与export adapter的probe callable必须返回上述exact enum。`Unknown`和`Unsupported`不能映射成negative outcome,也不能由error message / timeout text推断`NotPublished`、`NotPrepared`或`NotDelivered`。

四类handoff / export token都包含generated immutable intent ref、exact historical effect binding、kind-specific local refs和body-free material digest:

| Token | Stable local material | Binding rule |
|---|---|---|
| `HandoffPreparationToken` | handoff、evidence input、consumer、material digest | start / intent UoW resolve exact binding |
| `HandoffDeliveryToken` | handoff、preparation、consumer、material digest | copy matching preparation binding |
| `ExportPreparationToken` | export preparation、view、consumer、material digest | start / intent UoW resolve exact binding |
| `ExportDeliveryToken` | preparation、delivery、consumer、material digest | copy matching preparation binding |

| Durable phase | Probe | Allowed action | Forbidden |
|---|---|---|---|
| no intent | n/a | commit intent + binding beforeadapter call | adapter-generated token / call-time current route |
| intent,no local preparation | Prepared | validate token/material/binding;local finalize only | reprepare |
| intent,no local preparation | NotPrepared + formal abort proof | same-token prepare once ifpolicy permits | new token/material |
| intent,no local preparation | Unknown / Unsupported | stop indeterminate/manual | blind prepare |
| local Prepared | n/a | derive delivery token bycopying binding | rebuild current evidence/view or switch target |
| delivery intent,no Delivered marker | Delivered | exact receipt finalize-only | redeliver |
| delivery intent,no Delivered marker | NotDelivered + formal abort proof | same-token deliver ifpolicy permits | changed package/preparation |
| delivery intent,no Delivered marker | Unknown / Unsupported | stop manual | assume success/failure |
| local Delivered | any | no external call;finalize/replay local result | overwrite receipt / redeliver |

External receipt只证明transport lifecycle,不证明verdict、acceptance、signoff、真实run ID、真实evidence alias或external business truth。

### 12.9 Duplicate、in-flight 与 commit unknown

| Existing / phase | Required behavior | Durable write |
|---|---|---|
| invalid pre-dispatch | reject beforeUoW / reserve | none |
| absent reservation | one atomic Acquired writer | formal first-run writes |
| Reserved same digest + current owner | InFlight;no body / candidate scan | none in incoming attempt |
| Reserved same digest + single-UoW unknown | probe;timeout不授权接管 | read-only |
| Reserved same digest + staged Job valid plan,prior claim released/expired | fresh fenced resume exact execution | claim CAS only beforework |
| Reserved / Completed different digest | Conflict | old row unchanged |
| Completed same digest compatible result | rollback incoming;exact replay | none |
| Completed same digest corrupt result | consistency/manual | no rerun |
| Query repeat | normal current read | none |
| terminal outbox / external marker | skip / finalize-only / replay | fenced report classification only where needed |

Single-UoW unknown probe order:使用exact operation + actor + key读取reservation;Completed时验证stored result并Replay;只有reservation absent且backend formal abort proof成立才允许same-key new attempt;Reserved保持InFlight / indeterminate;different digest Conflict;missing / corrupt result manual;store unavailable或无abort proof继续`CommitOutcomeUnknown`。Transaction ref、cursor gap、clock和absence of outbox都不是abort proof。

| Unknown cut | Probe order | Allowed recovery |
|---|---|---|
| Job start | reservation -> plan -> Draft report -> binding/progress | all compatible resume;all absent + formal abort new start;partial manual |
| item commit | plan item -> local owner/marker -> report -> fence | equivalent terminal reconcile/skip;none + formal abort retry;ambiguous manual |
| Job finalize | terminal report -> stored result -> Completed reservation | complete replay;Draft + formal abort finalize-only;partial manual |
| publication / prepare local finalize | local marker -> exact external token probe -> report | known receipt/preparation finalize;Not* + abort proof same-token call;Unknown manual |
| delivery local finalize | Delivered marker -> receipt -> exact token probe -> result/report | known receipt finalize-only;NotDelivered + abort proof call;Unknown no redelivery |

Probe只读且不得在Query path运行。任何repair / reconciliation写都必须由独立operations flow/UoW拥有并遵守retention / no-write,不能把probe result反写external truth。

### 12.10 统一幂等与重入表

| Interface / scenario | Idempotency / work identity | Logical window | Duplicate / reentry behavior |
|---|---|---|---|
| 16 Command | operation variant + effective actor + metadata key;stable body digest | command reservation + Completed result same lifecycle | Replay exact;Reserved InFlight;different digest Conflict;ordinary pre-UoW rejection no reservation |
| 9 Consumer | consumer variant + system actor + dedup key;secondary producer/source event unique | at least broker redelivery / ack ambiguity;secondary identity not earlier thanreservation | exact receipt replay;key drift resolves original;older source no-write |
| 9 Job | Job variant + actor + metadata key;immutable plan digest | covers start/items/finalize/terminal retention;nonterminal refs forbid expiry | terminal replay;nonterminal fenced same-plan resume;different digest Conflict |
| publication item | global `Outbox(outbox_ref)` claim;stable publication token | outbox commit throughterminal/reconciliation | one local fence winner;same token/payload/binding;unknown probe;Failed不回Pending |
| other Job item | finite global work key + planned input digest | accepted execution resume/finalize | terminal item no rerun;retryable only underDraft + fresh claim;all fences/CAS rechecked |
| handoff / export phase | immutable intent + binding + exact material digest | intent throughterminal marker/report andprobe closure | Prepared/Delivered finalize-only;Not* requires abort proof;Unknown/Unsupported manual |
| 14 Query | none | none | current committed authorized read;no stored replay / repair |

| Reentry source | Protection | Recovery |
|---|---|---|
| caller timeout / broker redelivery | actor-scoped atomic reserve + result-before-complete | Replay / InFlight / Conflict;unknown probe |
| producer changes dedup key | source-event secondary unique | resolve original outcome;no alias reservation |
| out-of-order event | trusted producer/source comparator + local CAS | older no-write;equal compatible;newer CAS;unknown degraded |
| scheduler repeats Job start | one reservation/execution/plan/report | terminal replay;compatible fenced resume;partial mismatch manual |
| worker crash / claim expiry | global claim + monotonic fence + immutable item | probe durable effect;fresh claim only forremaining / eligible item |
| parallel finalizer | execution fence + plan completeness + report CAS | one terminal triple;loser replay / finalize audit |
| source mutation races rebuild | source read fence + view/report CAS + item fence | rollback item;old view + newer stale retained |
| retention release races protection | marker/protection versions + same-UoW recheck | loser reload;never source delete |
| external timeout / local finalize failure | intent-before-call + stable token + probe + marker CAS | exact finalize / same-token call only withabort proof / manual |

### 12.11 并发、幂等与重入测试切口

本章只定义planned verification contract,不声称测试已执行。Step 16 / 正式§15必须保留以下25个跨接口最小ID及逐Command / Consumer / Job细粒度race索引。

| Test ID | 最小断言 |
|---|---|
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-001` | parallel same scope/digest only one Acquired;others InFlight / Replay;domain/outbox once |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-002` | same scope different digest Conflict;old reservation/result unchanged;no domain call |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-003` | same raw key acrossactor/operation does not collide;authorization still current |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-004` | enum/ref/Option/set canonical stable;times/trace/run/attempt/claim excluded |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-RESULT-001` | any reservation/result identity/kind/schema/digest mismatch fail closed,no recompute |
| `TC-OBS-EVENT-DEDUP-001` | exact redelivery replaysreceipt;no parse/resolver/state/outbox second effect |
| `TC-OBS-EVENT-DEDUP-002` | dedup-key drift hits original reservation;same digest replay/in-flight,different conflict |
| `TC-OBS-EVENT-DEDUP-003` | newer then older no regression;equal mismatch conflict;unknown not ordered bytime |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-001` | terminal duplicate zero candidate read;nonterminal Active returns in-progress |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-002` | resume cannot alter/reorder items,input,config or current binding |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-003` | one Active global work claim;reacquire token increases;stale commit fence conflict |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-004` | crash resumes remaining;outcome tamper/report fold mismatch rejected;finalize-only whencomplete |
| `TC-OBS-COMMIT-UNKNOWN-001` | unknown does not use new key/blind mutation;reservation/result probe drives action |
| `TC-OBS-CONC-CAS-001` | stale row version zero write;reload reruns all guards |
| `TC-OBS-CONC-UNIQUE-001` | parallel current create one owner;unique loser not disguised as replay |
| `TC-OBS-PROJ-FENCE-001` | source revision aftercapture aborts replace;old bundle andnewer stale watermark preserved |
| `TC-OBS-OUTBOX-DUAL-001` | dual worker one authoritative finalize;same binding/payload/token;truth unchanged |
| `TC-OBS-OUTBOX-FINALIZE-001` | external Published + local fail finalize-only;unknown probe;Unsupported no resend |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-RESUME-001` | expiry does not prove rollback;equivalent terminal probe -> skip,else fresh claim |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-FINALIZE-001` | parallel finalizer one report/result/complete;loser cannot edit terminal |
| `TC-OBS-CONC-RETENTION-001` | new protection afterrelease read defeats stale release;no source delete |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-HANDOFF-001` | intent first;prepare/deliver binding same;rotation no drift;Unknown/Unsupported stop |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-EXPORT-001` | binding/package/view/material fixed;local failure no rebuild/switch/redeliver |
| `TC-OBS-QUERY-NOWRITE-001` | repeated/parallel 14 Query invoke zero write ports |
| `TC-OBS-FAKE-PARITY-001` | fake/durable agree on unique、reserve、rollback、CAS、claim/fence、probe outcomes |

以下实现必须拒绝:raw key单独作为unique;business unique当exact replay;`Replayable` / `Conflict`作为durable reservation state;volatile time/trace/run/attempt进入digest;Query reservation/write;Job resume重新list或使用current config;process lock代替durable claim;`Failed -> Pending`;execution claim替代source fence / row version;external call无intent / stable token;probe Unknown / Unsupported当Not*;old binding fallback current route;duplicate改写terminal report;任何retry反写source truth。

### 12.12 五协议族并发 / 幂等 closure index

本表是 Step 13 current closure 的正式实现审计入口。它证明五协议族均有 key / digest / dedup / no-write、
claim / fence / token 或显式 `not_applicable` 记录,不证明 affected 已关闭或 runtime positive path 可激活。

| 协议族 | 数量 | Logical identity / replay | Claim / fence / token | Unknown outcome 与 truth boundary | 当前结论 |
|---|---:|---|---|---|---|
| Command C01-C16 | 16 | `(Command(exact variant), actor_ref, idempotency_key)` + exact typed-body digest；Acquired / Replay / Conflict / InFlight | single-UoW mutation不使用Job claim；owner用loaded version,Event snapshot用local immutable identity | commit unknown先probe原reservation/result/owner marker；不换key、不重做mutation | `pass_with_affected_open`;UoW/result/secondary owner affected |
| Query Q01-Q14 | 14 | `not_applicable`;selector/page cursor不构造reservation或stored digest | no claim / fence / token / write UoW | 每次读取current committed authorized surface；zero history/outbox/gap/refresh/rebuild/read-audit | `pass_with_affected_open`;carrier/visibility owner affected |
| Inbound Consumer I01-I09 | 9 | `(InboundConsumer(exact variant), system_actor_ref, dedup_key)` + `(consumer,producer,source_event_ref)` secondary unique | local CAS；worker completion只消费known committed receipt | commit unknown先probe；ack failure后redelivery重放原surface；无wildcard ack/retry/dead-letter | `pass_with_affected_open`;I05 payload/binding、completion/outbox affected |
| Outbound Event E01-E12 | 12 | accepted source UoW冻结event/outbox/schema/binding/payload digest；publisher不建第二reservation | J01以`Outbox(outbox_ref)` claim/fence,复用stable publication token | external unknown先same-token probe；`Failed`不回`Pending`;禁止current-truth payload rebuild | `pass_with_affected_open`;producer schema/binding与external phase affected |
| Operations Job J01-J09 | 9 | `(Job(exact variant),actor_ref,key)` + immutable plan/config/input digest；terminal replay原report/result | execution与global item使用durable claim + monotonic fence；effect使用frozen same-binding token | 只恢复original plan未分类/typed retryable item；external unknown probe-first；terminal不重做item | `pass_with_affected_open`;J06 H13、report ref、external accounting affected |
| **Total** | **60** | **`16 + 14 + 9 + 12 + 9`;每个可重复入口有exact replay或no-write结论** | **claim/fence/token只用于真实Job/effect边界** | **无blind retry、无current-truth重建、无source truth writeback** | **`60/60 recorded_with_affected_open`;`0/60`无条件完成** |
