## 13. 配置引用与外部依赖绑定

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_14_config_external_binding.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 14 §8~§9 的config ownership与typed schema,§10~§16 的配置引用、adapter / event / cross-repo binding,§17~§19 的runtime builder、validation matrix与五类token传播。正文固定代码owner、typed field、required / disabled / degraded语义和assembly boundary;exact raw key、文件格式、source precedence、数值默认 / range、secret provider、schedule与activation策略由full-restart `04-配置设计.md`定义。

配置只做wiring、bounded execution parameter和product-neutral policy binding,不是truth source。配置不得改变Observability ownership、protocol schema、state machine、transaction order、body-free / redaction-first、Query no-write、retention protection、idempotency scope、claim fence或external token / probe语义。

### 13.1 配置 ownership 与三层形态

| Module | 可读取 / 接收 | 注入方式 | 禁止持有 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `contracts` | none | n/a | profile、mode、endpoint、timeout、secret、store binding |
| `domain` | validated pure policy input / formal outcome | application method parameter | raw config、retry、health、product type |
| `application` | executable parameter、ports、safe external binding catalog、Job snapshot | constructor / operation input | env、file、secret、endpoint、topic、infra section struct |
| `infra::config` | code baseline、file / env source、secret locator | parse + validate | raw value进入application / issue / evidence |
| `infra::runtime_builder` | complete `ValidatedObservabilityConfig` + infra-private resolved material | immutable build input | 修改domain invariant、依赖entry crate或生成business outcome |
| `api` | `ValidatedApiEntryConfig` + assigned façades/context factory | composition root | repository、UoW、resolver、publisher、raw env |
| `worker` | locator-free `ValidatedWorkerEntryConfig` + inbound/publication façades + context factory + `InboundConsumerRegistrar` | composition root；成功后持有opaque registered set | transport/policy locator、private registry、direct adapter/store、truth-write bypass |
| `jobs` | locator-free `ValidatedJobsEntryConfig` + current Job DTO + maintenance/publication façades + context factory + `JobScheduleRegistrar` | composition root；成功后持有opaque scheduled set | schedule locator、target catalog、snapshot assembly、resume hot-read、补造request/run/evidence |

```text
RawObservabilityConfig                 infra-private deserialize shape
  -> parse / type / range / cross-field / profile / redline validation
ValidatedObservabilityConfig           immutable complete assembly input,body-free refs only
  -> resolve private transport / actor-policy / scheduler slots
  -> construct ports, five services, context factory, safe entry slices and registrars
JobExecutionConfigSnapshot             application-owned relevant subset,stored with plan
```

`RawObservabilityConfig`的serde layout和source precedence不在本文锁定。只有`infra::config`可解析endpoint / credential / transport / actor-policy / schedule locator;resolved material仅存在concrete adapter或registrar的process-private memory。`ValidatedObservabilityConfig`不能包含raw secret,application constructors不能接收root config,entry slice不能保留raw locator或private registry back-reference。Accepted Job在start UoW冻结snapshot并进入plan digest;resume / finalize只读stored snapshot。

### 13.2 Typed configuration contract

关键可执行类型:

| Type | Owner | Exact semantics |
|---|---|---|
| `ConfigBindingRef` | application runtime | one body-free validated config revision;不是path / run / evidence identity |
| `ExternalEffectBindingRef` | application runtime | immutable destination + provider idempotency namespace revision;不含endpoint/topic/credential |
| `PositiveDurationMillis`;`PositiveLimit`;`PositiveByteSize` | application runtime | strictly positive and hard-bounded;overflow / zero / unbounded rejected |
| `RetryBackoffConfig` | application runtime | initial、maximum、multiplier milli、jitter milli;maximum >= initial,multiplier >= 1000,jitter <= 1000 |
| `RetryPolicyConfig` | application runtime | max additional attempts + backoff;zero means no automatic retry |
| `ClaimLeaseConfig` | application runtime | lease + heartbeat;heartbeat < lease;durable adapter decides Expired |
| `ExternalEffectPhase` | application runtime | `Publication`;`HandoffPreparation`;`HandoffDelivery`;`ExportPreparation`;`ExportDelivery` |
| `ProbeCapability` | application runtime | `Supported` / `Unsupported`;Unsupported不得变negative outcome |
| `StableTokenCapability` | application runtime | `Enforced` / `NotGuaranteed`;不移除token参数 |
| `RuntimeProfileClass` | infra config | `LocalTest`;`IntegrationLike`;`RuntimeLike` |
| `StoreAdapterMode` | infra config | `InMemory` / `Durable`;InMemory仅local test |
| `ExternalAdapterMode` | infra config | `Fake`;`Controlled`;`Endpoint`;`Disabled` |
| binding locator refs | infra config | adapter / credential / store / policy / transport / schedule opaque locator;不得进入wire/domain |
| `SchemaVersion`;`SourceFamilyKind`;`ObservationProducerFamily` | contracts metadata | exact variants / wire token见§6.2.1；config只选择合法subset / static pair，不新增值 |
| `ObservationQueryOperation` | application idempotency | exact 14 variants见§12.2；API enablement复用同一enum |
| `ReportConsumerRef`;`PeripheralConsumerRef` | contracts refs | distinct body-free newtype；catalog subject owner不可互换 |

`ExternalAdapterBindingConfig`必须包含mode、optional adapter / credential refs、positive call timeout和canonical phase capability set。Validation rules:

- `Fake`仅`LocalTest`,必须有body-free fixture binding且credential absent。
- `Controlled`仅`LocalTest` / `IntegrationLike`,必须返回formal success / degraded / unknown / unsupported outcomes。
- `Endpoint`需要binding,protected target需要credential;provider detail不越过infra。
- `Disabled`无binding / credential,调用返回`AdapterDisabled` / formal unavailable,绝不是no-op success。
- Publisher只声明Publication;handoff / export各声明Preparation + Delivery;resolver不声明effect phase。
- `NotGuaranteed + Unsupported`允许first attempt的phase必须在ambiguous outcome时manual,自动additional attempts固定为0。

Digest compatibility固定为`DigestProfileVersion(1)`的Step 13 canonical rules + deterministic JSON object byte model + SHA-256 + 64 lowercase hex。`DigestCompatibilityConfig`包含one write profile与canonical readable set;P0 write只允许v1,readable至少含v1且不能移除仍被reservation/result/outbox/plan/intent/report引用的profile。未来升级必须dual-read -> switch-write -> durable material迁移审计后retire-old,不得覆盖旧digest。

### 13.3 Root config 与 typed sections

```rust
pub struct ValidatedObservabilityConfig {
    pub config_ref: ConfigBindingRef,
    pub profile: RuntimeProfileClass,
    pub technical: TechnicalRuntimeConfig,
    pub boundary: BoundaryConfig,
    pub safety: SafetyBindingConfig,
    pub stores: StoreBindingConfig,
    pub digest: DigestCompatibilityConfig,
    pub idempotency: IdempotencyRetentionConfig,
    pub projection: ProjectionRuntimeConfig,
    pub execution: ExecutionRuntimeConfig,
    pub external: ExternalBindingSet,
    pub entries: EntryBindingConfig,
}
```

`entries` section保留infra-only raw binding，但三个entry只能取得builder派生的safe slice：

```rust
pub struct InboundConsumerBindingConfig {
    pub operation: ObservationInboundConsumerOperation,
    pub producer_family: ObservationProducerFamily,
    pub transport_binding_ref: TransportBindingRef,
    pub accepted_schema_versions: Vec<SchemaVersion>,
    pub actor_mapping_policy_ref: PolicyBindingRef,
}

pub struct JobScheduleBindingConfig {
    pub operation: ObservationJobOperation,
    pub schedule_binding_ref: ScheduleBindingRef,
}

pub struct EntryBindingConfig {
    pub enabled_commands: Vec<ObservationCommandOperation>,
    pub enabled_queries: Vec<ObservationQueryOperation>,
    pub inbound_consumers: Vec<InboundConsumerBindingConfig>,
    pub enabled_jobs: Vec<ObservationJobOperation>,
    pub job_schedules: Vec<JobScheduleBindingConfig>,
    pub outbox_loop_cadence: PositiveDurationMillis,
    pub outbox_loop_candidate_limit: PositiveLimit,
}

pub struct ValidatedWorkerEntryConfig {
    pub inbound_consumers: Vec<ValidatedInboundConsumerRegistration>,
    pub outbox_loop_cadence: PositiveDurationMillis,
    pub outbox_candidate_limit: PositiveLimit,
}

pub struct ValidatedJobsEntryConfig {
    pub enabled_jobs: Vec<ObservationJobOperation>,
    pub job_schedules: Vec<ValidatedJobScheduleRegistration>,
    pub job_timeout: PositiveDurationMillis,
}
```

`InboundConsumerBindingConfig` / `JobScheduleBindingConfig`只能在`infra::config`的validated root和runtime builder内部流转。每个enabled Consumer必须形成raw binding -> resolved transport/actor-policy slot -> `ValidatedInboundConsumerRegistration` -> exact handler的一一对应；每个scheduled Job必须形成raw schedule -> resolved complete-request trigger -> `ValidatedJobScheduleRegistration` -> exact handler的一一对应。Safe type不得实现到raw locator的getter / conversion，不得携带endpoint、topic、cron、provider、credential、resolved material或private registry key。

| Section | Minimum exact field group | Primary consumer |
|---|---|---|
| `technical` | clock mode + optional binding;ID generator mode + optional binding | clock / ID constructors |
| `boundary` | max request bytes、default/max page、query timeout、accepted inbound schema versions | API / worker / jobs pre-dispatch |
| `safety` | redaction、source allowlist、safe label、correlation mapping、visibility、body-free scanner policy refs | validators + application pure policy inputs |
| `stores` | observation、projection、idempotency/result、job execution adapter bindings;transaction timeout;required schema revision | repository / UoW builder |
| `digest` | write profile + readable profiles | context、payload、result、intent digest builders |
| `idempotency` | Command / Consumer / Job reservation retention、reserved reconciliation age、external intent retention | reservation/result/intent adapters |
| `projection` | max capture / relation closure、rebuild / refresh / gap / rollup batch、freshness policy | source reader、planner、Job start |
| `execution` | claim lease、parallelism、max plan items、resolver/publication/handoff/export retry、Job timeout | maintenance / publication services and roots |
| `external` | four resolver bindings、publisher、12 event targets、handoff/export target catalogs | adapter registry + safe catalog |
| `entries` | enabled Command / Query / Job sets、9 infra-only raw Consumer bindings、optional raw schedules、outbox cadence / limit | builder解析private slot后只投影three locator-free entry slices + two registrars |

All vectors canonical sorted/unique;typed key lists replace free-form nested maps. Cross-field gates:

1. Fixed clock / deterministic ID only in LocalTest;RuntimeLike requires System / Runtime modes。
2. default page <= max page;explicit request只能收窄hard limit,超界reject而非truncate。
3. InMemory only LocalTest;Durable requires binding;runtime accepted owner + idempotency/result must supportsame atomic UoW。
4. Any enabled Job requiresimmutable plan、global work claim、monotonic fence和report durability。
5. Job reservation covers nonterminal plan/claim/report lifetime;external intent covers probe/manual window;cleanup不能删除unresolved material。
6. Projection source / closure / candidate overflow failswhole boundary,不得partial plan或Fresh。
7. External capability phases exactly matchfamily and concrete adapter descriptor。
8. Root accepted schema set non-empty/canonical且是binary-supported subset；P0只允许`[SchemaVersion::V1]`，每个enabled Consumer schema set必须是其non-empty subset。
9. `source_family_allowlist` non-empty/canonical且只含§6.2.1 variants；它不能放宽§7.4 per-Consumer compatibility。
10. Enabled Query使用§12.2 enum的sorted/unique subset；enabled Consumer operation unique且producer必须匹配§7.4 static map；report/peripheral target ref在各自typed subject family内unique。

### 13.4 配置引用表

| Config group | Type / key examples | Reader | Default / missing behavior | `04` handoff |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| runtime identity/profile | config ref、`RuntimeProfileClass` | config loader / builder | required;不可由binary name猜 | identity / profile / source precedence |
| clock / ID | typed modes + bindings | technical adapter builder | runtime-like required;test-only fixed/deterministic | technical adapters |
| request / page / schema | positive byte/limits、query timeout、schema set | API / worker mapper | bounded baseline required;unknown schema不parse | boundary section |
| observation / projection store | mode + binding | repositories / source-index builder | runtime-like durable;no silent in-memory fallback | store binding |
| idempotency/result store | mode + binding | reservation/result builder | accepted write atomicity required;missing startup failure | atomic group |
| job execution store | mode + binding | plan/claim/report builder | durable global claim/fence required when any Job enabled | execution store |
| transaction / schema | positive timeout + required revision | UoW / migration validator | commit timeout may mean unknown;schema mismatch startup fail | transaction/migration |
| digest | write v1 + readable profile set | serializer / loaders | v1 / `{v1}`;retained profile不可移除 | digest migration |
| safety policies | six typed policy bindings / allowlist | infra validator + application constructors | all required;body-free scanner无Disabled | safety/redaction/visibility |
| projection bounds | capture/closure/four batch limits + freshness policy | source reader / planner / Job start | bounded values required;Job start freezes | projection maintenance |
| technical retention | command/consumer/job/reconciliation/intent durations | idempotency / intent stores | required windows;不授权source cleanup | technical retention |
| claim / execution | lease、parallelism、max plan、four retry policies、Job timeout | Job execution + services / roots | parallel baseline 1;retries default 0;timeout不证明rollback | execution controls |
| resolver bindings | four `ExternalAdapterBindingConfig` | resolver builders | missing -> explicit unavailable,never fake success | resolver adapters |
| publisher / target catalog | publisher binding + event -> effect/transport mappings | publisher registry + safe catalog | every enabled event exactly one mapping | outbound targets |
| handoff / export targets | consumer -> effect binding + adapter | delivery registries + safe catalog | missing exact target -> blocked/unavailable | target catalog |
| entry dispatch | enabled sets、raw Consumer / schedule bindings、loop cadence / limit | infra builder + API / worker / jobs roots | missing private slot/safe item/handler fails corresponding root;entry只收locator-free slice + prebuilt registrar;no schedule的enabled Job仍operator-callable | entry/schedule/registration |

`retention.*`仅保留technical reservation / plan / intent material,不替代`RetentionMarker` / `ActiveReferenceProtection`,也不授权删除source truth。Retry只有Step 12 recovery class允许且frozen policy有budget时才能执行;timeout、connection close、worker cancellation和lease expiry都不是external abort proof。

### 13.5 External target catalog 与 Job snapshot

`ExternalBindingSet` owns outbound / handoff / export target mapping;`EntryBindingConfig`只own dispatch、inbound transport和schedule,不能包含outbound destination。

```rust
pub enum ExternalEffectBindingSubject {
    OutboundEvent(ObservationOutboundEventName),
    ReportConsumer(ReportConsumerRef),
    PeripheralConsumer(PeripheralConsumerRef),
}

pub struct ExternalEffectBindingEntry {
    pub subject: ExternalEffectBindingSubject,
    pub family: AdapterFamily,
    pub effect_binding_ref: ExternalEffectBindingRef,
    pub call_timeout: PositiveDurationMillis,
    pub capabilities: Vec<ExternalEffectCapabilityConfig>,
}

pub struct ExternalEffectBindingCatalog {
    pub entries: Vec<ExternalEffectBindingEntry>,
}
```

Catalog由typed subject唯一索引并注入application。Application entry只看body-free binding ref、family、timeout与capability;infra separately maps binding ref to concrete adapter + raw route/target + credential。`require(subject,family,phase)`只验证safe metadata,不probe health。Missing enabled mapping应startup totality fail;runtime exact target disabled返回`AdapterDisabled`;duplicate subject、family / phase mismatch是config invariant failure。

| Effect | Selection point | Durable landing | Rotation / recovery |
|---|---|---|---|
| Publication | accepted flow by exact outbound event | outbox payload snapshot + publication plan | new route affects future snapshot;old Pending/Failed/Unknown resolves old ref |
| Handoff preparation | Job start by report consumer | plan snapshot + preparation intent | same-token probe;old target unavailable manual |
| Handoff delivery | matching preparation local finalize | delivery intent copies preparation binding | existing preparation never redirected |
| Export preparation | Job start by peripheral consumer | plan snapshot + preparation intent | Unknown/Unsupported remains manual |
| Export delivery | matching export preparation | delivery intent copies preparation binding | no current-default fallback;Delivered local finalize only |

Credential rotation may preserve binding ref only whendestination与external idempotency namespace完全相同且old token仍可解析;otherwise new binding ref。

```rust
pub struct JobExecutionConfigSnapshot {
    pub config_ref: ConfigBindingRef,
    pub operation_name: ObservationJobOperation,
    pub bindings: Vec<JobConfigBinding>,
}
```

`JobConfigBinding` finite variants are candidate limit、max parallelism、claim lease、resolver/publication/handoff/export retry以及one-or-more exact external effect binding + family + timeout + capabilities。Bindings canonical sorted;non-external kind unique;external binding ref unique and catalog-compatible。Required snapshot by Job:

| Job family | Frozen relevant bindings |
|---|---|
| `PublishObservationOutbox` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease、publication retry、all distinct per-item publisher bindings / timeout / capability |
| `RebuildObservationReadModels` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease |
| `RebuildSignalRollups` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease |
| `RefreshReferenceSnapshots` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease、resolver retry |
| `ScanObservationGaps` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease |
| `CoordinateObservationReplay` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease |
| `PrepareReportHandoffDelivery` | claim lease、handoff binding / timeout / preparation+delivery capabilities、handoff retry |
| `PrepareExternalAuditExportDelivery` | claim lease、export binding / timeout / preparation+delivery capabilities、export retry |
| `RebuildPeripheralViews` | candidate limit、parallelism、claim lease |

Snapshot digest enters plan digest and complete snapshot isdurable with plan。Terminal duplicate不读current config;Draft resume必须load original snapshot;missing/corrupt/unknown profile -> manual consistency,不得current-config reconstruction。

### 13.6 Adapter binding 与 availability

`AdapterFamily` exact variants: `ObservationStore`;`ProjectionStore`;`IdempotencyStore`;`JobExecutionStore`;`ObservationSourceResolver`;`RuntimeSandboxResolver`;`GovernanceArtifactEvidenceResolver`;`SubjectObservationResolver`;`EventPublisher`;`ReportHandoffDelivery`;`PeripheralExportDelivery`;`Clock`;`IdGenerator`。Type owner在`contracts`;availability carrier owner在application port boundary,infra只构造snapshot。

```rust
pub struct AdapterAvailabilityScope {
    pub adapter_family: AdapterFamily,
    pub effect_binding_ref: Option<ExternalEffectBindingRef>,
}

pub enum AdapterAvailabilityKind {
    Available,
    Degraded,
    Unavailable,
    Misconfigured,
}
```

Publisher / handoff / export operation必须probe exact `Some(effect_binding_ref)`;family aggregate不能授权target call。Availability不持久推进lifecycle、不证明operation success、不驱动domain transition;Query只能投影existing surface,probe不得触发write。

| Dependency | Port / binding | Timeout / retry authority | Degradation / forbidden fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| truth / marker repositories + UoW | seven family repositories + UoW manager | transaction timeout;no adapter-local blind mutation retry | startup-required;no in-memory / external-truth fallback |
| projection/source index | source reader/planner/store | bounded capture + transaction timeout;fence conflict reload | stale/degraded/unavailable;no inline rebuild / false Fresh |
| idempotency/result | reservation + result ports | commit unknown rules | accepted atomicity required;no cache replay |
| outbox | outbox repository | publication policy does not change row identity | exact Pending/Failed snapshot retained;truth unchanged |
| Job execution/report | plan/claim/fence/report ports | lease/heartbeat + CAS;unknown probe | no process-lock substitute;unsupported Job not registered |
| four resolvers | Step 07 safe-resolution ports | same typed ref/context;sync Query single attempt;Job frozen resolver policy | formal Resolved/NotVisible/Stale/Unresolved/Unavailable;never body/default truth |
| event publisher | publish + publication probe | known abort or NotPublished + frozen policy,same token | disabled retains outbox;Unknown/Unsupported manual;no payload/route switch |
| handoff / export | four phase ports per family | phase timeout;abort proof / Not* + frozen policy,same token | blocked/failed/indeterminate;receipt not verdict/signoff |
| availability registry | exact family/binding probe | health timeout only updates availability snapshot | Available not accepted / delivered;no domain write |

Requirement classes:store/UoW/clock/ID/digest/safety are startup-required;projection + Job stores are enabled-Job-required;resolver is operation-required and may beUnavailable;publisher is propagation-required while durable outbox remains valid;handoff/export are explicit peripheral and may beDisabled without affecting unrelated core truth。

### 13.7 Event、entry 与 cross-repo binding

Each enabled Consumer raw binding fixes exact consumer operation、producer family、transport binding、accepted schema set和system actor mapping，并只由infra解析为private transport/actor-policy slot。Duplicate/ambiguous producer or actor mapping fails worker startup;schema config only selects Step 08-defined versions;Disabled consumer不consume、不ack、不写marker,也不fallback resolver / fake。

Config validator必须内置与§7.4相同的9行operation -> producer table，并在entry slice暴露前验证：operation无重复；producer exact；binding schema non-empty且是root/binary supported intersection；optional source family策略不扩大protocol compatibility；raw binding、resolved private slot与safe item count / operation / producer / schema exact total。任一unknown/empty/mismatch/duplicate返回`InvalidConfiguration`或`EntryBindingIncomplete`，禁止fallback v1、generic producer、first enum或current route。

All 12 enabled outbound event names must have exactly one target mapping。Transport ref/topic/route never entersoutbox snapshot、digest、public DTO或domain;accepted flow stores only`effect_binding_ref`,infra resolves historical route。API / worker / jobs roots只接收validated slice + allowed façade/context factory;worker/jobs额外只取得one-assembly registrar并在成功后持有opaque registered set。None receives target catalog、repository、adapter、UoW、locator或private registry。

Raw schedule ref只用于infra构造pre-resolved trigger。Scheduler capability必须在startup证明可转交完整existing `ObservationJobRequest<T>` invocation；不得从descriptor、current config或default补造`job_execution_ref`、actor、idempotency key、trace、requested time、scope、target、cursor、consumer或input，也不得生成真实run / evidence identity。无法转交complete request时在adapter capability gate失败，不能注册后留到first call失败。

| Entry root | Receives | Local composition | Forbidden |
|---|---|---|---|
| `api` | API slice + truth/read façades + context factory | static route totality and mapper construction | raw config、adapter/store lookup |
| `worker` | locator-free worker slice + inbound/publication façades + context factory + inbound registrar | exact 9-slot catalog -> `register_all` -> retain opaque set | transport/policy locator、generic callback map、partial active loop |
| `jobs` | locator-free jobs slice + maintenance/publication façades + context factory + schedule registrar | exact 9-slot catalog -> `register_all` -> retain opaque set | schedule locator、request synthesis、partial active schedule |

Worker/jobs catalog必须enabled=`Some` / disabled=`None`，且field/operation/body/producer/schema与safe item/private slot exact一致。Registrar group必须prepare-all -> totality -> arm-all；返回`Ok`前不得dispatch callback，任一失败必须revoke/join本次全部item并返回`EntryBindingIncomplete`，留下zero active root。

| Repository / system | Dependency kind | Local path / collaboration | Implementation handling |
|---|---|---|---|
| `quantalithos-core` | only compile-time cross-repo dependency | `/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-core`;workspace `core-contracts = { path = "../quantalithos-core/crates/contracts" }` | package/type incompatible blocks compile;no shadow copy |
| bus / identity / governance | runtime event / resolver collaboration | local repos found;no Cargo dependency | formal event/port/fake;unavailable explicit,never import truth owner |
| artifact / runtime / sandbox / archive | runtime collaboration | repos currently missing;no Cargo dependency | body-free formal port/event/fake may proceed;real integration pending |
| SDK / console | downstream API consumer | no Cargo dependency | absence does not affect truth;no implementation imported |
| report / acceptance / dashboard / GRC | product-neutral runtime consumer | handoff / export ports + explicit target binding | optional blocked/degraded;no run/evidence/signoff fabrication |
| target observability repo | implementation target,not dependency | `/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-observability` currently missing | design proceeds;blocks implementation kickoff until`07` confirms/creates |

Current stage uses local path dependency only;public crates.io is not prerequisite。Private git tag/rev needs future implementation-plan pin / rollback;none is fabricated here。Any new non-core sibling Cargo dependency is a design blocker requiring Step 03/04/05/14 and architecture review。

### 13.8 Runtime builder、startup error 与 validation

```rust
pub trait ObservabilityRuntimeBuilder {
    async fn build(
        &self,
        config: ValidatedObservabilityConfig,
    ) -> Result<BuiltObservabilityRuntime, RuntimeAssemblyError>;
}
```

```rust
pub struct BuiltObservabilityRuntime {
    pub truth_write_service: Arc<dyn ObservationTruthWriteService>,
    pub read_service: Arc<dyn ObservationReadService>,
    pub inbound_event_service: Arc<dyn ObservationInboundEventService>,
    pub maintenance_service: Arc<dyn ObservationMaintenanceService>,
    pub publication_service: Arc<dyn ObservationPublicationService>,
    pub operation_context_factory: Arc<dyn ObservationOperationContextFactory>,
    pub availability_probe: Arc<dyn AdapterAvailabilityProbe>,
    pub api_entry: ValidatedApiEntryConfig,
    pub worker_entry: ValidatedWorkerEntryConfig,
    pub jobs_entry: ValidatedJobsEntryConfig,
    pub inbound_consumer_registrar: Arc<dyn InboundConsumerRegistrar>,
    pub job_schedule_registrar: Arc<dyn JobScheduleRegistrar>,
}
```

`operation_context_factory`是§5.3/Step 07已有application-owned context factory，不是第六个business façade。Builder不依赖entry crates；infra registrar trait可以被worker/jobs消费，但infra不反向引用entry handler implementation。Entry不能通过`Any`、downcast、concrete getter或registrar lookup取得store、adapter、locator、material或private registry。

`RuntimeAssemblyError` exact variants: `ConfigSourceUnavailable`;`InvalidConfiguration`;`SensitiveReferenceUnavailable`;`StoreCompatibilityMismatch`;`AdapterConstructionFailed`;`RequiredCapabilityMissing`;`EntryBindingIncomplete`。Error只携带body-free `RuntimeAssemblyIssueRef`、optional config / family / binding / phase refs;不得携带raw config/path/secret/provider body,也不是application rejection、Job outcome、run/evidence/acceptance。Builder只能return complete runtime或one error,不能partial runtime + warning。

| Order | Assembly stage | Stop invariant |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | load raw sources | unavailable -> no adapter;source precedence deferred to`04` |
| 2 | parse / type / range | unknown key/type/range/overflow invalid;no truncation |
| 3 | cross-field / profile / redline | fake/runtime、atomicity、limit、retention、lease/retry、route/capability mismatch fail |
| 4 | establish body-free config identity | no secret/body/run/evidence inidentity |
| 5 | resolve sensitive refs ininfra | adapter-private memory only |
| 6 | validate store schema / atomicity / CAS / fence | mismatch failsrequired surface;no auto migration |
| 7 | construct base technical adapters | fake/durable invariants cannot be disabled |
| 8 | construct external + entry technical adapters and compare descriptors | private transport/actor-policy/scheduler slot exact；scheduler可转交complete request；no silent fallback/capability overclaim |
| 9 | build availability registry | body-free snapshot;health not operation success |
| 10 | assemble five façades + existing context factory with safe catalog / snapshot factory | application receives no concrete infra config/adapter；entry不自行补actor/idempotency/source identity |
| 11 | derive API slice, locator-free worker/jobs slices and two one-assembly registrars | raw/private/safe count + operation/producer/schema total；slice/registrar不扩权 |
| 12 | return complete built runtime | service/probe/slice/context-factory/registrar一次返回；assembled != active/healthy/delivered/accepted |
| 13 | entry-local finite catalog registration | assigned façade/context/slice/registrar only；prepare-all -> totality -> arm-all；failure revoke/join all and zero exposed root |

Stage 8 / 11 / 13的entry failure口径固定为：raw binding无exact implementation时在runtime exposure前返回`RequiredCapabilityMissing`或`EntryBindingIncomplete`；safe item/private slot/handler不一致或Nth prepare/arm失败时revoke/join整组并返回`EntryBindingIncomplete`；scheduler不能carry complete request时不创建registrar slot。Callback运行期失败仍使用existing Worker/Job receipt、ack/dead-letter、response/exit映射，不复用startup error或泄漏private cause。

Validation boundaries also require:every enabled outbound subject total-mapped beforefirst accepted write;secret resolution before adapter exposure;store compatibility beforebusiness row;raw entry binding/private slot/safe item/catalog totality beforeroot exposure;accepted event binding frozen insideUoW;Job config frozen beforeitem/external call;resume validatesstored snapshot;external preflight requires token/binding/material/phase equality and historical binding availability。Registration是operation开始前的technical composition，不创建business UoW、reservation、state、row、outbox、Job identity或evidence。

P0 config immutable afterassembly;change builds/activates a new complete runtime,not in-place adapter swap。Existing Job remains pinned toold snapshot;old ambiguous binding cannot retire until probe/finalize remains possible or operations classifies manual。Activation/rollback never rewritesdomain state、reservation、outbox、plan、intent、receipt或report。

### 13.9 禁止配置化与停审规则

以下开关 / fallback不得存在:`allow_raw_body`;disable redaction/body-free scanner;Query refresh-on-miss;Consumer/Job repair-source;force retention release;Suppressed=Resolved;Delivered=Accepted;configured evidence/run/signoff/verdict;configurable idempotency scope/digest field list;disable fencing/token/probe;Unknown=NotSent;Failed reset Pending;dynamic sibling Cargo dependency;missing dependency自动fake/in-memory/default route/local filesystem success。

`Disabled`是explicit config choice,`Unavailable`是runtime inability,`Misconfigured`是validation/descriptor mismatch,`Degraded`是subset / uncertain recovery capability;不得折叠为bool或业务state。若实现需要配置改变owner/schema/state/transaction、从current route恢复old token、或让entry读取raw env/secret,必须停止并回设计,不能留给`04`自行决定。

### 13.10 五协议族 binding / snapshot / intent closure index

| 协议族 | 数量 | Typed binding source | Freeze / persistence point | Old-binding / no-write rule | 当前结论 |
|---|---:|---|---|---|---|
| Command C01-C16 | 16 | static API route + validated boundary/safety policy；effect Command解析exact binding ref | accepted mutation同UoW冻结Event snapshot；handoff/export intent在对应phase start冻结 | config不改DTO/digest/truth owner；old snapshot/intent不读current route | `pass_with_affected_open`;UoW/result/secondary owner affected |
| Query Q01-Q14 | 14 | static query route + validated page/read/visibility policy | 不建plan、snapshot、intent、token或write UoW | disabled/stale/unavailable只映射read surface；不写marker/gap/audit/result | `pass_with_affected_open`;carrier/visibility affected |
| Inbound Consumer I01-I09 | 9 | exact producer family、schema set、system actor与transport-safe binding ref | startup组装locator-free handler；accepted local fact在single UoW落地 | unknown schema/family在payload前fail closed；disabled不consume/ack/write | `pass_with_affected_open`;I05与Consumer action/outbox affected |
| Outbound Event E01-E12 | 12 | typed event catalog解析exact external binding ref | typed encoder在accepted UoW冻结payload/schema/binding；J01冻结publication config | old Pending/Failed/Unknown解析old ref；payload损坏不从current truth重建 | `pass_with_affected_open`;producer binding与external phase affected |
| Operations Job J01-J09 | 9 | locator-free entry + validated execution parameters + exact target catalog | start UoW冻结config snapshot、canonical plan/item binding；resume只读原snapshot | schedule只触发；resume不热读config/relist/换target；old binding unresolved转manual | `pass_with_affected_open`;H13/report-ref/external accounting affected |
| **Total** | **60** | **每项有typed binding source或显式no-write boundary** | **accepted UoW / Job start / phase intent冻结点均有记录** | **无raw config穿透、current-route重定向或health替代binding truth** | **`60/60 recorded_with_affected_open`;`0/60`无条件完成** |

上述五族继续继承 §12.12 所列 12 项 affected。`04-配置设计.md` 只能收口 exact key、format、source
precedence、numeric range、secret provider、physical retention 与 activation/rollback,不得反向改变 owner、schema、
state、UoW order、snapshot、intent、token、probe、Query no-write 或 source truth boundary。

## 14. 可观测性与审计埋点契约

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_15_observability_audit.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 15 §7~§9 的信号分层、字段词表与channel矩阵,§10~§13 的日志、指标、span和durable audit exact表,§14~§17 的redaction、递归保护、handoff / retention / no-write及60协议覆盖。正文保留代码位置、固定名称、字段allowlist、emit timing、durable landing和failure behavior;threshold、bucket、sampling、backend、dashboard、retention days、pager与runbook不在本章定义。

本章的“审计事件”表示既有native history / audit / marker / intent / report的写入切口,不是新增通用`OperationAuditEvent` DTO、repository或第二套ledger。Runtime telemetry是best-effort的process side effect;mandatory durable audit是accepted UoW的一部分。两者不得互相替代、重建或授权业务状态转换。

### 14.1 信号分层与真相边界

| Layer | 内容 | Owner / durability | 核心边界 |
|---|---|---|---|
| A process runtime telemetry | structured log、runtime metric、runtime span | process / host-managed sink;out-of-band、best-effort | 不进入business UoW,无replay / acceptance / retry authority |
| B observability-owned durable facts | receipt、safety、correlation、safe signal、audit projection、evidence linkage、handoff、retention / no-write / gap / reference和native history | 本仓domain owner + repository | 只保存经正式protocol / safety / redaction接受的body-free fact,不保存runtime telemetry body |
| C propagation / execution material | outbox snapshot / publication state、immutable Job plan、claim / fence、external intent / token、item outcome、report | outbox / execution repositories | 缺失或损坏时fail closed,不得从log / metric / span重建 |
| D read / handoff surface | Query view、diagnostic、evidence-index input、report handoff、peripheral export | committed read或formally prepared local handoff | 不反写Layer B或upstream truth,不生成verdict / signoff |

必须同时满足:

1. accepted mutation的log成功或span OK不能替代mandatory history / audit;mandatory record写失败必须rollback whole accepted UoW。
2. `SafeSignal`只能来自正式intake + safety + redaction flow,不能把本进程raw log / metric / span直接落库。
3. outbox、intent、plan、token或report不能从telemetry、dashboard或backend query恢复。
4. telemetry backend ack、availability、retention或query result不是accepted / published / delivered / fresh判定来源。
5. accepted / published / delivered telemetry只能在对应local commit / finalize后emit;commit或external outcome unknown固定为`indeterminate`。
6. duplicate / replay只记录runtime replay结果,不得新增history、outbox、intent、item outcome或accepted change metric。
7. Query只允许Layer A,不得append read audit、repair、gap、stale marker、stored result或idempotency material。

### 14.2 公共字段安全与日志级别

所有message template、field key、metric / label和span / attribute必须由finite vocabulary映射。禁止用route、函数名、free text、driver message或provider body临时构造分类。

| 字段族 | 合法来源 | Channel用途 | 约束 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `operation_name` / `operation_family` | `ObservationOperationName`与finite family | log / metric / span分类 | 不从route或handler字符串猜测 |
| `phase` | entry / validate / reserve / load / transition / persist / commit / external / finalize | 全channel阶段分类 | 不使用SQL、函数名或动态字符串 |
| `result_kind` / state | Step 08 outcome、Step 10 state、formal adapter outcome | 有限结果分类 | accepted只在正式落点后使用 |
| `error_layer` / `error_kind` | Step 12 typed error mapping | log / metric / span错误分类 | 不解析raw message或display chain |
| `recovery_class` | `ObservationRecoveryClass` | 说明允许恢复姿态 | 不直接授权retry |
| `trace_ref` | trusted protocol metadata / envelope / Job request | runtime / durable correlation | opaque;不解释业务含义 |
| `correlation_context_ref` | committed `CorrelationContext` | safe relation | 仅在visibility与safe-ref guard通过时输出 |
| `issue_ref` / `reason_ref` | typed body-free issue / reason | 安全诊断关联 | 不展开body |
| owner / result / outbox / report refs | existing committed typed ref | 必要的单记录定位 | metrics一律禁止;log / span按具体切口allowlist |
| `config_ref` / `adapter_family` / `effect_phase` | Step 14 validated runtime contract | assembly / adapter / external分类 | endpoint、topic、credential、binding body不得输出 |
| `duration_ms` / bounded count | boundary timer / bounded collection | log value、metric value、span attribute | 不由domain object生成 |

| Material | Log | Metric label | Span attribute | Durable audit / report |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| finite operation / phase / result / error / recovery | allow | allow | allow | existing schema语义相关时allow |
| trace / causation / correlation ref | restricted | deny | trusted context或restricted ref | 仅existing object / history field |
| actor / subject / source / owner ref | restricted;actor默认deny | deny | restricted | existing native owner允许 |
| idempotency / dedup key、digest、source version、fence、external token | deny | deny | deny | 只留其既有reservation / snapshot / claim / intent owner,不复制到audit |
| config / effect binding ref | restricted | deny | restricted | existing plan / snapshot / intent owner only |
| endpoint / topic / route / path / credential / secret | deny | deny | deny | deny |
| raw request / event / log / metric / trace / evidence / config / provider body | deny | deny | deny | deny |
| real `run_id`、evidence alias、verdict、signoff | deny | deny | deny | 当前无owner flow,禁止伪造 |

| Level | 使用场景 | 禁止用法 |
|---|---|---|
| `debug` | 高频entry start、read、UoW begin、claim、resolver success、provider-return detail | 不作为accepted / delivered证明 |
| `info` | committed accepted、exact duplicate replay、consumer accepted、Job terminal、local Published / Delivered finalize、runtime assembled | commit / finalize前不得提前写success |
| `warn` | validation / domain reject、conflict / in-flight、not-visible / stale / degraded、temporary dependency failure、quarantine、CAS / fence loss | 不输出rejected body或winner data |
| `error` | consistency defect、stored material missing / corrupt、commit unknown、rollback failure、dead-letter、forbidden emission、runtime assembly failure | 不格式化raw error、stack、path、SQL或provider response |

### 14.3 日志埋点表

Entry、application、persistence和runtime边界按下表emit。表中optional ref仍需通过visibility + safe-field guard。

| 位置 | 日志级别 | 字段 | 目的 / timing |
|---|---|---|---|
| Command / Query entry进入静态route | `debug` | `operation_name`,`operation_family`,`phase=entry`,`trace_ref?` | body-free entry;Query标记read-only |
| route/body、metadata/ref/page校验失败 | `warn` | `operation_name?`,`operation_family`,`phase=validate`,`error_layer=contracts`,`error_kind`,`issue_ref?` | 证明UoW / repository未调用 |
| entry disabled / unavailable | `warn` | `operation_name`,`adapter_family`,`result_kind`,`issue_ref?` | 区分entry不可用与application rejection |
| response mapping完成 | `debug` | `operation_name`,`result_kind`,`error_layer?`,`duration_ms` | 记录final public surface,无response body |
| operation context建立 / reservation acquired | `debug` | `operation_name`,`phase=validate_or_reserve`,`result_kind?`,`trace_ref?` | 不记录key / digest |
| duplicate stored result replay | `info` | `operation_name`,`result_kind=duplicate_replayed`,`result_ref` | 证明未重跑domain / outbox |
| idempotency conflict / in-flight | `warn` | `operation_name`,`result_kind`,`error_kind?`,`recovery_class?` | 不泄漏old result / key / digest |
| owner missing或domain / policy rejected | `warn` | `operation_name`,`phase=load_or_transition`,`error_layer?`,`error_kind`,`reason_ref?` | staged writes全部rollback |
| version / unique conflict | `warn` | `operation_name`,`phase=persist`,`error_kind`,`recovery_class=RetryAfterReload` | 不输出version值或winner row |
| mandatory history / index / outbox / result失败 | `error` | `operation_name`,`phase=persist`,`error_kind`,`issue_ref?` | accepted UoW不得partial commit |
| commit known failure | `error` | `operation_name`,`phase=commit`,`result_kind=aborted`,`error_kind` | backend确认未提交 |
| commit / rollback unknown | `error` | `operation_name`,`phase`,`result_kind=indeterminate`,`recovery_class`,`issue_ref?` | probe / manual before retry,禁止success/failure certainty |
| Command accepted commit完成 | `info` | `operation_name`,`result_kind=accepted`,`result_ref`,`changed_count`,`outbox_count`,`duration_ms` | 只能在commit后emit |
| formal quarantine committed | `warn` | `operation_name`,`result_kind=quarantined`,`quarantine_ref?`,`gap_count`,`result_ref?` | 仅body-free marker |
| Query read / final surface | `debug` / `info` / `warn` | `operation_name`,`phase=load`,`result_kind`,`state_kind?`,`item_count?`,`gap_count?`,`duration_ms` | visible / empty / not-visible / stale / degraded分类,zero write |
| Query consistency defect / blocked write attempt | `error` | `operation_name`,`phase?`,`error_kind`,`issue_ref?`,`recovery_class=ManualIntervention` | no placeholder、no inline repair、no automatic violation flow |
| Consumer envelope / schema validation | `debug` / `warn` | `operation_name?`,`producer_family?`,`phase=entry_or_validate`,`result_kind?`,`error_kind?`,`trace_ref?` | payload parse前切口,无event body / ref |
| Consumer forbidden body | `error` | `operation_name`,`producer_family`,`result_kind=quarantined_or_rejected`,`issue_ref?` | 不记录body或hash surrogate |
| Consumer duplicate / old source no-write | `info` | `operation_name`,`producer_family?`,`result_kind`,`result_ref?`,`reason_ref?` | 不按occurred_at猜顺序,不重复write |
| Consumer accepted local commit | `info` | `operation_name`,`producer_family`,`result_kind=accepted`,`result_ref?`,`changed_count`,`outbox_count` | 只表达local fact |
| Consumer delayed / rejected / dead-lettered | `warn` / `error` | `operation_name`,`producer_family`,`result_kind`,`error_kind`,`gap_count?`,`issue_ref?` | finite receipt surface |
| ack失败 / dead-letter handoff失败 | `warn` / `error` | `operation_name`,`phase=ack_or_dead_letter`,`result_kind=failed`,`error_kind`,`result_ref?`,`issue_ref?` | local commit不变;redelivery走duplicate |
| repository / UoW call | `debug` / `warn` / `error` | `operation_name`,`repository_family`,`phase`,`result_kind?`,`error_kind?`,`recovery_class?` | logical family only,无SQL / table / handle |
| cursor assigned | `debug` | `operation_name`,`cursor_kind`,`phase=persist` | 只记录tag kind,不记录值 |
| composite / membership invariant失败 | `error` | `operation_name`,`phase=persist`,`error_kind`,`issue_ref?` | owner mutation rollback |
| stored result或outbox snapshot缺失 / 损坏 | `error` | `operation_name`,`result_ref?`,`outbox_ref?`,`error_kind`,`recovery_class` | 禁止current-truth reconstruction |
| Job entry / plan capture | `debug` | `operation_name`,`job_execution_ref`,`phase=start`,`trace_ref?` | `job_execution_ref`不是real run id |
| Job terminal duplicate / nonterminal in-flight | `info` / `warn` | `operation_name`,`result_kind`,`report_ref?`,`result_ref?`,`recovery_class?` | 不创建second plan / report |
| immutable plan / config snapshot saved | `info` | `operation_name`,`plan_ref`,`report_ref`,`config_ref`,`item_count` | 无plan digest / raw config |
| claim lifecycle / stale fence | `debug` / `warn` | `operation_name`,`item_kind`,`claim_state?`,`phase=item`,`error_kind?`,`recovery_class?` | 不记录fencing token;stale worker无write authority |
| Job item / failure accounting | `debug` / `info` / `error` | `operation_name`,`item_kind`,`result_kind?`,`error_kind?`,`report_ref`,`duration_ms?`,`recovery_class?` | structured outcome only |
| Job finalize conflict / terminal commit / unknown | `warn` / `info` / `error` | `operation_name`,`phase=finalize`,`result_kind`,`error_kind?`,`report_ref`,`result_ref?`,`changed_count?`,`failed_count?`,`duration_ms?`,`recovery_class?` | 不重做item;report不是验收结论 |
| resolver returned formal outcome | `debug` / `warn` | `operation_name`,`adapter_family`,`result_kind`,`duration_ms`,`issue_ref?` | 无external body |
| outbox preflight / provider return | `debug` | `outbox_ref`,`event_name`,`effect_phase=publication`,`result_kind?`,`duration_ms?` | provider success仍需local finalize |
| publication finalized / failed / unknown | `info` / `warn` / `error` | `outbox_ref`,`event_name`,`result_kind`,`error_kind?`,`report_ref?`,`recovery_class?`,`issue_ref?` | Published不等于consumed;unknown禁止blind retry |
| external intent commit / provider return | `debug` | `operation_name`,`effect_phase`,`adapter_family?`,`intent_ref?`,`config_ref?`,`result_kind?`,`receipt_ref?` | 无token / endpoint / material digest;success非Prepared / Delivered |
| handoff / export local finalize或unknown | `info` / `error` | `operation_name`,`effect_phase`,`result_kind`,`handoff_ref?`,`delivery_ref?`,`receipt_ref?`,`report_ref?`,`recovery_class?`,`issue_ref?` | transport fact不是verdict;unknown不换target |
| config load / validation / runtime assembly | `debug` / `info` / `error` | `phase`,`result_kind`,`config_ref?`,`adapter_family?`,`effect_phase?`,`error_kind?`,`issue_ref?`,`adapter_count?` | raw config / env / secret / endpoint / provider body deny |
| adapter availability observed | `debug` / `warn` | `adapter_family`,`availability_state`,`issue_ref?` | health不是operation success |
| unsafe field、sink failure或recursion | no same-sink log/span | process-local suppression / sink-failure counter only | 禁止fallback dump或调用own Command / Consumer |

### 14.4 指标埋点表与标签约束

所有runtime metric使用`observability_`前缀。Counter记录finite occurrence;histogram后缀`_duration_ms`;gauge必须来自完整scan / registry snapshot,不能用事件加减推算durable truth。Domain不依赖metric backend。

| 指标 | 类型 | 打点位置 | 标签 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `observability_command_total` | counter | Command final surface | `operation`,`result_kind`,`error_layer` |
| `observability_command_duration_ms` | histogram | Command entry -> final surface | `operation`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_command_committed_change_total` | counter | accepted commit后 | `operation`,`change_family` |
| `observability_query_total` | counter | Query final surface | `operation`,`surface_kind`,`freshness_state` |
| `observability_query_duration_ms` | histogram | Query entry -> response | `operation`,`surface_kind` |
| `observability_query_consistency_defect_total` | counter | typed defect mapping后 | `operation`,`defect_kind` |
| `observability_inbound_event_total` | counter | Consumer receipt形成后 | `operation`,`producer_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_inbound_event_duration_ms` | histogram | envelope -> receipt | `operation`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_inbound_schema_rejected_total` | counter | malformed / unsupported判定后 | `operation`,`producer_family`,`rejection_kind` |
| `observability_worker_delivery_total` | counter | ack / dead-letter runtime调用后 | `delivery_phase`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_idempotency_total` | counter | reserve / complete / replay / conflict / in-flight判定后 | `operation_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_stored_replay_defect_total` | counter | missing / wrong-kind stored surface | `operation_family`,`defect_kind` |
| `observability_uow_total` | counter | begin / commit / rollback返回后 | `operation_family`,`phase`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_uow_duration_ms` | histogram | one UoW boundary | `operation_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_repository_operation_total` | counter | repository call后 | `repository_family`,`operation_class`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_repository_duration_ms` | histogram | repository call boundary | `repository_family`,`operation_class`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_concurrency_conflict_total` | counter | CAS / unique / fence conflict mapping后 | `resource_family`,`conflict_kind` |
| `observability_resolver_call_total` | counter | resolver formal outcome后 | `adapter_family`,`source_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_resolver_duration_ms` | histogram | resolver port boundary | `adapter_family`,`source_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_outbox_publication_total` | counter | publication local finalize后 | `event_name`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_outbox_publish_duration_ms` | histogram | publish / probe phase | `event_name`,`effect_phase`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_outbox_items` | gauge | complete pending scan snapshot | `event_name`,`publication_state` |
| `observability_job_total` | counter | Job terminal / duplicate surface | `operation`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_job_duration_ms` | histogram | Job entry -> terminal response | `operation`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_job_item_total` | counter | item outcome committed后 | `operation`,`item_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_job_claim_total` | counter | acquire / release / expire / fence reject后 | `operation`,`claim_result` |
| `observability_projection_rebuild_total` | counter | read-model / rollup / peripheral item commit后 | `projection_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_projection_rebuild_duration_ms` | histogram | rebuild item boundary | `projection_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_projection_items` | gauge | complete projection scan / read snapshot | `projection_family`,`freshness_state` |
| `observability_reference_refresh_total` | counter | reference item outcome committed后 | `source_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_gap_transition_total` | counter | gap transition committed后 | `gap_kind`,`to_state` |
| `observability_retention_transition_total` | counter | marker / protection transition committed后 | `transition_kind`,`to_state` |
| `observability_no_write_violation_total` | counter | violation transition committed后 | `violation_kind`,`to_state` |
| `observability_handoff_total` | counter | prepare / deliver / feedback transition committed后 | `effect_phase`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_handoff_duration_ms` | histogram | handoff external phase | `effect_phase`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_peripheral_delivery_total` | counter | export / feedback state committed后 | `delivery_family`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_config_validation_total` | counter | config candidate validation后 | `validation_stage`,`result_kind` |
| `observability_runtime_assembly_total` | counter | builder complete / error后 | `assembly_stage`,`result_kind`,`error_kind` |
| `observability_adapter_availability_state` | gauge | registry snapshot | `adapter_family`,`adapter_mode`,`availability_state` |
| `observability_runtime_telemetry_suppressed_total` | counter | allowlist / recursion拒绝 | `signal_kind`,`reason_kind` |
| `observability_runtime_telemetry_sink_failure_total` | counter | sink失败且不递归emit | `signal_kind`,`sink_result` |

Label只允许Step 08 / 10 / 12 / 14已有finite operation、family、result、state、error、phase与mode。任何具体actor / owner / trace / job / plan / report / issue ref、key、digest、cursor、version、fence、token、config / binding ref、tenant、endpoint、topic、path、credential、free text、动态产品名均不得作为label。Unknown动态值只能映射固定`unknown`类别或suppressed。

| Outcome label | 唯一合法时机 | 不代表 |
|---|---|---|
| `accepted` | accepted UoW commit后 | downstream published或upstream truth accepted |
| `duplicate_replayed` | exact stored surface兼容校验后 | new mutation |
| `published` | provider result已由local publication finalize commit确认后 | downstream consumed |
| `prepared` / `delivered` | corresponding local handoff finalize commit后 | verdict / signoff |
| `fresh` | complete replacement + target finalize成立后 | source repaired |
| `assembled` | builder返回complete runtime后 | adapter healthy或operation成功 |
| `sink_failure` | telemetry sink返回失败后 | business operation失败 |

### 14.5 Trace / span 切口与传播规则

只有validated protocol metadata / envelope / Job metadata中的`trace_ref`可进入`ObservationOperationContext`与durable snapshot。Missing trace允许process-local span,但不得回填DTO、digest、`CorrelationContext`、outbox或stored result。`CausationRef`只来自已验证input / object,不能从span parent、route或time推导。

| Span cut | Start | Required attributes | End condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| `observability.command` | static route通过、application调用前 | `operation`,`operation_family=command` | final response;accepted仅commit后,unknown为indeterminate |
| `observability.query` | static route通过 | `operation`,`operation_family=query` | visible / empty / not-visible / stale / degraded / error |
| `observability.consumer` | envelope header通过、payload parse前 | `operation`,`producer_family` | typed receipt或pre-write error |
| `observability.job` | metadata / route通过 | `operation`,`operation_family=job` | terminal / duplicate response或start failure |
| `observability.uow` | `ObservationUnitOfWorkManager.begin`前 | `operation_family`,`phase` | commit / rollback / indeterminate |
| `observability.repository` | logical repository调用前 | `repository_family`,`operation_class` | typed result / application error |
| `observability.idempotency` | reserve / complete前 | `operation_family`,`phase=reserve_or_complete` | acquired / replay / conflict / in-flight / error |
| `observability.resolver` | safe-summary resolver前 | `adapter_family`,`source_family` | resolved / not-visible / stale / unresolved / unavailable |
| `observability.job.item` | plan item loaded + claim acquired | `operation`,`item_family`,`effect_phase?` | committed item outcome或accounting failure |
| `observability.outbox.publish` | stored snapshot / binding / token preflight后 | `event_name`,`effect_phase=publication` | local Published / Failed / DeadLettered finalize或indeterminate |
| `observability.external.effect` | committed intent + historical binding preflight后 | `adapter_family`,`effect_phase` | provider result + local finalize classification或indeterminate |
| `observability.projection.rebuild` | item source capture开始 | `projection_family`,`item_family` | replacement / failure accounting outcome |
| `observability.runtime.assembly` | raw source load开始 | `assembly_stage` | complete runtime或`RuntimeAssemblyError` |

Entry span是root或trusted parent child;repository / resolver / external / item span是operation child。Consumer、outbox和Job resume只能传播incoming / stored trusted trace;current worker span不能覆盖历史snapshot。Current design不保存`TraceSpanRecord`,backend retention不受`RetentionMarker`控制。

Span结束分类固定为`invalid`、`rejected`、`duplicate_replayed`、`accepted`、`indeterminate`、`finalize_failed`或typed semantic surface。Query not-visible / stale是成功语义surface而非internal error。Span status只能在Step 08 / 10 / 12 typed outcome产生后映射,不得驱动业务branch。

### 14.6 Durable 审计事件与写入顺序

| Durable carrier | Owner / landing | 承载内容 | 禁止内容 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `IntakeDecisionRecord` | `ObservationIntakeRepository` | receipt / safety decision、actor / reason / time | raw material body |
| `CorrelationLinkRecord` | `CorrelationSignalRepository` | correlation / signal relation transition | raw trace / log / metric body |
| `AuditAppendRecord` | `AuditEvidenceRepository` | projection / evidence append kind与subject relation | source audit / evidence body |
| `HandoffLifecycleRecord` | `ReportHandoffRepository` | handoff / authenticity / delivery lifecycle | verdict、signoff、real run id |
| `RetentionChangeRecord`;`NoWriteViolationRecord` | `RetentionGuardRepository` | marker / protection / blocked violation transition | cleanup authorization、source repaired claim |
| `GapTransitionRecord`;`GapScanRecord` | retention / maintenance repositories | gap lifecycle与scan result | fabricated missing material |
| `PeripheralDeliveryRecord` | peripheral owner repository | export preparation / delivery transition | external audit truth / product state |
| `ReferenceRefreshRecord` | reference repository | resolution / refresh transition | external body / lifecycle truth |
| `ProjectionMaintenanceRecord`;`ReplayExecutionRecord` | maintenance repositories | derived replacement或observation-side replay result | source repair / upstream replay truth |
| `ObservationOutboxRecord` + snapshot | outbox repository | committed propagation fact / publication state | current-truth rebuild / consumed proof |
| plan / intent / item outcome / Job report | execution repositories | exact execution authority、external phase、structured result | test result、acceptance evidence、signoff |

| 审计切口 | Trigger / flow | Existing landing与关键字段 | Consumer |
|---|---|---|---|
| intake / safety decision | `SubmitObservationMaterial`;`RecordSafetyDisposition` accepted UoW | receipt / safety refs、decision / from-to / reason / actor、cursor、`IntakeDecisionRecord`、outbox / result refs | intake、gap、diagnostic |
| correlation / signal change | `BindCorrelationContext`;`RecordSafeSignal` accepted UoW | context / receipt / source / signal refs、state / kind / reason、`CorrelationLinkRecord`、outbox / result | signal、rollup、audit、diagnostic |
| audit / evidence change | `AppendAuditProjection`;`LinkBodyFreeEvidence` accepted UoW | projection / linkage / subject / context / boundary refs、purpose / state、existing digest summary、`AuditAppendRecord` | timeline、evidence index、handoff |
| handoff / authenticity change | `PrepareReportHandoff`;`EvaluateAuthenticityHint` accepted UoW | handoff / input / consumer / hint / gap refs、readiness / state、`HandoffLifecycleRecord` | report / archive / review consumer |
| retention / protection change | `SetRetentionMarker`;`ProtectActiveReference` accepted UoW | marker / protection / protected refs、from-to、eligibility refs、`RetentionChangeRecord` | retention、replay、handoff guard |
| replay / no-write / gap change | `DefineReplayScope`;`RecordNoWriteViolation`;`RecordGapState` accepted UoW | scope / violation / forbidden target / gap / source refs、state、native replay / violation / gap record | operations、diagnostic、handoff |
| export preparation | `PrepareExternalAuditExport` Command accepted UoW | preparation / consumer / view refs、delivery state、`PeripheralDeliveryRecord` | delivery Job、operations |
| reference change | register / update snapshot accepted UoW | snapshot / source refs、resolution / from-to / reason、existing source version、`ReferenceRefreshRecord` | Query、handoff、refresh Job |
| Consumer accepted local fact | one of 9 Consumer accepted UoW | local receipt / projection / linkage / reference / signal / handoff / delivery refs + corresponding native record / gap / outbox | local Query / Job / operations |
| outbox publication finalize | publication item finalize UoW | outbox / event refs、publication state、existing stable provider identity、item outcome、report | operations / delivery diagnostics |
| projection / rollup / peripheral rebuild | matching item / finalize | target / view / rollup / progress refs、source cursor、maintenance record、item outcome、report | Query / operations |
| reference refresh / gap scan / replay finalize | matching item / finalize | snapshot / gap / scope / violation refs、native refresh / scan / replay record、report | Query / handoff / operations |
| handoff / export delivery finalize | matching local finalize | handoff / preparation / delivery / receipt refs、native lifecycle / delivery record、report | external consumer / operations |

Accepted write order固定为:

```text
validate protocol / context
  -> reserve idempotency
  -> load owner + guards
  -> domain transition
  -> stage owner/post-state + membership plan
  -> assign tagged committed cursor
  -> construct/append cursor-bound native history / audit + source membership
  -> construct/append exact outbox snapshot where required
  -> mark affected projection stale
  -> save stored result / consumer receipt
  -> complete idempotency
  -> commit
  -> emit accepted runtime log / metric and finish span
```

| Branch | Durable write | Runtime telemetry | 禁止 |
|---|---|---|---|
| pre-UoW invalid / unsupported schema | none | log / metric / span | audit / history / result / outbox |
| ordinary domain / policy rejection | none after rollback,除非既有formal negative owner | log / metric / span | hidden generic rejection transaction |
| accepted negative decision | exact receipt / safety / gap / no-write owner + history | log / metric / span | 与ordinary reject混同 |
| duplicate / conflict / in-flight | none in current attempt | finite result telemetry | second history / outbox / item / key |
| Query surface / consistency defect | none | log / metric / span | read audit / repair |
| dependency failure | none by default | log / metric / span | automatic gap;gap只走formal flow |
| commit unknown | no compensation event | indeterminate telemetry | accepted / rejected certainty |
| publisher / delivery failure | native marker / report only afterformal short UoW commit | log / metric / span | rollback original truth或generic Error event |
| startup / config failure | no business-store write | runtime telemetry | business audit / Job report / evidence |

### 14.7 Redaction 与 forbidden material

| Material family | Allow | Deny |
|---|---|---|
| API / protocol | finite operation / result、safe issue / result refs、duration / count | request / response body、header、credential、page cursor |
| Inbound Event | operation、producer、schema result、safe receipt / gap refs | payload、broker header、topic、source token、dedup key |
| SafeSignal / correlation | kind / state、required signal / context refs | raw log / metric / trace body、opaque trace text |
| Audit / evidence | projection / linkage / boundary refs、purpose / state、existing durable digest field | source audit / evidence / artifact / governance / identity body;digest in Layer A |
| Repository / UoW | logical family、operation class、typed result / recovery | SQL、table、row、driver message、connection string |
| Outbox / Job | event / job / item、safe plan / outbox / report refs、state / counts | payload、digest、route、raw input、fence / token、real run id |
| External effect | adapter family、phase、safe intent / receipt refs、formal outcome | endpoint、path、credential、token、material digest、provider body |
| Config / runtime | config ref、family、assembly variant、issue ref | raw config、env、secret、endpoint、registry dump |
| Diagnostic / fake | typed category与formal adapter outcome | free text、stack、panic、fixture body、private map、fabricated evidence |

实现必须先做typed allowlist mapping再serialization。禁止`Debug`整个request / object / error / config;raw infra error先映射typed category + safe issue ref;body-free ref还需visibility guard。Forbidden material不能通过hash、digest、base64或fallback message绕过。Mapping / serialization失败时suppressed whole field set / signal并增加non-recursive counter;fake / controlled / disabled不得比durable adapter披露更多private material。

### 14.8 Self-observation recursion guard

| ID | Invariant |
|---|---|
| `OBS-TELEM-001` | log / metric / span emitter不得调用`ObservationTruthWriteService`、`ObservationInboundEventService`、`ObservationMaintenanceService`或`ObservationPublicationService`。 |
| `OBS-TELEM-002` | sink失败不得创建Command、Consumer、outbox、gap、no-write violation、Job或业务retry。 |
| `OBS-TELEM-003` | 本仓runtime telemetry不得经同process / route / topic回流为`SubmitObservationMaterial`、`RecordSafeSignal`或任一`Consume*`输入。 |
| `OBS-TELEM-004` | sink ack / drop / retry / retention / query result不是accepted、published、delivered、fresh、healthy、evidence或signoff authority。 |
| `OBS-TELEM-005` | emitter不为自身emit建立nested log / span;suppression与sink failure只更新process-local non-recursive counter。 |
| `OBS-TELEM-006` | 当前禁止异步回采。未来需要时必须在`04/07`重新设计origin / hop guard和bounded route,实现不得私加。 |

```text
business / application / infra cut
  -> safe-field mapper
  -> recursion guard
  -> process telemetry facade
  -> external / host-managed sink

forbidden:
  telemetry facade -> own Command / Consumer / Job facade
  sink callback     -> own accepted mutation
  sink failure      -> business retry / idempotency decision
  own runtime span  -> SafeSignal durable write
```

Unsafe field或recursive attempt应suppress并增加safe counter;original operation不变。Sink unavailable / timeout / unknown不得通过own application probe,不得产生durable marker,不得改变已commit truth或原failure reason。Telemetry initialization unavailable只可作为host startup/process issue暴露,不能伪造成business rejection / evidence。

### 14.9 Correlation、evidence、retention、handoff与no-write

- `TraceCorrelationRef`、`CausationRef`、`CorrelationContextRef`与safe refs只用于关联,不得反推actor、business key、source truth或execution outcome。Outbox / Job resume / external finalize使用stored refs,不能以current worker context覆盖。
- `EvidenceLinkage`可保留既有`digest_summary`;Layer A不得复制digest。Log / span、dashboard screenshot、sink receipt或metric sample不会自动成为evidence。
- `RetentionMarker` / `ActiveReferenceProtection`只管理observation-owned durable material。Outbox / plan / intent / report保留需维持replay / finalize解释能力;telemetry backend retention与本仓marker无等价关系。
- `ReportHandoffRecord`、immutable `EvidenceIndexInputView`、`HandoffLifecycleRecord`与delivery intent / receipt解释交接。Prepared / Delivered只在local finalize commit后成立,不是verdict / signoff / real run id / evidence alias。
- External audit / GRC / report consumer反馈只经正式Consumer更新local handoff / delivery state,不能直接改audit projection或upstream truth。

| Path | Allow | Deny |
|---|---|---|
| Query / diagnostic | Layer A telemetry | durable read audit、repair、gap / stale write |
| projection / rollup rebuild | derived state、native maintenance history、report | source truth mutation |
| replay coordination | approved observation-side effect、native violation / gap flow | upstream replay / repair command |
| handoff / export | local intent / lifecycle / receipt / report | consumer truth / verdict writeback |
| telemetry emitter | out-of-band emission | any own write facade |

### 14.10 协议覆盖与停审规则

| Family | Count | Runtime cut | Durable landing |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Command | 16 | entry / reserve / reject / commit / accepted;`observability.command` | accepted native owner + history / outbox / result only |
| Query | 14 | visible / empty / not-visible / stale / defect;`observability.query` | none;strict no-write |
| Inbound Consumer | 9 | envelope / schema / duplicate / accepted / ack;`observability.consumer` | accepted local history / snapshot / outbox / receipt only |
| Outbound Event | 12 | append / preflight / publish / finalize;`observability.outbox.publish` | existing outbox snapshot / publication state,无second ledger |
| Operations Job | 9 | start / replay / claim / item / finalize;`observability.job` / `.job.item` | plan / intent / native history / outcome / report |

| Command group | Exact operations | Durable audit landing |
|---|---|---|
| intake / safety | `SubmitObservationMaterial`;`RecordSafetyDisposition` | `IntakeDecisionRecord` |
| correlation / signal | `BindCorrelationContext`;`RecordSafeSignal` | `CorrelationLinkRecord` |
| audit / evidence | `AppendAuditProjection`;`LinkBodyFreeEvidence` | `AuditAppendRecord` |
| handoff / authenticity | `PrepareReportHandoff`;`EvaluateAuthenticityHint` | `HandoffLifecycleRecord` |
| retention / protection | `SetRetentionMarker`;`ProtectActiveReference` | `RetentionChangeRecord` |
| replay / no-write / gap | `DefineReplayScope`;`RecordNoWriteViolation`;`RecordGapState` | `ReplayExecutionRecord`;`NoWriteViolationRecord`;`GapTransitionRecord` |
| external export | `PrepareExternalAuditExport` | `PeripheralDeliveryRecord` |
| reference | `RegisterReferenceSnapshot`;`UpdateReferenceSnapshotState` | `ReferenceRefreshRecord` |

| Inbound Consumer | Accepted local durable landing |
|---|---|
| `ConsumeBusObservationMaterial` | receipt / safety / intake decision / optional gap + outbox |
| `ConsumeSourceAuditMaterial` | `AuditProjection` + `AuditAppendRecord` + optional gap / outbox |
| `ConsumeIdentityObservationContext` | reference snapshot / refresh record / gap |
| `ConsumeGovernanceAuditContext` | boundary snapshot / audit or reference native record / gap |
| `ConsumeArtifactEvidenceContext` | evidence linkage input / audit or reference native record / gap |
| `ConsumeRuntimeSignalSummary` | `SafeSignal` / correlation native record / reference snapshot |
| `ConsumeSandboxSignalSummary` | safety / signal / intake / correlation native record |
| `ConsumeArchiveHandoffFeedback` | handoff lifecycle / receipt native record |
| `ConsumeReportConsumerFeedback` | peripheral delivery / receipt native record |

| Operations Job | Native audit / report landing |
|---|---|
| `PublishObservationOutbox` | outbox publication state + item outcome + Job report |
| `RebuildObservationReadModels` | projection maintenance record / progress / report |
| `RebuildSignalRollups` | rollup maintenance state / report |
| `RefreshReferenceSnapshots` | reference refresh record / report |
| `ScanObservationGaps` | gap scan / transition record / report |
| `CoordinateObservationReplay` | replay execution / violation / gap native record / report |
| `PrepareReportHandoffDelivery` | handoff lifecycle / intent / receipt / report |
| `PrepareExternalAuditExport` public Job;internal `PrepareExternalAuditExportDelivery` | peripheral delivery record / intent / receipt / report |
| `RebuildPeripheralViews` | projection maintenance record / progress / report |

12个event的`event_name`标签只允许`ObservationReceiptChanged`;`SafetyDispositionChanged`;`SafeSignalRecorded`;`AuditProjectionAppended`;`EvidenceLinkageChanged`;`ReportHandoffChanged`;`RetentionMarkerChanged`;`NoWriteViolationRecorded`;`GapStateChanged`;`ReferenceSnapshotChanged`;`DerivedProjectionChanged`;`PeripheralDeliveryChanged`。Topic、route、binding ref、payload digest和outbox ref不得成为label。

| Query group | Exact operations | Runtime classification | Durable effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| intake | `GetObservationReceipt`;`GetIntakeStatus` | result / surface / duration | none |
| signal | `GetSafeSignal`;`GetSignalRollup` | result / freshness / duration | none |
| audit / evidence | `GetAuditTimeline`;`GetEvidenceIndexInput` | visibility / empty / gap / duration | none |
| handoff / retention | `GetReportHandoff`;`GetRetentionProtection` | readiness / state / visibility / duration | none |
| read model / diagnostic | `GetObservationReadModel`;`GetDiagnosticView` | freshness / rebuilding / defect / duration | none |
| gap / peripheral | `GetGapStatus`;`GetPeripheralExportView` | gap / degraded / availability / duration | none |
| reference / progress | `GetReferenceSnapshotView`;`GetRebuildProgress` | resolution / progress / consistency / duration | none |

全部14个Query共享strict no-write。即使not-visible、stale、rebuilding、missing或consistency defect,也不得append `ReadAccessRecord`、创建gap、refresh reference、mark projection、save result或调用telemetry sink以外的write boundary。

本章不定义alert threshold、SLO、bucket、sampling、dashboard、backend产品、retention days、pager或runbook。若实现需要新增telemetry business port、durable span/log/metric owner、generic audit ledger、read-audit write、telemetry回采route,或需要把sink outcome用于业务decision,必须停止并回到Step 06~15重新校准,不得由`04`配置或实现自行补齐。

### 14.11 五协议族 telemetry / audit schema closure index

| 协议族 | 数量 | Runtime telemetry schema / timing | Durable landing | Safety / truth boundary | 当前结论 |
|---|---:|---|---|---|---|
| Command C01-C16 | 16 | finite operation/family/phase/result/error；accepted只在UoW commit后emit | owner history/audit/marker/outbox/stored result | serialization前redaction；无body/key/digest/secret/provider response | `pass_with_affected_open` |
| Query Q01-Q14 | 14 | read entry/load/result/defect + finite freshness/visibility labels | none | zero reservation/result/history/outbox/gap/refresh/rebuild/read-audit | `pass_with_affected_open` |
| Inbound Consumer I01-I09 | 9 | envelope/schema/producer/duplicate/accepted/ack；decode前只记safe header class | accepted local receipt/snapshot/marker/history与协议授权outbox | body只记detection kind/issue ref；ack不升级truth | `pass_with_affected_open` |
| Outbound Event E01-E12 | 12 | append/preflight/publish/probe/finalize；provider success不等于Published | immutable outbox snapshot/publication state + existing Job report | 无route/topic/token/digest label；不从current truth重建 | `pass_with_affected_open` |
| Operations Job J01-J09 | 9 | start/replay/claim/item/probe/finalize/terminal；execution ref仅technical identity | plan/config/claim/fence/outcome/intent/receipt/report/result | Prepared/Delivered不是verdict/signoff/run/evidence alias | `pass_with_affected_open` |
| **Total** | **60** | **所有入口有body-free phase/result/error位置与finite labels** | **只复用既有native owner；Query无durable effect** | **redaction-first、correlation-only、no truth writeback、no recursion** | **`60/60 recorded_with_affected_open`;`0/60`无条件完成** |

Telemetry coverage不关闭 §12.12 的 12 项 affected。尤其 I05 不得用schema error log冒充payload owner，J06
不得用span/log success冒充H13或execution result；retention metric不创建`RetentionMarker`，handoff/export sink receipt
不生成evidence、verdict或signoff。

## 15. 测试切口与最小验证清单

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_16_test_cuts.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 16 §7~§9 的测试层次、harness和shared protocol rules,§10~§15 的60协议与27 state owner + Job item逐项切口,§16~§18 的transaction / consistency、25个并发幂等ID、config / runtime / telemetry safety,以及§19的planned script contract。本文只固定最小入口、断言与建议类型;exact fixture、suite、priority、environment、coverage、CI、artifact schema、真实evidence和exit gate由current `05/06/07`定义。

本章所有测试、脚本、artifact和report均为`planned/not_run`。表中的`positive` / `abnormal`是未来测试切口名称,不是已创建case或通过结果;`TC-OBS-*`只是稳定设计ID,不是run、artifact、evidence alias或验收ID。

### 15.1 测试层次与最小 harness

| 测试类型 | Primary owner | 适用内容 | 不可替代 |
|---|---|---|---|
| contract unit | `contracts` | typed ref、DTO、enum、serialization、schema、body-free scan | application behavior |
| domain unit | `domain` | factory、policy、transition、history factory、不变量 | repository / UoW atomicity |
| application service | `application` | flow order、idempotency、error mapping、no-write、port orchestration | durable adapter conformance |
| repository contract | `infra` + shared suite | unique、CAS、append、cursor、rollback visibility、scan order | service authorization |
| adapter contract | `infra` | resolver / publisher / handoff / export formal outcome、token、probe | external product E2E |
| entry test | `api` / `worker` / `jobs` | parse、route、dispatch、surface、ack / exit semantics | application mutation logic |
| integration | cross-module | accepted UoW、outbox、projection、staged Job、split external finalize | production performance |
| static / schema scan | workspace / scripts | dependency、forbidden field、metric label、entry isolation | runtime behavior |
| property / golden vector | contracts / application | canonical digest、set order、outcome fold、stable token | actual delivery |

| Harness capability | Minimum behavior | Redline |
|---|---|---|
| deterministic clock / ID fake | 通过`ClockPort` / `IdGeneratorPort`给出predictable value | private sequence不是business truth |
| repository write spy | 记录formal method、order与parameter family | 不读private map替代public assertion |
| failpoint UoW | 在begin/stage/history/index/outbox/result/complete/commit/rollback注入typed failure / unknown | 不发明error variant |
| deterministic barrier | 控制parallel reserve、CAS、claim、finalize交错 | 不用sleep证明并发顺序 |
| resolver / publisher / delivery stub | 返回§5/§11 formal outcome、stable receipt、Unknown / Unsupported probe | 不返回provider body |
| telemetry recorder | 捕获structured field、metric label、span attribute、suppression count | 不成为accepted audit / retry authority |
| fake / durable conformance runner | 同一contract suite运行in-memory fake与durable adapter | fake不得有额外success path |
