| 测试切口 | 对应契约 | 最小断言 | 建议类型 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `contracts_protocol_roundtrip` | §5.1 / §7 | 16/14/9/12/9 name、metadata、request / response / receipt / report / error roundtrip保持required fields与variant | contract unit |
| `contracts_typed_ref_owner_isolation` | §5.1 / §6 | receipt / signal / audit / evidence / handoff / retention / gap / outbox / report ref不可混用或降为裸String | contract unit |
| `contracts_body_free_schema_scan` | §7 / §14 | public DTO、event、report、error无raw body、secret、endpoint、provider response、real run / evidence field | schema scan |
| `domain_factory_policy_invariants` | §5.2 / §9 | owner factory / policy接受合法safe ref,拒绝missing / wrong owner / body-bearing input | domain unit |
| `domain_state_and_history_pairing` | §9 | legal transition返回native history;illegal / terminal / reserved无state/history effect | domain unit |
| `domain_no_external_dependency` | §3 / §5.2 | domain不读config、repository、clock backend、telemetry sink或external adapter | static scan |
| `application_command_consumer_uow_order` | §8 / §10 | reserve -> load -> transition -> stage owner/membership -> assign cursor -> construct/append cursor-bound history/index/outbox/stale -> result -> complete -> commit | application service |
| `application_query_no_write` | §8 / §10 / §14 | 14 Query所有surface零write port调用 | service + write spies |
| `application_job_plan_item_finalize` | §8 / §12 | start/item/finalize分UoW,immutable plan、claim/fence、outcome与report fold成立 | application service |
| `application_typed_error_mapping` | §11 | error mapping不解析message并保持recovery class | application unit |
| `infra_repository_conformance` | §5.4 / §10 / §12 | fake / durable的unique、CAS、append、cursor、reservation、claim/fence、rollback一致 | shared contract |
| `infra_external_adapter_conformance` | §5.4 / §11 / §13 | resolver / publisher / delivery只返回formal outcome且遵守historical binding / token / probe | adapter contract |
| `infra_runtime_builder_totality` | §13 | fixed-order build为complete runtime或startup error,无partial runtime | builder test |
| `infra_forbidden_material_boundary` | §13 / §14 | secret、endpoint、route、provider body不进入service / log / report | integration + scan |
| `api_handler_facade_only` | §5.5 / §8 | API只route / metadata / DTO / error mapping + façade call,不直连repository/UoW/domain | handler test |
| `worker_consumer_publisher_boundary` | §5.6 / §8 | worker只做envelope / ack / loop mapping,不绕过consumer/publication service | worker test |
| `jobs_runner_facade_only` | §5.7 / §8 | runner只parse request、call service、map report / exit,不直连adapter/repository | runner test |
| `workspace_dependency_direction` | §3~§5 / §13 | crate direction正确,唯一cross-repo compile dependency为`core-contracts` | Cargo/static check |

### 15.3 Command 与 Query 接口切口

Shared Command tests必须覆盖same-UoW accepted、abnormal zero partial effect、exact duplicate replay和conflict / in-flight no-second-writer。Shared Query tests必须覆盖visible / empty、not-visible / missing / stale / rebuilding / disabled / degraded区分,且任何surface均不begin write UoW、不reserve、不save、不mark stale、不refresh、不emit durable audit。

| Command test cut | 对应契约 | Minimum positive / abnormal assertion | 建议类型 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SubmitObservationMaterial_positive` | §7 / §8 intake | safe source summary建立receipt / safety / decision,commit产生exact event / result且不存raw material | API + application |
| `SubmitObservationMaterial_abnormal` | §11 safety / dependency | forbidden body仅body-free quarantine/reject;resolver unavailable为Delayed;无主线partial effect | failure injection |
| `RecordSafetyDisposition_positive` | §8 / §9 | Pending -> Safe / Redacted并原子推进eligible receipt、history、outbox | service |
| `RecordSafetyDisposition_abnormal` | §9 terminal guard | missing owner / summary、forbidden marker或terminal rewrite返回typed error且rollback | domain + service |
| `BindCorrelationContext_positive` | §8 correlation | accepted/degraded-allowed receipt以safe source / trace / causation ref形成Bound或Partial | service |
| `BindCorrelationContext_abnormal` | §9 conflict | owner mismatch、opaque ref conflict或Invalid rebind拒绝且不推导identity | domain + service |
| `RecordSafeSignal_positive` | §8 signal | body-free summary + valid context形成Recorded,rollup / views stale并append event | service |
| `RecordSafeSignal_abnormal` | §9 / §14 | Invalid context、raw log/metric/trace或Suppressed rewrite拒绝,raw input不入store/telemetry | service + scan |
| `AppendAuditProjection_positive` | §8 audit | body-free source/context/subject append projection + `AuditAppendRecord`,handoff/timeline stale | service |
| `AppendAuditProjection_abnormal` | §11 boundary | missing context/source、wrong owner或source body拒绝,无generic audit ledger | service + scan |
| `LinkBodyFreeEvidence_positive` | §8 evidence | projection、boundary、purpose、digest summary经policy后Linked并保存audit/outbox | service |
| `LinkBodyFreeEvidence_abnormal` | §9 evidence | body input为BodyBlocked/reject;missing / NotVisible / stale不伪造Linked | domain + service |
| `PrepareReportHandoff_positive` | §8 / §10 snapshot | 完整`EvidenceIndexInputView`逐项重读并immutable save后建Draft/Prepared handoff | integration |
| `PrepareReportHandoff_abnormal` | §11 guard | ref-only mismatch、blocking gap、not-visible、retention/no-write block无deliverable handoff | failure injection |
| `EvaluateAuthenticityHint_positive` | §8 authenticity | body-free basis形成RealEvidenceLinked / PlaceholderDetected / Insufficient并关联handoff | domain + service |
| `EvaluateAuthenticityHint_abnormal` | §9 boundary | missing input、无origin声明real、terminal rewrite拒绝;无run/evidence alias | domain + service |

| `SetRetentionMarker_abnormal` | §9 guard | active protection release、Released reopen或source cleanup拒绝,marker/history保持 | domain + repository |

| `ProtectActiveReference_abnormal` | §9 guard | active consumer release或protected-ref mismatch为Conflict/error,无source delete | domain + concurrency |
| `DefineReplayScope_positive` | §8 replay | nonempty observation/projection scope + allowed effect经guards转Approved | service |
| `DefineReplayScope_abnormal` | §9 boundary | source/external target、empty或forbidden effect转Blocked/reject,zero replay call | service + spies |
| `RecordNoWriteViolation_positive` | §8 violation | forbidden target调用前阻断,Detected -> Blocked并原子保存record/outbox | service |

`planned/not_run`。

### 15.5 状态机测试切口

每行必须覆盖至少一个legal transition和一个illegal / terminal / reserved路径。非法路径除typed error外,还需断言owner、history、outbox、stale marker与stored result均无accepted effect。

| 测试切口 | State contract | Legal / illegal minimum | 建议类型 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state_observation_receipt` | `ObservationReceiptState` | Received -> four outcomes;Rejected/Superseded不重开,current supersede reserved | domain unit |
| `state_safety_disposition` | `SafetyDispositionState` | Pending -> Safe/Redacted/Rejected/Quarantined;terminal不互转 | domain unit |
| `state_correlation_context` | `CorrelationContextState` | Unbound/Partial -> Bound、Bound -> Partial;Invalid terminal/conflicting ref拒绝 | domain unit |
| `state_safe_signal` | `SafeSignalState` | Candidate/Stale -> Recorded、Recorded -> Stale、nonterminal -> Suppressed;Suppressed不恢复 | domain unit |
| `state_audit_projection` | `AuditProjectionState` | Pending/Restricted -> Appended、Appended -> Restricted、attach gap保态;Suppressed reserved | domain unit |
| `state_evidence_linkage` | `EvidenceLinkageState` | Candidate -> three outcomes、Linked <-> Stale/NotVisible;BodyBlocked不恢复 | domain unit |
| `state_report_handoff` | `ReportHandoffState` | Draft/Failed -> Prepared -> Delivered、Prepared -> Failed;Draft不直达Delivered,terminal不重开 | domain unit |
| `state_handoff_readiness` | `HandoffReadinessState` | policy产生four states;blocking gap不Ready,Query reevaluation不persist | policy + service |
| `state_authenticity_hint` | `AuthenticityHintState` | Unassessed/Insufficient -> two terminal hints;real需body-free origin,terminal不改 | domain unit |
| `state_retention_marker` | `RetentionMarkerState` | hold/release-candidate/conflict + archive hint保态;Released reserved且不等delete | domain unit |
| `state_active_reference_protection` | `ActiveReferenceProtectionState` | attach -> Protected -> Expired/Conflicted、empty -> Released;有consumer不release | domain unit |
| `state_replay_scope` | `ReplayScopeState` | Defined -> Approved -> Completed或Blocked/Cancelled;external target拒绝,terminal新scope | domain unit |
| `state_no_write_violation` | `NoWriteViolationState` | Detected -> Blocked/Escalated -> Closed;Closed不重开且forbidden adapter zero call | domain + spy |
| `state_gap_lifecycle` | `GapLifecycleState` | Open -> Acknowledged -> Resolved + mitigate保态;no-basis/reserved/reopen拒绝 | domain unit |
| `state_degraded_output` | `DegradedOutputKind` | None -> Active/Blocked;recovery需new policy replacement,Query不reset | policy + service |
| `state_signal_rollup` | `SignalRollupState` | Pending/Fresh/Stale/Failed -> Rebuilding -> Fresh/Failed;incomplete/raw source拒绝 | domain + Job |
| `state_read_visibility` | `ReadVisibilityKind` | four outcomes按request独立评估;not-visible != missing且无persistent transition | policy + Query |
| `state_diagnostic_freshness` | `DiagnosticFreshnessState` | assembler three outcomes,accepted change -> Stale,maintenance replacement恢复;Query不修 | domain + projection |
| `state_reference_snapshot` | `ReferenceSnapshotStateKind` | noninvalid可formal refresh至five states;Invalid terminal,Resolved必须safe summary | domain + resolver |
| `state_projection_maintenance` | `ProjectionMaintenanceStateKind` | Fresh/Failed -> Stale -> Rebuilding -> Fresh/Failed;Fresh不直达Rebuilding,Query不start | domain + Job |
| `state_replay_coordination` | `ReplayCoordinationKind` | Pending -> Coordinating -> terminal或Blocked;terminal不重开且scope coupling成立 | domain + Job |
| `state_rollup_rebuild` | `RollupRebuildKind` | Pending -> Running -> Completed/Failed;Cancelled reserved,terminal不回Running | domain + Job |
| `state_peripheral_delivery` | `PeripheralDeliveryKind` | Pending/Failed/Blocked -> Prepared -> Delivered/Failed;Delivered terminal,cancel reserved | domain + stub |
| `state_export_preparation` | `ExportPreparationState` | Draft/Failed/Blocked -> Prepared -> Delivered/Failed/Blocked;Delivered不重开,无conclusion | domain + stub |
| `state_outbox_publication` | `OutboxPublicationState` | Pending/Failed -> Published/Failed/DeadLettered;terminal不重开,无Failed -> Pending | repository + publisher |
| `state_idempotency_reservation` | `IdempotencyReservationState` | empty -> Reserved -> Completed;Replay/Conflict/InFlight不改old row,Completed不回Reserved | repository contract |
| `state_job_report` | `JobReportState` | Draft -> five terminal outcomes且fold完整;terminal不refinalize,duplicate replay only | Job unit |
| `state_job_plan_item` | `ObservationJobPlanItemState` | Planned -> Running -> structured terminal;retryable仅Draft+fresh fence,sealed plan immutable | Job repo + barrier |

### 15.6 事务、一致性、错误与恢复切口

| 测试切口 | 对应契约 | 最小断言 | 建议类型 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `uow_accepted_order` | §8 / §10 | stage owner/post-state + membership plan后assign one cursor,再construct/append cursor-bound history/index/outbox/stale,随后result、complete、commit | service + UoW spy |
| `uow_fail_before_cursor` | §10 | owner stage、membership plan或cursor allocation任一点失败rollback,无visible history / outbox / result / Completed reservation | failpoint UoW |
| `uow_fail_after_cursor_before_commit` | §10 | 后续任一点失败全部不可见;cursor gap可存在但不复用 | failpoint UoW |
| `uow_commit_known_failure` | §11 | confirmed abort不返回Accepted,新attempt重走全部guards | UoW fake |
| `uow_commit_unknown` | §11 / §12 | indeterminate + exact reservation/result probe,不blind retry或new key | ambiguity simulation |
| `uow_rollback_unknown` | §11 | fail closed / manual,不声称accepted/rejected certainty | UoW fake |
| `history_mandatory_atomicity` | §10 / §14 | native audit失败rollback owner;telemetry sink失败不rollback committed owner | service + telemetry fake |
| `repository_expected_version_unique` | §5.4 / §10 | stale version零写,canonical unique loser不覆盖winner,append-only无update/delete | repository contract |
| `cursor_namespace_separation` | §10 | observation/reference/page/outbox cursor不可比较,同UoW one tagged committed cursor | contract + repository |
| `projection_exact_membership_replace` | §10 | old+new relation的added/retained/removed全部advance;empty表示full withdrawal | projection integration |
| `projection_first_target_binding` | §10 | first target同UoW初始化,member/target positions read-your-writes,无zero fabrication | projection integration |
| `projection_capture_replace_fence` | §10 / §12 | capture后source/member/target变化使replace rollback,old bundle + newer stale保留 | barrier integration |
| `diagnostic_composite_integrity` | §10 / §11 | scope/view/summary/freshness/progress/binding任一missing/mismatch fail closed | query integration |
| `evidence_index_preview_to_snapshot` | §8 / §10 | Query preview零写;Command重读后先immutable input再handoff | service + spies |
| `outbox_record_snapshot_pair` | §10 / §13 | record/event/subject/cursor/snapshot/binding exact;missing/corrupt不重建 | outbox contract |
| `external_intent_plan_landing` | §10 / §12 / §13 | plan/config/intent/token/binding/material identity匹配,restart从durable source恢复 | Job integration |
| `error_protocol_before_uow` | §11 | route/body/ref/page/envelope错误不begin UoW,unsupported不parse payload | entry test |
| `error_domain_reject_rollback` | §11 | invalid state/policy/body返回exact code,无message parsing或accepted effect | domain + service |
| `error_dependency_family_mapping` | §11 / §13 | same unavailable按Command/Consumer/Query/Job映射不同formal surface,不伪造NotFound | adapter fake |
| `error_commit_external_indeterminate` | §11 / §12 | commit/external unknown保持indeterminate并按exact token/state probe | failure injection |
| `error_manual_consistency_defect` | §11 | missing result/payload/index/sidecar/report为ManualIntervention,不silent repair | corruption fixture |
| `error_finalize_only` | §11 / §12 | external success + known local failure只local finalize,零second external call | stub + failpoint |
| `error_no_write_persistence_failure` | §11 | violation marker失败仍block target,typed failure且不伪造audit/event | service + spies |

以下25个ID必须保持名称与语义稳定。它们仍是`planned/not_run`的设计切口。

| Test ID | Contract focus | Minimum assertion | 建议类型 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-001` | Command parallel reserve | N个same scope/digest仅one Acquired;其余InFlight或Replay;domain/outbox once | service + barrier |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-002` | digest conflict | same operation/actor/key不同digest为Conflict,old reservation/result不变且不进domain | digest + repository |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-003` | actor/operation scope | same raw key在不同actor/operation不串result,同actor仍重新authorization | repository + service |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-004` | canonical digest | set/Option/enum稳定;request time、trace、execution、attempt、claim/fence排除 | property + golden |
| `TC-OBS-IDEM-RESULT-001` | stored replay compatibility | identity/operation/actor/digest/kind/schema/surface任一mismatch fail closed,不重算 | result repository |
| `TC-OBS-EVENT-DEDUP-001` | exact redelivery | replay receipt且零parse/resolver/state/outbox second effect | consumer + spies |
| `TC-OBS-EVENT-DEDUP-002` | source-event secondary unique | dedup key drift仍定位original;same digest replay/in-flight,different conflict/quarantine | repository concurrency |
| `TC-OBS-EVENT-DEDUP-003` | source order | newer后older不回退;equal mismatch conflict;unknown不按occurred_at选winner | comparator fake |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-001` | Job start | terminal duplicate只replay report且zero candidate read;nonterminal返回in-progress | Job + spies |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-002` | immutable plan | resume不增删重排item、不改input、不以current config替snapshot | plan repository |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-003` | claim/fence | global work key one Active;reacquire token递增;stale commit为FenceConflict | fake/durable concurrency |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-IDEM-004` | item/finalize | crash只resume remaining;tampered outcome/report拒绝;report为canonical fold | phase injection |
| `TC-OBS-COMMIT-UNKNOWN-001` | unknown commit | no new key/blind mutation;reservation/result probe决定Replay/InFlight/manual | UoW ambiguity |
| `TC-OBS-CONC-CAS-001` | mutable row CAS | stale expected version zero write;reload后rerun all guards | repository fake + durable |
| `TC-OBS-CONC-UNIQUE-001` | create-if-absent | parallel canonical create one owner;unique loser不伪装stored duplicate | repository concurrency |
| `TC-OBS-PROJ-FENCE-001` | projection fence | post-capture source advance使replace rollback;old bundle + newer stale保留 | projection integration |
| `TC-OBS-OUTBOX-DUAL-001` | dual publisher | one authoritative finalize;binding/payload/token一致;rotation不重定向old event | outbox + barrier |
| `TC-OBS-OUTBOX-FINALIZE-001` | publication finalize | provider Published后known local failure只finalize;unknown probe;Unsupported no republish | publisher + failpoint |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-RESUME-001` | Job resume | Expired/Released不证明rollback;probe terminal后Skip/finalize,否则fresh claim | recovery integration |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-FINALIZE-001` | parallel finalizer | one terminal report/result/complete;loser不编辑terminal report | Job repository concurrency |
| `TC-OBS-CONC-RETENTION-001` | retention race | release read后new protection使CAS/recheck失败;zero source delete | domain + transaction |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-HANDOFF-001` | handoff phases | intents先commit且binding一致;rotation不改token;known state finalize;Unknown stop | handoff contract |
| `TC-OBS-JOB-EXPORT-001` | export phases | binding/package/view/material固定;local failure不重建、不换target、不redeliver | export contract |
| `TC-OBS-QUERY-NOWRITE-001` | 14 Query parallel | no reservation/result/history/outbox/stale/refresh/rebuild/cursor write | Query + spies |
| `TC-OBS-FAKE-PARITY-001` | fake/durable parity | unique、reserve、rollback、CAS、claim/fence、probe classification一致 | shared conformance |

### 15.9 脚本契约与停审规则

下列路径是future implementation contract,当前不表示文件、run、artifact或report已经存在。

| 脚本 | 类型 | 参数 | 输入 | Planned output | Failure semantics |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts/gates/run_ci_gate.sh` | gate | `--run-id` / `--artifact-root` / `--config-profile` | source、config、test suites、formal fake profile | `artifacts/test/<run_id>` | required suite/check任一nonzero则整体nonzero;保留failure summary,不伪造pass |
| `scripts/reports/generate_reports.sh` | report | `--run-id` / `--artifact-root` / `--report-root` | `artifacts/test/<run_id>` | `reports/runs/<run_id>` | input missing/incomplete/unparseable则nonzero并列出missing,不补default success |
| `scripts/checks/check_redaction.sh` | check | `--artifact-root` / `--report-root` | artifacts + reports | `reports/runs/<run_id>/redaction-check.md` | raw body、secret、credential、endpoint/topic/path、provider/package/receipt body或hash escape为nonzero |
| `scripts/checks/check_metric_labels.sh` | check | `--artifact-root` / `--report-root` | captured metric descriptors / samples | `reports/runs/<run_id>/metric-label-check.md` | undeclared metric、nonallowlist label或ref/key/digest/free text为nonzero |
| `scripts/checks/check_dependency_boundary.sh` | check | `--artifact-root` / `--report-root` | Cargo metadata / lockfile snapshot | `reports/runs/<run_id>/dependency-boundary-check.md` | non-core sibling path、direction reversal或undeclared member为nonzero |

`--artifact-root`必须与`--run-id`一致并落在`artifacts/test/<run_id>`;report root固定`reports/`,run output固定`reports/runs/<run_id>`。Exit code只表达command execution,不等于acceptance signoff。Checker不得编辑source、truth store、artifact或input report来修复失败,不得从template、design doc或旧evidence生成passed result。

本章不定义fixture全集、test framework、suite owner / priority、coverage、CI topology、environment、performance load、artifact schema、report正文、evidence alias、entry / exit或真实执行结果。实现若无法提供deterministic barrier、failpoint UoW、write spy、formal external stub、telemetry recorder或fake/durable conformance,必须停止并回设计或`05`补测试方案,不得以sleep、private fake map、log text或metric value替代formal assertion。

## 16. 详细设计到实施计划的承接清单

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_17_implementation_handoff.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 17 §8~§10 的可实施inventory、主承接清单和前置阅读矩阵,§11~§13 的implementation precondition、字段 / DTO / Query / state / naming / pre-phase closure与未进入实施项,以及§14的`07`逐boundary整体审计输入。本文只交付实施计划可引用的设计事实和审计规则,不定义phase、task、commit boundary或排期。

本章完成只表示正式详细设计提供了足够的implementation-planning input,不表示项目implementation ready。Current `04/05/06/07`、目标仓、逐boundary审计、implementation ledger / skeleton及真实test / artifact / evidence仍未完成。`07`必须引用本文,不得复制schema、flow、state或logical store形成第二真相源；§16.10的12项affected必须逐boundary消费,不得把“已有设计记录”解释为positive path已经激活。

### 16.1 可实施契约 inventory

| 承接面 | 正式来源 | 已交付给`07`的契约 | `07`不得重写 / 省略 |
|---|---|---|---|

| workspace / file | §3~§5 | seven role crates、file tree、Cargo direction、owner / dependency | 不按十capability拆十crate;不加`common/utils`桶 |
| object / field | §5~§6 | public helper、domain/application object、required field group、factory / invariant、owner | 不由实现者加default / placeholder / body carrier |

| function flow | §8 | five shared templates + 60 exact surface、side-effect order、external split cut | 不因实现便利重排UoW / outbox / external call |
| state | §9 | 27 formal owner + Job item、variant、trigger、illegal / terminal / reserved rule | 不用`done/success/processed`等口语state |
| persistence | §10 | logical ownership、key / index / version / cursor、transaction、consistency / recovery | 不把logical store当product DDL;不以telemetry补一致性 |
| error / recovery | §11 | typed layers、public mapping、rollback / retry / quarantine / dead-letter / manual / degraded | 不解析message text或用generic retry覆盖formal class |
| concurrency / idempotency | §12 | actor-scope key、canonical digest、source order、plan / claim / fence、token / probe | 不换key/token/binding,不重列plan或blind retry |
| config / runtime | §13 | typed validated config、raw-to-safe entry projection、builder/context factory、prebuilt registrars、safe catalog、historical binding、startup error | config不改owner / DTO / state / no-write；entry不读locator/material/private registry |
| telemetry / audit | §14 | Layer A~D、safe field、41 metric、13 span、native audit、redaction / recursion | telemetry不成为truth / result / retry / acceptance authority |
| tests / scripts | §15 | module / 60 protocol / 27+1 state / consistency / safety cut、25 ID、7 entry-registration cuts、5 planned scripts | 不声称已实现、运行或通过 |

| `contracts` | `crates/contracts`;`observability-contracts` | typed refs、metadata、DTO / view / event / Job / public error | `core-contracts` | depend on domain/application/infra;raw body |
| `domain` | `crates/domain`;`observability-domain` | observation-owned state、policy、native record formation | contracts + core | config / repository / telemetry / external adapter access |
| `application` | `crates/application`;`observability-application` | façades、ports、UoW/idempotency orchestration、flow | domain + contracts + core | depend on infra / product adapter |
| `infra` | `crates/infra`;`observability-infra` | store/resolver/publisher/delivery/config/runtime implementations + entry technical registrar shape/implementation | application + domain + contracts + core | new business policy/port mutation；depend on entry crates |
| `api` | `crates/api`;`observability-api` | sync Command / Query entry mapping | application + infra + contracts + core | direct repository / UoW |
| `worker` | `crates/worker`;`observability-worker` | Consumer、publication loop entry、9-slot handler catalog、opaque registered-set ownership | application + infra + contracts + core | direct resolver/publisher/store；transport/policy locator lookup |
| `jobs` | `crates/jobs`;`observability-jobs` | one-shot Job entry/report/exit mapping、9-slot handler catalog、opaque schedule-set ownership | application + infra + contracts + core | schedule locator/request synthesis、signoff、real run/evidence alias generation |

#### 16.1.1 Entry registration exact file closure

| Planned file | Must define / implement | Must consume | Must not contain |
|---|---|---|---|
| `crates/infra/src/config.rs` | raw `InboundConsumerBindingConfig` / `JobScheduleBindingConfig` validation and locator-free safe item derivation | current `04` exact source/key/schema | resolved secret/material、entry handler、business invocation |
| `crates/infra/src/runtime_builder.rs` | object-safe registrar/handler/catalog/opaque-handle shape;private registrar implementation;context-factory + two registrar runtime fields;stage 8/11 totality | raw bindings、resolved private transport/actor-policy/scheduler slots、safe items | dependency on worker/jobs、generic service locator、public DTO replacement |
| `crates/infra/src/lib.rs` | re-export only entry-required technical types | runtime builder public composition surface | private registry/concrete adapter export、`Any` / downcast hook |
| `crates/worker/src/consumers.rs` | 9 exact `InboundConsumerHandler` implementations and typed envelope dispatch | inbound façade、context factory、safe actor、existing envelope/receipt | transport locator/ack token/topic、repository/resolver/UoW |
| `crates/worker/src/lib.rs` | exact `InboundConsumerHandlerCatalog` constructor | worker services + worker safe slice | generic map/default handler、raw config |
| `crates/worker/src/main.rs` | `register_all` after catalog totality;retain `Box<dyn RegisteredInboundConsumerSet>` for process lifetime | worker slice + inbound registrar + service bundle | adapter construction/private registry lookup、root exposure before success |
| `crates/jobs/src/lib.rs` | 9 exact `ObservationJobHandler` implementations/catalog and variant/body/operation total map | publication/maintenance façades、context factory、existing Job wrappers | schedule locator、generated metadata/input、second DTO/error taxonomy |
| `crates/jobs/src/bin/*.rs` | one-shot exact handler entry;scheduled composition where assigned;retain opaque schedule set in host root | jobs slice + schedule registrar + service bundle | current-config resume、direct adapter/repository、fake run/evidence identity |

Step 04的planned tree没有`entry_registration.rs`，因此technical seam先归`runtime_builder.rs`。若current `07`根据实际代码体量拆文件，必须在对应boundary开工前同步更新formal file ownership和全部planned skeleton，不得由实现者现场漂移真相源。

#### 16.1.2 Definition order, planned tests and pause gate

1. Infra先定义boxed-Future aliases、safe metadata、finite invocation/result、handler/catalog、registrar和opaque registered-set trait。
2. `config.rs`完成raw binding parse/validate/canonical sort，不先暴露raw type给entry。
3. Runtime builder stage 8解析private transport、actor-policy和scheduler capability；scheduler必须可转交complete existing Job request。
4. Stage 11投影safe items并构造one-assembly registrar slots，校验raw/private/safe count、operation、producer和schema totality。
5. Worker/jobs只使用existing façade/DTO实现exact handler并构造9-slot catalog，enabled=`Some`、disabled=`None`。
6. Composition root调用group `register_all`；失败revoke/join全部并返回`EntryBindingIncomplete`。
7. 只有成功取得opaque handle后才能expose process root；handle存活期只绑定process-local callback ownership。

Planned minimum gates是§15.8的`entry_registration_safe_projection`、`entry_registration_catalog_totality`、`entry_registration_atomic_start`、`entry_registration_no_synthesized_job_request`、`entry_registration_bounded_envelope`、`entry_registration_invocation_mapping`和`entry_registration_no_reverse_dependency`。它们尚未实现、运行或产生artifact/result；current `05/06/07`必须分别展开suite/evidence/boundary后才能开工。

任一implementation boundary遇到以下情形必须暂停并回写设计：selected transport只能暴露provider envelope/ack object；actor mapper不能产生stable body-free `ActorSafeRef`；scheduler只能发tick而不能转交complete request；registrar无法revoke/join partial registration；runtime要求entry按locator查private registry；需要第10个handler、generic unknown handler、business port、durable registration state或新startup error variant。禁止以unsafe/downcast/global singleton/default input/first-call validation绕过。

### 16.2 主承接清单

| 承接项 | 已定义位置 | 实施计划如何使用 | Boundary开工前必须回答 | 缺口处理 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ownership / non-scope | §1~§3 | 绑定current capability、writer和excluded target | 这个field / write target的truth owner是谁? | owner不明立即暂停 |
| module / file owner | §4~§6 | 列exact create/modify files与Cargo edge | type / trait为什么属于这个crate? | 回查owner,不放公共杂物桶 |
| required fields | §5 / Step 06 | 引用exact object card和source | 每个required field来自DTO、lookup、derive、generator还是policy? | 禁止default / placeholder |
| callable ports | §5~§6 / Step 07 | 绑定exact methods、fake / durable implementer与caller | method参数、version、transaction和return是否齐全? | 不留future fake signature |
| public protocol | §7 / Step 08 | 绑定request/response/event/job schema与entry mapper | outer envelope、body、result/view/receipt/report是否能构造? | 回写design,不建替代DTO |
| function order | §8 / Step 09 | 列boundary内exact vertical flow | reserve/load/domain/save/outbox/result/commit顺序是什么? | 不以“等价重排”处理 |
| state / trigger | §9 / Step 10 | 绑定enum、transition helper、negative tests | current transition有正式trigger吗? | 无trigger不得实现 |
| persistence / side effect | §10 / Step 11 | 映射physical adapter、version/cursor和UoW gate | write set是否同UoW?expected version从何处load? | 不用log或source compensation |
| error / recovery | §11 / Step 12 | 绑定internal/public/entry mapping和recovery gate | failure是reject/conflict/retry/quarantine/dead-letter/manual/degraded哪类? | 不从message猜 |
| idempotency / concurrency | §12 / Step 13 | 绑定key/digest/order/claim/fence/token/probe test | duplicate/in-flight/unknown是否会重跑effect? | 不换key/token/binding |
| config / binding | §13 + current `04` | 绑定exact key/profile/capability和historical lifecycle | config是否仅wiring/parameter而不改contract? | config不能放宽invariant |
| telemetry / native audit | §14 / Step 15 | 绑定cut、field allowlist、emit timing、failure behavior | signal应进入Layer A还是native durable owner? | telemetry不得代替result |
| test / acceptance | §15 + current `05/06` | 绑定positive/abnormal/state/failure command、artifact和AC/VETO | command、artifact producer、evidence consumer是否真实可执行? | 未闭合不得开工/commit |

| Flow family | Allowed local write | Required atomic / ordered closure | External / runtime effect | Explicitly forbidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 16 Command | owner / marker、history、cursor、snapshot、stored result、reservation complete | §8 accepted template + §10 UoW | commit后publication / Layer A | source truth、raw body、telemetry决定commit |
| 9 Consumer | receipt / snapshot / projection input / gap / history / outbox / stored receipt | envelope/dedup/source-order before mutation | commit后ack/retry/dead-letter | copy body、upstream write、older regression |
| 14 Query | none | authorized committed reads only | response + Layer A | reservation/read-audit/repair/refresh/stale/outbox |
| 12 Event append | exact immutable payload snapshot | owner/history/cursor/snapshot/result same UoW | publication later | publisher reconstruct current truth |
| publication | marker、item outcome、Job report | stable token + current claim/fence/version | exact historical binding call | rollback source、switch binding、Failed -> Pending |
| derived/reference Job | plan/report、projection/reference/gap/maintenance/progress | start/item/finalize short UoW + item fence | resolver outside UoW,formal classification inside new UoW | long transaction、source repair、current-config plan drift |
| handoff/export Job | intent、local lifecycle/receipt/report/outbox | intent-before-call、probe-before-retry、finalize-only | exact historical target | verdict/signoff/alias、blind redelivery |

### 16.4 Implementation Preconditions

以下条件全部满足前,不得移交实现agent:

1. 本正式`03`已装配§1~§18并通过全文cross-reference、name、table、fence和truthfulness门禁。
2. Current `04/05/06/07`已按各自full-restart SOP逐Step重建,不得沿用historical material。
3. `07`已定义全部phase / commit boundary,每个boundary具有dependency、type、callable、protocol、persistence、test、acceptance和rollback / pause closure。
4. `07`已按每个boundary对正式`03/05/06/07`完成整体可落码审计,所有适用blocker关闭。
5. `07`完成时已同时创建current `implementation_execution_ledger.md`与**全部** planned `implementation-boundaries/<boundary_id>.md` skeleton,未来boundary保持planned / unauthorized。
6. 目标仓`/home/aris/Projects/quantalithos-observability`已确认存在,或由`07`明确的initialization boundary创建;workspace、edition、rust-version、crate name与`core-contracts` path可现场核实。
7. 目标仓项目级git identity在目标仓现场核实;design repo config不能代替。
8. 使用真实存在的design baseline。若`07`要求hash,只能在实际commit存在后记录,不得写placeholder。
9. Current boundary所需test command、fixture/environment、artifact producer、report/evidence consumer和AC/VETO已在`05/06/07`闭合。

|---|---|---|
| target `user.name` | `quantalithos-labs` | target repo修正并重新核实后才允许commit |
| target `user.email` | `quantalithos.ai@gmail.com` | 同上 |
| source language | identifier、rustdoc、comment、test name默认English | review前修正 |
| commit title | `<type>(<scope>): <subject>`;implementation repo uses English | noncompliant message不得提交 |

### 16.6 Phase / commit boundary closure rules

本章不预定义任何具体phase、task、boundary ID或commit ID。Current `07`划分的每个boundary必须同时满足:

| Closure | Current boundary必须证明 | Failure action |
|---|---|---|
| type | 所有construct/save/return/serialize/test所需primary与secondary types在current或predecessor可用 | 调整boundary或回写§5~§7 |
| callable | service所需trait/repository/policy/helper/error surface可调用,无future fake signature | 前移完整callable closure |
| protocol | request + response/receipt/report + entry/error mapping形成可运行vertical slice | 不允许只实现半个public surface |
| Job | public Job的metadata/input/output/report/idempotency/plan/claim/fence不能后置 | 合并dependency closure |
| persistence | key/version/UoW/history/cursor/outbox/result/consistency mapper可验证 | 不得用temporary in-memory shortcut |
| external effect | snapshot/intent/binding/token/probe/local finalize/ambiguous recovery同一可解释闭包 | 不拆成blind-retry boundary |
| test | current `05` positive/abnormal/state/failure command可执行并产真实artifact | 补`05/07`,不得声称pass |
| acceptance | current `06` AC/VETO/evidence consumer/reviewer可检查 | 补`06/07`,不伪造evidence |
| no future evidence | completion不依赖future script/report/signoff才解释 | 调整boundary,禁止placeholder success |
| naming | public/internal family mapping唯一且无historical alias | 回写all consumers |

Public Command / Job同名`PrepareExternalAuditExport`必须由typed family静态映射为`ObservationCommandOperation::PrepareExternalAuditExport`与`ObservationJobOperation::PrepareExternalAuditExportDelivery`;route string不得参与判定。该collision已在§7/§12闭合,但`07`每个相关boundary仍须审计。

### 16.10 Inherited affected activation gates

下表是current `07`必须逐boundary消费的激活门禁,不是普通backlog。`design record exists`只允许规划
受影响boundary；在对应owner的current正式文档或受控验证关闭affected前,不得实现positive path、启用entry、
声明vertical slice完成,也不得以placeholder、默认值、private alias或telemetry结果绕过。

| Affected ID | 当前状态与缺口 | `07`必须阻塞的boundary | 未关闭前唯一允许行为 | 关闭真相源 |
|---|---|---|---|---|

激活审计必须同时保留`60 = 16 + 14 + 9 + 12 + 9`、`60/60 recorded_with_affected_open`、
`0/60`无条件完成以及27个正式状态机已有设计记录。任何状态机、测试切口、配置快照或telemetry记录都
不能替受影响protocol补schema、owner、UoW、recovery或external capability。

## 17. 风险与待确认事项

> 校准来源:
> - `design-calibration/03_ddd_step_18_risks_open_questions.md`
>
> 延伸阅读:
> - 建议继续阅读 Step 18 §6~§9 的风险分类、已关闭项与来源覆盖,§10~§12 的14项风险、12项待确认、readiness区分及未确认前处理,§13的正式装配红线。本文只记录仍需保持可见的风险、确认状态和安全默认,不在装配时替owner选择产品、数值、boundary或evidence格式。

本轮没有发现要求回退正式`00/01/02`的**新**上游blocker；这不关闭I05 payload/schema与producer
binding的`open_upstream_internal`,也不关闭H13的`open_controlled`或其余inherited affected。
Implementation readiness=`blocked`。正式`03`已完成current Step 19全文门禁并停在用户审查；current
`04~07`、目标仓、逐boundary审计、implementation ledger / skeleton及真实script / test / evidence未完成前,
不得移交实现。

风险与待确认事项不是实现自由度。Product / transport / capability / numeric item未确认时只阻塞引用它的boundary,实现不得猜测;forbidden body、source-truth writeback、reserved writer、typed-family混用、blind retry、non-core compile dependency或fabricated evidence一旦出现,立即否决受影响boundary。

### 17.4 Readiness 与阻塞范围

| Check surface | Current state | Upstream blocker? | Blocks | Close at |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| formal `00/01/02` + DDD source | consistent | no | none | closed |
| 本轮新发现上游blocker | `none` | no | none | current Step 18/19 review |
| I05 payload/schema + producer binding | `open_upstream_internal` | yes,既有且非本轮新发现 | I05 positive activation | upstream owner + current propagation |
| H13/J06 | `open_controlled` | yes,既有且非本轮新发现 | J06 positive execution/completion | H13 owner decision |
| 其余inherited affected | 9项`inherited_affected` | no,但均为boundary gate | 对应UoW/mapper/external/Consumer/Job/type/flow boundary | §17.1 owner + current `07` audit |
| formal `03` full assembly | §1~§18 assembled;full-document gate passed | no | none；正式文档停在用户审查 | closed by current Step 19 document gate |
| current `04/05/06/07` | historical / pending restart | no,但implementation precondition | config/test/acceptance/handoff | per-document full-restart |
| target repo | absent | no,但kickoff precondition | code/compile/test/commit | `07` initialization boundary |
| physical/transport/capability | product-neutral only | no, boundary-specific | durable/integration/effect | `04/07` or approved spike |
| scripts/artifact/report/evidence | nonexistent by design | no, execution precondition | gate / acceptance | `05/06/07` + real execution |
| implementation ledger / skeleton | current version absent | no, kickoff precondition | recovery/boundary activation | current `07` completion |

风险关闭必须有正式来源:document precondition需对应formal doc通过停审并更新ledger;physical/capability需current `04/07`或approved spike;numeric/NFR需workload/range/environment/decision source;evidence需real command output + authorized review。File存在、README候选、mock always-success、health reachable、single local sample、placeholder alias或agent口头声明均不能关闭风险。`invariant_guarded`风险只能由positive/negative/fail-closed gate证明未偏离,不能改成config或accepted risk。

上述结论必须同时保持三层：本轮新发现上游blocker=`none`；既有I05/H13 blocker与其余inherited
affected=`open`；implementation readiness=`blocked`。不得把第一层简化成“所有上游blocker均为none”,
也不得把`60/60`有设计记录改写成可实施或测试已通过。

### 17.5 未确认前处理规则

| 场景 | 未确认前允许 | 必须停止点 | 禁止 | 回报 / 关闭入口 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| current `04~07`未重建 | 按用户确认逐doc full-restart | implementation kickoff | 用旧doc作current gate | matching flow / `OQ-002~004` |
| target repo absent | 仅记录path与initialization precondition | code/test/git/commit前 | 在design repo代写code或假定workspace | current `07` / `OQ-005` |
| physical store未定 | 仅在`07`明确允许时做port/fake conformance | durable adapter boundary | product schema反改logical contract | `04/07` or spike / `OQ-006` |
| route/transport未定 | route-neutral protocol + disabled/unavailable | enabled entry boundary | hardcode route或string family guess | `04/07` / `OQ-007` |
| adapter capability未定 | formal fake/controlled outcome;Unknown/Unsupported | production external call | blind retry、new token/current target、timeout=known failure | spike / `OQ-009~010` |
| numeric/NFR未定 | bounded fail-closed baseline;functional/safety tests | config acceptance或NFR verdict | historical numbers / single-sample pass | `04~06` / `OQ-008/011` |
| reserved flow requested | record future request并reopen design | add route/writer before | bypass guard或direct repository writer | §5~§15 / `R-010` |
| typed family cannot resolve | pre-UoW protocol mismatch | façade/reserve/digest前 | route/body guessing或shared operation key | §7/§12 / `R-011` |
| execution assets absent | planned/pending/not-run | gate/acceptance/signoff前 | fake alias、empty report、static pass、fake run/reviewer | current `05~07` / `OQ-003/012` |
| implementation finds design gap | report exact type/source/impact/safe stop | placeholder/default/private map前 | implementer invents schema/state/key/phase | return to owning formal section |
| forbidden body/source write/non-core edge found | retain body-free minimal reproduction | immediate affected boundary stop | risk acceptance/config switch/temporary bypass | `R-013~014`;return architecture/DDD |

Historical README、old formal `03~07`、old Step 18和old implementation ledger / boundaries均不是真相源。任何后续关闭或设计改变必须写入对应formal document、approved ADR/spike或真实execution ledger;聊天确认若改变契约,也必须回填truth source。

| `projects/L1-artifact/00-需求文档.md` through `07-实施计划.md` | body-free evidence、artifact handoff、persistence/test/acceptance文档密度参考 | Artifact body、storage/product、acceptance事实 |

| `projects/L0-bus/00-需求文档.md` through `07-实施计划.md` | event envelope、dedup、ack/dead-letter、outbox协作与dependency boundary参考 | Bus transport truth、retry/replay authority或compile dependency |

### 18.5 引用优先级与停审规则

发生冲突时采用以下优先级:

```text
current standards
  -> current formal 00 / 01 / 02
  -> current full-restart 03 Step 01~18 calibration
  -> Step 19 assembly record
  -> assembled formal 03

README / old formal 03~07 / old ledgers / reference projects
  -> historical or granularity input only
```

Formal `03`与current calibration冲突时必须暂停并在Step 19修正,不能让实现者二选一。后续current `04~07`若发现真实设计冲突,必须回写formal truth chain并固定新baseline;不得以“正式文档优先”掩盖缺口。Reference project、candidate product、path existence、planned script或example command都不是本项目实现 / 测试 / acceptance fact。
